01  清算 / Reckoning Time

他者的悲哀
On Another's Sorrow

清算
Reckoning Time

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

审理委员会
Courtroom

特别检察官：——请各位保持安静。
Special Tribunal Officer: Order, order.

法槌以恰到好处的气势砸向槌座——
既不会过于戏剧化，也足够有警告性。
The gavel strikes with perfect momentum, not overly dramatic, but enough to serve as a warning.

刻雷乌斯：……
Creius: ...

刻雷乌斯：陪审席上的争吵暂停了，喧哗声转变为一种富有穿透力的宁静。
Creius: The commotion from the jury box yields to a penetrating silence.

特别检察官：再提醒你一次，刻雷乌斯，这次传唤你回总部，主要是为了问询，而非你认为的“审判”。
Special Tribunal Officer: Once again, Mr. Creius, this proceeding is not an "interrogation." But you are obligated to respond to the questions put forth to you by this tribunal.

特别检察官：你应当继续在庭前宣读第十次“暴雨”后，也就是1920年10月1日至今的所有涉嫌行为，不得对委员会隐瞒。
Special Tribunal Officer: Now, please give a full account of your unsanctioned actions proceeding from October 1st, 1920 onward, following the tenth "Storm."

特别检察官：我希望这个小房间里能建立起如实问答的信任氛围，这会为在场所有人省下不少时间。
Special Tribunal Officer: You are reminded that your testimony must be complete, truthful, and without omission. Our time is limited.

刻雷乌斯：如您所愿，尊敬的检察官。
Creius: Understood, Your Honor.

在各级官员投来的目光中，男人低下头，一五一十地念诵早已准备好的材料。
Foundation officers and staff watch as he looks down at his prepared statement and begins to read.

刻雷乌斯：1920年10月，我在未获基金会授权的情况下派出自己的队员叶尔莫莱，前往前线执行任务。
Creius: On October 1st, 1920, I issued an unsanctioned order to Sergeant Yermolai, a member of my squad, directing him to carry out a mission on the Western Front in Europe.

刻雷乌斯：同时，接任安全区负责人期间，我在没有获得联合委员会批准的情况下擅自执行了“应急预案”。
Creius: In addition, during my tenure as head of security in the safe zone, I instituted a "frontline contingency plan" without prior authorization from the Joint Committee.

刻雷乌斯：在此之后，我借联合委员会在1920年12月通过的《收容人员扩大决议》，签发了超过基金会预计数量的收容资质许可。
Creius: Following that, I took advantage of the policy approved by the Committee in December—the Resolution on the Expansion of Admitted Persons—and issued admission licenses far exceeding my allowance under the regulations.

特别检察官的眼神越过刻雷乌斯，看向旁听席上的“鹰派”代表。
The tribunal officer stares right past Creius, looking instead at the representative of the Hawks.

伊里内伊：……
Irinei: ...

委员先生保持严肃，表情毫无变化。
Irinei maintains a stern expression.

特别检察官：你签发许可的对象主要是军事服务人员，其中涉嫌对许可资格定义模糊处的刻意利用。为什么要这么做？
Special Tribunal Officer: Then you admit to acting beyond the regulations of this policy in order to distribute these licenses, most of which were issued to military-related individuals. Would you care to explain why?

刻雷乌斯：因为我看到基金会需要他们。
Creius: I believed the Foundation would need them.

特别检察官：本庭需要具体的解释。
Special Tribunal Officer: Elucidate, please.

刻雷乌斯：过去，基金会在“暴雨”期间只收容核心成员，等下一个时代到来后，再重建大部分外围组织。
Creius: All this time, the Foundation has been sheltering only a small number of core personnel from each era. We've had to rebuild our ranks from the top down after every "Storm."

刻雷乌斯：就像一场地震过后，矿洞虽被毁坏，但晶核得以保存，晶体则要重新生长。
Creius: I don't doubt we had our reasons. We sheltered only the best of the best. After all, a farmer sets aside their most productive seeds to survive a coming flood.

刻雷乌斯：我们已经看到了结果——
晶体生长的速度过慢，不再适应当下的战争需求，这限制了基金会影响力的上限。
Creius: But we're fighting a war out there. I hardly think there's any need to "elucidate" how this policy has kept us on the back foot. We're scrambling.

特别检察官：可你收容的这些士兵对基金会乃至“暴雨”毫无了解，他们最初愿意与我方并肩作战，只是为了保护自己不受重塑之手的侵害。
Special Tribunal Officer: Irrespective of our shortages, you can't possibly claim these people you've taken in are fitting candidates. They aren't qualified specialists; they're soldiers. The only reason they're fighting for us is to protect themselves from Manus Vindictae.

特别检察官：你如何保证在“暴雨”之后，他们仍然愿意与其他时代的陌生人合作，为了基金会的理念而战？
Special Tribunal Officer: How do you intend to ensure their continued cooperation after the "Storm" takes us to a different era?

刻雷乌斯：我们会教给他们战争的意义。
Creius: We tell them what they're fighting for.

刻雷乌斯：何况，这个时代的士兵已经学会了服从命令。他们早已领教过和“暴雨”类似的剥离感，背井离乡，远赴异国的边境，为一场没有赢家的战争奉献一切。
Creius: Every man and woman that fought in those trenches has already come to terms with the idea that they may never see their home again, all for the sake of a senseless war without any hope of an end. What better qualifications could we ask for?

特别检察官：你是这么看待这些人的吗？
Special Tribunal Officer: Would you state that as a matter of fact, or as your opinion?

刻雷乌斯：因为我也是其中之一。
Creius: I speak from my own experience, Your Honor.

刻雷乌斯：我们都曾经被从自己的时代中剥离，眼看“暴雨”冲刷家乡，以及每一个熟识的故人。
Creius: Every one of us here has been snatched from our eras. We've all lost homes and loved ones to the "Storm."

特别检察官：……
Special Tribunal Officer: ...

刻雷乌斯：如今，我十分感谢基金会的庇护，并愿意在不属于自己的时代，继续为更崇高的理想服务。
Creius: But the Foundation saved us and gave us a reason to fight, not for vengeance but for the future itself.

刻雷乌斯：我的做法，只是延续了基金会在每个时代，对每一拨收容者的做法。数量的扩大，不会动摇基金会一贯的方针。
Creius: We are the lucky few who benefited from the Foundation's old policy. I only aim to give that chance to more people. Expanding our numbers will not change our goals. We can save more people, and I believe we must.

刻雷乌斯：而且，真相本身就是最有力的说服——
“暴雨”和重塑之手的威胁会团结起所有遭受其害的人。
Creius: Besides that, the truth of the "Storm" will help persuade them—to say nothing of Manus Vindictae. Just think about how many they've already driven to our side.

检察官借着整理文件的时间，制造了一个停顿的契机。
The tribunal officer organizes her files—an excuse for a brief pause.

特别检察官：有关收容人员的话题先到此为止，我们还有其他问题要处理，有关你对自己的小队成员使用“示踪剂”一事。
Special Tribunal Officer: You've made your point. Now let's turn to the matter of your use of tracking serum on a member of your squad.

特别检察官：我想先听取你对此事的完整陈述。
Special Tribunal Officer: First, please provide a factual account of the events.

叶尔莫莱：之后她就跟我走了，玛尔纱。刻雷乌斯要我给她打上跟踪药剂。
Yermolai: I'll be keeping tabs on her from now on, Marsha. Even got a tracking serum—Creius's order.

玛尔纱：跟踪药剂？
Marsha: "Tracking serum"?

梅蕾尔：我不会了，相信我，我一定不会再跑了！我保证！
Merel: I-I won't run again. Trust me, I swear!

？？？：……曼陀罗提取物已发挥作用，受试者的精神状态趋于平静。
???: Mandrake extract taking effect. Subject calmed.

？？？：对，从生理上来说，她现在十分健康。
???: Don't sweat it. Her vitals are fine.

？？？：出去吧，各位，注射“示踪剂”应该不需要太多帮手。
???: Give us the room, will you? We don't need any help with the injection.

梅蕾尔：……！
Merel: ...!

电梯门徐徐打开，将梅蕾尔从冷峻的银色封闭空间中解放出来。
The elevator's doors open, freeing Merel from its cold, silvery walls.

维尔汀：梅蕾尔，你怎么了？
Vertin: Is there something wrong, Merel?

梅蕾尔：我……没事。我们走吧，司辰老大。
Merel: I ... I'm fine. Let's go, Ms. Timekeeper.

维尔汀：叫我维尔汀就好。
Vertin: Please, call me Vertin.

梅蕾尔：好的，维尔汀老大。
Merel: Yes, Ms. Vertin.

维尔汀：维尔汀。
Vertin: Just Vertin is fine.

梅蕾尔：——好吧，维尔汀。
Merel: Oh, er, right. Vertin.

梅蕾尔嘟囔着收好身份识别卡和更新过许多版的《探访须知》，走出电梯门。
Her voice drifts downward as she stows away her ID card and copy of Visitor Guidance—already on its umpteenth edition. When the elevator comes to a stop, she seems only too eager to hop forward and out.

康复中心·走廊
Hallway, Rehab Center

梅蕾尔：1119号病房，应该就是这里了。
Merel: Ward 1119. Ah, this way.

她很快在康复中心回廊中找到正确的方向，来到了1119号病房前。
She leads them through the Rehabilitation Center's halls. Before long, they arrive outside Ward 1119's door.

梅蕾尔：玛尔纱？是我，梅蕾尔，还有维尔汀也来了。
Merel: Marsha, are you in there? It's me, Merel, and M—Vertin's with me too.

玛尔纱：是你们？——快进来吧。
Marsha: Merel? Please, come on in.

康复中心·病房
Ward, Rehab Center

在靠窗的病床上，一捧显眼的红发窝在洁白柔软的枕头里。
Striking scarlet hair stands out against the soft, white pillow beneath Marsha's head. She looks out from her sickbed through the nearby window.

维尔汀：你好，玛尔纱。
Vertin: It's good to see you again, Marsha.

梅蕾尔：玛尔纱，你的脸色看起来好多了！
Merel: Oh, you're looking much better!

玛尔纱：哈哈，医生说我脸色好，只是因为我头发的反光。
Marsha: Hah. The doctor says I only look so rosy-cheeked because my hair is red.

玛尔纱：现在我还不被允许下床，也不能用神秘术缓解疼痛。
Marsha: I'm not even allowed to stand up. And I was given strict instructions not to use my arcane skills.

玛尔纱：鉴于我知道一个不听话的患者有多让人头疼，我只能这么躺着和你们说话了。
Marsha: I wish I could at least get out of this bed, but I understand that would probably just make me a pain in the neck for the doctor.

梅蕾尔：没事没事，你继续躺着就好。感觉好些了吗？
Merel: Uh, best just to stay where you are, then. How are you really feeling, though? Inside, I mean.

玛尔纱：我觉得我已经完全恢复了，尤其是之前为卡戎做手术时积攒的恐惧感也在三天前释放完了。
Marsha: I believe I've fully recovered. The last of the fear I accumulated during Charon's surgery was released three days ago.

玛尔纱：但小梅斯梅尔小姐担心那些感觉会对精神状态造成潜在的持续影响，想要我再留院观察一阵子。
Marsha: But Ms. Mesmer is afraid that it might have a long-term effect on my mental state, so she has asked me to stay awhile longer for observation.

探望者一齐靠到病床边。
The visitors approach their injured comrade.

梅蕾尔：我们原本想给你带一些吃的，但在进入康复中心的时候都被扣下了，医生说不允许给病人外带食物。
Merel: We brought you some snacks, but security seized them at the door. Apparently, visitors aren't allowed to bring food.

少女的目光稍有黯淡，忽而又明亮起来。
Merel's face turns from downcast to bright and sunny.

梅蕾尔：不过，他们还是准许我把这个小礼物带进来。
Merel: But they did let me keep this.

一只体态圆润的小鸟出现在梅蕾尔的掌心。
A plump bird appears in Merel's palm.

玛尔纱：一只小鸟？……真可爱，但是我好像没见过这个品种。
Marsha: A bird? How cute. What species is it? I don't think I've seen such a bird before.

梅蕾尔：我是在《珍奇神秘学生物饲养者走访札记》里看到的。
Merel: I saw it in The Arcane Creature Keeper's Travelogue. It didn't mention the species, though.

梅蕾尔：很可爱吧，书上的还原图画得很粗糙，看不清的地方我就自己发挥了一下。
Merel: Actually, I think it said that even the author hadn't seen it. The picture was just a rough reconstruction. So I improvised a few bits.

玛尔纱：还原图……它们已经灭绝了？
Marsha: A reconstruction? Are they extinct?

梅蕾尔：这个物种是在40年代灭绝的，或者说，在那之后人们再也没有见过它们。
Merel: Apparently. No one's seen one for over 40 years, so it seems like they're gone for good.

梅蕾尔：说不定它们只是躲在了什么地方……
Merel: *sigh* What a shame! I hope there are some left out there. I bet the real version's even cuter.

梅蕾尔：我在总部待命的这段时间整理了一些读书时的旧东西，找到了我当时在上课时偷偷画的“设计图”。
Merel: I made it from some old bits and bobs I left at headquarters. One of them was a "blueprint" I drew during a maths lesson.

梅蕾尔：当时我想用神秘术做一只能动会飞的模型，不过失败了。
Merel: It was supposed to be a flying model powered by arcane skills, but, er, that was a bit beyond my ability at the time.

梅蕾尔：我想，说不定现在我的施术能力比过去要好一点，所以我试着把当时的想法重新整理，再稍加改进了一下……
Merel: But, since my arcane skills have improved, I decided to give it another shot. I retooled the design, added a few new ideas ...

梅蕾尔：然后，我就做出了这个。
Merel: And, ta-da! Go on. See what it can do!

“小鸟”：叽啾——
"Bird": *chirp*

手工制作的小鸟扑扇着短小的翅膀，从梅蕾尔的掌心跳起，最后栽倒在玛尔纱的病床上。
The handmade bird flaps its little wings and leaps from Merel's hand to the bed.

维尔汀：……要实现这个效果，应该要用到相当精巧的神秘术。
Vertin: It's amazingly lifelike. The arcane skill you cast on it must be sophisticated.

玛尔纱：谢谢，梅蕾尔，我会把它留在身边的。
Marsha: Thank you, Merel. I'll treasure it as if it were a sacred relic.

玛尔纱从被窝里伸出手，把小鸟捧到枕边。
She stretches an arm out from the blankets and places the bird beside her pillow.

玛尔纱：它很容易让我想起你。
Marsha: Somehow, the way it moves reminds me of you.

梅蕾尔：希望它能代替我陪着你。我在附魔的时候还加上了很多很多我能想到的咒语，大概都是“早日康复”之类的用途……呃，倒也不知道有没有用。
Merel: I gave it all the healing incantations I could think of. Not sure if any of them actually work, though. Anyway, I thought it might keep you company while we're away.

梅蕾尔：还有……我在这里没法待很久，玛尔纱，我和维尔汀马上要去第一防线学校了。
Merel: Vertin and I have been assigned a mission at the SPDM. We'll need to go in a minute.

玛尔纱：“Ⅻ”小队和司辰小队都要去学校执行任务？
Marsha: Huh? They're sending both Team Timekeeper and the "XII" Squad to the SPDM?

红发骑士露出了意外的表情，她很难想象如“猎犬”那般形象的人出现在第一防线学校。
A brief flash of surprise crosses her face. It's hard to imagine someone like Yermolai playing chaperone at the SPDM.

梅蕾尔：不……事实上只有我会去，我现在暂时被划到司辰小队了。
Merel: Uh, no, heheh, not the "XII" Squad. I've been seconded to Team Timekeeper for the mission.

维尔汀：最近几个月，第一防线学校接收了很多“暴雨”孤儿，同时还在筹备新学期的开学典礼。
Vertin: The SPDM has taken in a large number of "Storm" orphans in the last few months, and they're preparing for next term's opening ceremony.

维尔汀：所以现在学校很缺人手，我和梅蕾尔过去都是那里的学生，所以应邀去帮个忙。
Vertin: With our shortages in manpower, they've been recalling any available alumni to assist, including Merel and me.

玛尔纱：我明白了……梅蕾尔，相信你这次在司辰小队能留下一个好看的记录。
Marsha: I see. I'm sure you'll do well this time.

梅蕾尔：我……我当然会好好干的。
Merel: O-Of course! I'll try my best.

玛尔纱：说起来，刻雷乌斯长官那边怎样了？上次帕拉维扬过来看我的时候，说他被审理委员会带走了。
Marsha: How is Commander Creius? Paravyan mentioned that the Committee has brought him to court.

维尔汀：不用太担心，审理委员会应该只是想在“暴雨”前从刻雷乌斯身上搜集一些线索。
Vertin: I'm sure it isn't anything too serious. I believe the Committee only wants to collect as much intelligence as possible before the next "Storm."

梅蕾尔：也是因为这个事，我们小队的人都被临时分配到了其他岗位，就像我现在这样。
Merel: Yeah. That's why the squad members have all been reassigned.

梅蕾尔：我们原本想和帕拉维扬还有叶尔莫莱一起过来，但他们都没有空。也许我们要等到“暴雨”之后才能有时间聚一聚了。
Merel: I asked Paravyan and Yermolai to come visit you with us, but they were tied up. Looks like we'll have to wait till the "Storm" ends before we reunite.

玛尔纱：“暴雨”……这个时代要消失了吗？
Marsha: The "Storm" ... Then the end of this era is coming.

维尔汀：嗯，基金会已经在组织召回人员了。别担心，玛尔纱，你在这里很安全。
Vertin: Yes, but don't worry. The Foundation has already begun recalling its staff, and you'll be safe here.

玛尔纱：医生也是这么和我说，只要留在康复中心就什么感觉也不会有。但这是我第一次经历这样的事……
Marsha: The doctor said that as long as I stay in the rehab center, I won't feel a thing when it happens. But it's my first time braving the "Storm."

玛尔纱：我在入队的时候，刻雷乌斯告诉我，穿越“暴雨”的人总有一天会对此习以为常。
Marsha: Commander Creius walked me through what to expect. He told me I would get "used to it."

玛尔纱：你们都是穿越过好几次“暴雨”的人，对你们来说……真的是这样吗？
Marsha: You've each been through it many times now, right? Have you gotten used to it?

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

从病房出来之后，梅蕾尔脸上仍然挂着忧虑。
Only once she's outside the ward does Merel allow the worry on her face to show.

梅蕾尔：没有人会习惯“暴雨”的，对吧，维尔汀？
Merel: No one ever really gets used to it, do they, Vertin?

维尔汀：我认同你的回答。
Vertin: I still haven't.

梅蕾尔：唉……该去学校了，我们现在出发吗？
Merel: *sigh* It's almost time. Shall we set off early?

维尔汀：抱歉……我不久前接到了一些消息，临时有些事情要在基金会总部处理。
Vertin: Sorry, I can't. I just received a message. There's something I need to handle back at headquarters before I go.

梅蕾尔：那我等等你？
Merel: Should I wait for you, then?

维尔汀：不用，你可以先出发，玛蒂尔达会在开学典礼的会场等你。在我到学校之前的这段时间，她会告诉你需要做什么。
Vertin: No need. Just go and find Matilda at the ceremony hall. She'll tell you what to do before I arrive.

梅蕾尔：玛蒂尔达？——那个玛蒂尔达？之前的纪律辅员？
Merel: Matilda? You mean Matilda Bouanich, our old monitor assistant?

维尔汀：是的，她现在的主要身份是基金会调查员，这次和我们一样是以基金会职员的身份去学校提供帮助的。
Vertin: Yes. She's an investigator now—a "star" investigator, she'll insist. She'll also be helping out.

梅蕾尔：好吧，希望回学校的班车现在还在运行……毕业之后再也没有回过学校了。
Merel: Got it. I hope the school shuttle's still running. I haven't been back since I graduated.

维尔汀：那就趁此机会回学校看看吧，我和十四行诗马上会来和你们会合。
Vertin: Take it as a good chance to revisit, then. Sonetto and I will join you soon.

02  “暴雨”倾盆 / The "Storm" Downpours

雨前外勤观测总结
Pre-Storm Field Observation Reports

据马库斯、塞梅尔维斯等外勤队员自欧洲各分部发回的观测报告显示，当前历史节点中具有“时代临界点”特征的地点几乎遍布欧洲各地，其数量之多、分布之广，已大大超过了以往的历史数据。
这是一个天然动荡的时代，战争遗留的领土和政权问题令欧洲地图仍在流血和谈判中重新绘制，许多国家都处在秩序重建当中。饥荒、失业、流感后遗症等战争后创伤充斥社会生活，达达主义等反叛文化在人们迷惘的精神中流行，无线广播与小家电的发明带来了技术革新的萌芽……
在屡次增派外勤人员后，更多的“时代临界点”仍不断在各个国家、不同城市的角落被发现，我们依然难以确定重塑之手活动最频繁的地点。由于总部检查站出现巨大人力缺口，我们已不能派出更多人手继续观测。
综上所述，结合各小队报告判断，当前不能确认重塑之手的具体行动方向，无法进行针对性反击，亦无法延缓如此多“时代临界点”的恶化与膨胀，总部已决定立即执行撤离预案，尽可能保留我方有生力量。各外勤队长应要求、督促队员尽快返回总部，或就近前往避难点进行“暴雨”避难。
According to field reports submitted by Marcus, Semmelweis, and other operatives across European branches, locations marked as "critical points of time" are now scattered across Europe in the current historical period. Both their number and distribution far exceed anything previously on record.
This is a turbulent era. The territorial and political disputes left by the war have Europe redrawing its map in blood and paperwork, while many nations are still rebuilding. Famine, unemployment, the lingering effects of influenza, and other post-war wounds pervade everyday life. Rebellious movements like Dadaism take root in the spiritual confusion of the age. The invention of wireless radio and small home appliances sparks the first stirrings of a technological revolution ...
Despite repeated reinforcements, new "critical points of time" continue to surface in cities and corners across multiple countries, and we are still unable to pinpoint where Manus Vindictae are most active. With a severe manpower shortage at the headquarters checkpoint, we can no longer spare operatives for continued observation.
In summary, based on reports from all teams, we are unable to confirm Manus Vindictae's operational direction, mount a targeted counteroffensive, or slow the deterioration and expansion of these "critical points of time." Headquarters has decided to execute the evacuation plan immediately in order to preserve as many of our forces as possible. All field team leaders are to order and urge their operatives to return to headquarters without delay or proceed to the nearest shelter to take refuge from the "Storm."

03  临时派遣 / Temporary Dispatch

某位临时门卫的牢骚
Complaints From a Part-Time Guard

“说真的，当初要是有人告诉我，加入基金会后有一天我会被派去当保安看大门，我一定死也不让刻雷乌斯那东西离开夜巡特遣。但是现在一切都晚了，你、我，还有这些基金会的好员工们，马上就要挂起微笑一起站在门口大喊‘欢迎光临’了。
“说到底这还要怪刻雷乌斯和那些‘鹰派’们极力推行的扩大收容法案，这次司辰发布了‘暴雨’倒计时之后，全世界的难民都跑到了基金会门口，把总部大门围了个水泄不通，那些一眼望不到头的队伍连起来说不定真可以绕地球一圈。
“真不知道这次基金会的筛选条件放宽到了什么地步，要我说，那些老练的士兵里但凡有几个间谍，马上就能把基金会从里面炸开花。刻雷乌斯绝对比我还清楚那些老兵的厉害，但他完全不担心要怎么管教这些人，就像他有的是手段对付我和‘乌鸫’，当然，还有你这条根本不需要对付的忠心小狗。
“行吧，这就是他把我们扔到这里的目的，除了站岗和迎宾，还得兼职防暴、侦察和反间谍，关键时刻或许还得在大楼外面挖一条战壕呢。走吧，打起精神，现在得从巡逻换岗去门卫了……停，不是真要你笑得那么恶心的意思。”
"Honestly, if someone had told me that joining the Foundation would one day have me posted as a door guard, I never would have let that Creius leave the Vigiles Bureau. But it's too late now. You, I, and all these fine Foundation employees will soon be standing at the door, plastering on smiles and shouting 'Welcome!'"
"At the end of the day, this all comes down to Creius and those 'Hawks' aggressively pushing through the Shelter Expansion Act. Ever since the Timekeeper announced the 'Storm' countdown, refugees from all over the world have flooded to the Foundation's doorstep, surrounding headquarters with no end in sight. Those lines stretch so far they could probably wrap around the entire earth."
"Who knows how loose the Foundation's screening criteria are this time. If you ask me, if even a handful of those veterans turn out to be spies, the Foundation could be blown apart from the inside in no time. Creius knows better than anyone what those soldiers are capable of, yet he's not the least bit worried about keeping them in line—just like he always has his ways of dealing with me and 'Blackbird.' And of course, you—his loyal little dog who doesn't need dealing with at all."
"Fine, this is why he dumped us here. On top of standing guard and welcoming guests, we're also on riot control, reconnaissance, and counter-espionage duty—and at the critical moment, we might just end up digging a trench outside the building. Let's go, heads up. Time to rotate from patrol to the front door ... Stop it. I didn't mean for you to actually smile like that."

04  返校日 · 1/2 / Homecoming Day · 1/2

返校日
Homecoming Day

基金会走廊
Hallway, St. Pavlov Foundation

奥斯本队长：帕拉维扬，叶尔莫莱……好，请到这边来。
Captain Osborn: Paravyan, Yermolai, front and center, please.

在队长的示意下，两名“新兵”来到他面前。
The two "new recruits" stand at attention before their captain.

帕拉维扬&叶尔莫莱：嘿，早啊。
你好，先生。
Paravyan & Yermolai: Hello, sir.
Good morning.

奥斯本队长：来自“Ⅻ”小队的吧……我明白，现在你们正没处可去，你们的长官也够倒霉的。
Captain Osborn: You two are from the "XII" Squad. Got nowhere else to go, huh? Sorry about your boss.

奥斯本队长：那么，雨前反应部队第一梯队很高兴能得到二位的临时帮助，我是奥斯本，在“暴雨”结束前算是你们的长官。
Captain Osborn: Anyway, welcome to the First Squad of the Pre-Storm Quick Reaction Force. I'm Captain Osborn. I'll be your supervisor until the "Storm" is over.

奥斯本队长：你们应该没有接触过总部的避雨程序吧？
Captain Osborn: I don't believe you two are acquainted with our Storm Reaction Protocols, are you?

帕拉维扬：是的，这是我第一次在基金会总部执勤。我们应该做什么？
Paravyan: No. This is our first mission here. What are our responsibilities, sir?

奥斯本队长：雨前反应部队的职责是在开启人员收容程序时，为基金会总部提供机动性武装力量。
Captain Osborn: Our primary duty is to provide security for the Foundation's personnel admission process.

奥斯本队长：我们的顶头上司是雨前调度中心，每次“暴雨”前24小时，他们都会接管基金会的主要军事和安保力量。
Captain Osborn: We'll receive our orders directly from the Pre-Storm Dispatch Center, which takes over the Foundation's security forces in the last 24 hours prior to every "Storm."

奥斯本队长：不过这一次总部的情况比过去任何时候都要复杂，所以我们不得不动用所有能找到的人手。
Captain Osborn: Usually, we handle the admission process without additional support, but things are understandably more complicated this time. That's why you've been assigned here.

叶尔莫莱：呵，在哪儿不是干活呢。
Yermolai: Well, I wasn't expecting a vacation.

奥斯本队长：有心理准备就好。过来，我在路上给你们说明情况。
Captain Osborn: Good to hear. Now, follow me. I'll fill you in along the way.

面向北方的宏伟门扉历经时代变迁仍不失光洁，在倾斜的阳光下，它向地面投下沉重的阴影。
The grand north-facing gate, unweathered by time's passing, casts a heavy shadow beneath the sun's slanted rays.

基金会大门登记处
Registration Desk, St. Pavlov Foundation

在历次“暴雨”前夕，它都是外来人员合法进入基金会的唯一通道。
On the eve of each prior "Storm," it has served as the only entrance to the Foundation open to visitors and refugees.

在门外，等待收容的人们排成一条惴惴不安的漫长队伍，像病弱的蛇一般蜷缩在白色高墙的根部。
Beyond the gate, people form an uneasy line along the exterior wall, their slow and trudging progress like some fattened caterpillar inching toward safety.

奥斯本队长：看见这些人了吗？感谢《收容人员扩大决议》，这次“暴雨”前，进入基金会的人比过去要多了起码十倍。
Captain Osborn: See these lines? Thanks to the Resolution on the Expansion of Admitted Persons, we're taking in ten times as many people as before.

奥斯本队长：军人、精神病学家、理论物理学家，甚至还有带案底的“灰色人员”……有人简直想一次性搬空整个时代的人才库。
Captain Osborn: Soldiers, psychiatrists, theoretical physicists, and more than a few questionable individuals ... Someone's really opened the floodgates this time around.

奥斯本队长：这些来自本时代的收容对象大多还不了解“暴雨”，基金会是用其他理由让他们来总部的。
Captain Osborn: Most of them don't even know about the "Storm" yet. The Foundation's given out the usual excuses for our sudden invitation.

奥斯本队长：但我们不能忽视重塑之手的宣传，尤其是在这个战乱的年代，有不少人会想方设法混进来避雨。
Captain Osborn: But with all of these new faces, this is a prime opportunity for the Manus to infiltrate us. With so many looking for an escape from this war, it wouldn't be difficult to coax them into doing their dirty work.

奥斯本队长：你们的工作就是配合安检系统，阻止没有资质的人进入总部。
Captain Osborn: We've set up a security checkpoint here. Your job is to guard it and stop any unauthorized access to headquarters.

帕拉维扬：如果我们抓到了这些人，是应该把他们送去监禁室吗？
Paravyan: And what if we find an "unauthorized person"? What's the protocol? Should we haul them off to a cell?

奥斯本队长：当然不是，监禁室只会让他们得偿所愿——这些人可以顺势在监禁室里躲过“暴雨”。
Captain Osborn: Of course not. That's just what they'd want—an opportunity to cross the "Storm" safely.

奥斯本队长：合规的做法是把这些人撵出去。
Captain Osborn: Send them away, and make sure they don't come back.

帕拉维扬：撵出去？如果他们只是想避雨，为什么不让他们……
Paravyan: But anyone we send away will die in the "Storm." Can't we just ...

奥斯本队长：因为我们无法确认他们的真实目的。何况这次收容人员过多，很多人都没来得及接受可靠的背景调查。
Captain Osborn: Those are your orders. We can only admit those who are already authorized, and there's no time to run background checks.

奥斯本队长：宽容是一个危险的词，年轻人。我们面对的是严肃的秩序问题：
有这一次，是不是还有下一次？有这个人，是不是还有下一个人？
Captain Osborn: Showing any mercy, no matter what the sob story, could put everyone in danger. You let one man in, and a second will follow, and then a third, and a fourth, and before you know it, it's utter chaos.

奥斯本队长：基金会不可能收容全世界的人避雨。
Captain Osborn: We don't have the manpower nor the space to deal with that.

帕拉维扬还想说些什么，但叶尔莫莱一把按住了他的肩膀，示意他不要再多说。
Paravyan opens his mouth only to feel a tug on his shoulder. Yermolai's grim expression conveys the point wordlessly.

叶尔莫莱：抱歉，这小子其实对基金会的做法并没有异议，我也一样。
Yermolai: Understood, sir. Paravyan and I won't have any trouble following protocol.

叶尔莫莱：我们会遵循总部一以贯之的收容准则——“致谢，行礼，目送。决不惊扰登记者的后续生活。”
Yermolai: We'll do everything as ordered: "Express gratitude, salute, and see them off. Never disturb the lives of the registrants," right?

帕拉维扬：……
Paravyan: ...

奥斯本队长：你们明白就好。基金会是航行在洪水中的方舟，只有秩序能保护我们抵达终点。
Captain Osborn: Good to know at least one of you understands. The Foundation is like an ark sailing the flood. The only way we'll reach land again is through strict order.

？？？：放我进去！
???: Let me in!

入口处的人群里传来一声喊叫，接着是激烈的肢体碰撞声。
The crowd at the entrance erupts into a flurry of shouting and shoving.

奥斯本队长平静地向骚乱的来源转过头。
Captain Osborn's face remains stalwart and professional as he scans for the source of the commotion.

奥斯本队长：像这样的事情总会发生。在你们来之前，我们已经处理过三次类似的事件了。
Captain Osborn: Expect to see plenty more where that came from. We already stopped three of them before you got here.

闯入者：基金会不能把我们扔在外面不管！
Intruder: You can't just leave us out here to die!

闯入者：你们看着我做什么？你们——都是些蠢货，被基金会骗得团团转。你们安全了，那你们的家人呢！
Intruder: What are you all looking at, huh? The Foundation's only letting you in because they think you're useful! Your families are still going to die! Just like the rest of us!

奥斯本队长：我希望你们在他造成更大的破坏之前，把事件解决。
Captain Osborn: Gentlemen, please get rid of him before this escalates.

叶尔莫莱：……
Yermolai: ...

帕拉维扬：叶尔莫莱准备动身，随即瞟到身旁的“警官”仍在犹豫的眼神。
Paravyan: As Yermolai moves to carry out his orders, he spots his comrade's hesitation.

叶尔莫莱：呵……在我干活之前，我想问问，你在这个位置干多久了，奥斯本？
Yermolai: Oh, brother. Before I get started, can I ask how long you've been at this, Captain?

奥斯本队长：五次担任雨前反应部队第一梯队的队长，这之前作为队员干过三年。
Captain Osborn: Five "Storms" as captain of the First Squad and three years as a squad member.

奥斯本队长：在第一次见证“暴雨”后，我才意识到自己抛下了妻子和孩子。
Captain Osborn: It wasn't until I saw the "Storm" with my own eyes that I fully realized I'd never get to see my wife and children again.

帕拉维扬：……
Paravyan: ...

奥斯本队长：如果你想问我对这一切是什么态度，我会说，我已经发誓要把自己的人生奉献给基金会。
Captain Osborn: But if you want to know my take on this, I'd say I swore to devote my life to the Foundation.

奥斯本队长：没有牺牲，时代就不会前进，先生们。
Captain Osborn: It takes sacrifice to push time forward, gentlemen.

叶尔莫莱点点头，转过身，拍了拍帕拉维扬的肩膀。
Yermolai nods, then turns to give Paravyan an affectionate pat.

叶尔莫莱：走吧，“警官”，我们去收拾那个大喊大叫的家伙。
Yermolai: C'mon, Beagle. Let's take care of this lad.

“猎犬”很快捕获了猎物。擅闯基金会的外来人员已无路可逃。
Hound has cornered his prey. The intruder has nowhere left to run.

闯入者：去死吧，你们这些基金会养的恶狗！
Intruder: Go to hell, Foundation lapdogs!

叶尔莫莱：啧。
Yermolai: Tsk.

闯入者：——哎哟！
Intruder: Urgh!

他的屁股被狠狠踢了一记，身体失去了平衡，摔在地上。
A kick from behind sends the intruder stumbling to the ground.

闯入者：求求你们……不要把我赶出去……
Intruder: Please don't kick me out.

奥斯本队长：这人就交给我们吧，你们回到岗位上去。
Captain Osborn: Leave him to me. Now, back to your positions.

叶尔莫莱：知道了。
Yermolai: Understood.

现场在渐行渐远的哭叫声中恢复了秩序。“猎犬”走到帕拉维扬面前，拦住了他看向大门外的视线。
The wails and commotion give way back to quiet order. Yermolai approaches Paravyan, blocking his view of the scene at the gate.

叶尔莫莱：接着干活吧，你知道该怎么做的。
Yermolai: Let's get to it. You know what to do.

学生合唱团：我们渴求着，渴望着生活的和平与欢乐
Oh life of peace, and joy within, we yearn.

学生合唱团：我们准备着，准备着投身于奋斗的焰火
Our hearts, they stay on guard to strive and fight.

学生合唱团：因那深处的黑暗仍旧萦绕
For darkness lingers steadily within.

学生合唱团：而光明如白鸽，给予期许
Light flies toward thee like a dove.

……
...

开学典礼会场
Opening Ceremony, SPDM

孩子们稍显局促地鞠躬致意，随后被教员领下演出台。
The children make an awkward attempt at bows and curtsies, then exit the stage following their teacher's instruction.

老校长在掌声中登台。音箱爆发出一阵不协和的尖啸后，他开始惯例的演讲。
The applause continues as the principal takes the stage. He taps the microphone, drawing out a screech of feedback before he begins.

校长：感谢六年级生合唱团的演出。欢迎各位师生参加本学年的开学典礼。
Principal: Thank you for that wonderful performance. Distinguished teachers, dear students, ladies and gentlemen, welcome to this year's opening ceremony.

校长：同时，我很荣幸地告诉各位，来自基金会的巡检小组会与我们共同度过这难忘的一天。
Principal: We are especially honored today to be joined by the Foundation's Inspection Team, who will be spending this memorable day by our side.

校长：我知道，在座的很多学生是第一次踏入这座特殊的校园。你们来自世界各地，有着不同的文化和经历。
Principal: For many of you, this will be your first time leaving home. And for all of you, this opportunity to study at a unique institution with students from all over the world may seem daunting at first.

校长：但第一防线学校会调和差异性制造的冲突，让神秘学家的天赋发挥出真正的作用，为人类世界的和平做出贡献。
Principal: There will be challenges, even conflicts, along the way. But here, you will study the skills and values needed to overcome them. Every young arcanist will have the opportunity to realize their potential and to contribute to the cause of world peace—

校长：这也是第一防线学校创立的初衷，数个世纪以来，我们秉持着创立初期的理念……
Principal: A mission this institution has pursued for centuries. Our philosophy ...

会场上的扬声器还在孜孜不倦地传递着校长的教诲。
The loudspeakers carry the principal's speech across the campus, same as ever.

已经毕业的学生靠在会场边缘的护栏上，不断地检查着时间。
Matilda leans against the guardrail, finding her eyes drifting over and over to the time.

玛蒂尔达：维尔汀说她和十四行诗会迟些过来，但会先派一个助手给我，怎么现在还没到？
Matilda: Vertin said she and Sonetto would be a tad tardy, I know, but where is that assistant she said would be here?

玛蒂尔达：校长的演讲还是老一套……这讲稿说不定比我的年龄还大。
Matilda: Ugh, that same old speech again? I bet that old fogey wrote it before I was even born.

梅蕾尔：——玛蒂尔达？
Merel: Matilda?

一小片紫色从白茫茫的学生制服中挤了出来。
A flash of purple squeezes out from the sea of white uniforms.

玛蒂尔达：梅蕾尔！你就是我的助手？！
Matilda: Merel! You're the assistant Vertin sent?!

玛蒂尔达：你终于到了，我在这里等了你三十四分钟了！
Matilda: Heck! What took you so long? I've already been waiting for 34 minutes!

梅蕾尔：真是抱歉！学生通道里的人太多了，我排了很久的队才进入会场。
Merel: Sorry! You wouldn't believe how crowded the student entrance is. Took me ages to get in.

玛蒂尔达：学生通道？可你已经不是学生了。
Matilda: The student entrance? You're not a student anymore, Merel.

玛蒂尔达：你应该走工作人员通道才对，就说你是秩序协助小组组长玛蒂尔达的临时助手，他们就会放你进来了。
Matilda: You were meant to go to the staff entrance. They would have let you in right away.

梅蕾尔：啊……这——
Merel: Oh. Uh, haha, ha ...

梅蕾尔：……对喔。
Merel: You're right. I should've known.

玛蒂尔达：……
Matilda: ...

玛蒂尔达：你已经毕业两年多了，梅蕾尔！
Matilda: You graduated two years ago, Merel.

梅蕾尔：我、我知道，只是一回到学校看见教员的眼神不自觉地就……
Merel: I-I know. It's just ... I chickened out when I made eye contact with the instructor at the gate.

玛蒂尔达：算了，现在开始工作也还来得及。
Matilda: *sigh* Never mind. It's not too late to start our mission.

梅蕾尔：说起工作，我们回学校来是要做什么？
Merel: Right. About that, what is our mission exactly?

玛蒂尔达：在开学典礼期间协助维持学校的秩序，清除学校的安全隐患。
Matilda: To maintain order on the campus and deal with any and all threats during the opening ceremony.

梅蕾尔：这不是校园警卫的工作吗？
Merel: Isn't that the guards' job?

玛蒂尔达：正常来说确实是这样，不过……这里的学生太多了，我们还是换个地方说吧。
Matilda: Well, yes, but, hmm, we need some privacy. Follow me.

空教室
Classroom

玛蒂尔达仔细地检查了一遍空荡荡的教室，确认没有调皮的学生躲藏之后，才警惕地开口解释。
Matilda inspects the classroom. Only once she's confirmed it to be empty does she speak, albeit cautiously.

玛蒂尔达：唉……和以前一样，“暴雨”是不能让学生知道的，所以我们得找个没人的地方。
Matilda: *sigh* We can't let the students know about the "Storm." I am certain you remember.

玛蒂尔达：你应该比我更清楚这个时代的情况，外面的战争让很多人失去了家庭。
Matilda: I suppose you know this better than I do, but many children have lost their parents to the war in this era.

玛蒂尔达：所以第一防线学校收容了一大批神秘学家孤儿，多到学校老师都照顾不过来了。
Matilda: Therefore, the SPDM has taken in a great deal more arcanist orphans than ever before. The intake this year is so large that the staff can hardly handle it.

梅蕾尔：的确……我从来没有在学校里看见过这么多学生。
Merel: I see. No wonder everywhere feels so crowded.

玛蒂尔达：更不用说几乎所有安保人员都扑在维持典礼秩序上了。
Matilda: So, then, you've seen for yourself how order is barely being maintained, even with all the guards on hand.

玛蒂尔达：所以，学校就把平时教职工的工作委托给了我们这些在基金会总部的毕业生。
Matilda: That is why the SPDM has requested some alumni from headquarters to assist.

玛蒂尔达：除了维持秩序这样的表面工作之外，我们还肩负着一件更重要的使命——负责巡检“暴雨气象稳定鸽”。
Matilda: Still, we have a more important mission than just maintaining order, that is, to inspect the Shelter Doves around the campus.

解说者将目光投向窗外，校园围墙转角上的一截瞭望塔刚好嵌在窗框中央。
She looks out the window, which offers a clear view of a nearby watchtower at the corner of the school's walls.

梅蕾尔也循着目光看去。瞭望塔与她记忆中的没有不同——它总是以森严的姿态向整个校园投下监视的目光。
Merel follows her gaze. It's a familiar sight, the same watchtower that stared down at her when she was a student here.

梅蕾尔：你说的是瞭望塔顶上的气象鸽？这东西……是我们能碰的？
Merel: You mean those things installed on top of the watchtowers? Are you sure we're allowed to touch them?

玛蒂尔达：哦，你对它有多少了解？也许我可以省一些口舌。
Matilda: Oh, you know about them already? Good. Then we can skip the introduction.

梅蕾尔：我只知道这是一种能让“暴雨”免疫区边界变形的装置，这是加入“Ⅻ”小队之后从我长官那里听来的。
Merel: I think so. My commander from the "XII" Squad told me about them. He said headquarters only has a limited immunity range against the "Storm."

梅蕾尔：他告诉我，整个基金会辖区内其实只有中心地带有天然免疫能力。
Merel: Nearby facilities, like the SPDM, fall outside that range.

梅蕾尔：其他附属设施——像是离基金会总部有一段距离的第一防线学校——就要依赖气象鸽才能避免被“暴雨”回溯。
Merel: And that's where these Shelter Doves come in. They reshape the immunity area to cover those facilities.

毕业生们收回目光。
The alumni look away.

梅蕾尔：不过这些鸽子到底是从哪儿来的？
Merel: There is one thing I've been wondering, though. Where did these Doves come from?

玛蒂尔达：它们都是由鸽子屋提供的，连拉普拉斯里专门研究“暴雨”的研究员都不知道它的运作原理。
Matilda: It's Pax House technology. Not even the Laplace researchers know how it works.

梅蕾尔：你也不知道是怎么运作的？那我们又要怎么检查它？
Merel: But if we don't know how they work, how are we supposed to inspect them?

玛蒂尔达：过去每次鸽子屋都会派专人来。不过今年他们提供了一个小工具，就算不是专业人员也能干这份活了。
Matilda: Ordinarily, the Pax House sends an inspector to check on their status. But this time, they sent over the testing equipment so that we can do the job ourselves.

玛蒂尔达：只要把它插进气象鸽基座的校验面板就行，如果装置有问题，它就会响警报。
Matilda: You just need to plug it into the maintenance panel on the pedestal and wait. It will sound a warning if the Doves need repair.

玛蒂尔达微微低头，从口袋里拉出一截纸条——她提前做功课，将150页使用指南中的关键信息浓缩在了纸上。
Matilda glances downward as she pulls a piece of paper from her pocket. She's done her homework, condensing the entire 150-page manual onto a single sheet of crucial information.

玛蒂尔达：它还能检测气象鸽的能源供应情况，嗯……看起来这些装置是需要定期充能或者更换能源的。
Matilda: And of course, its power doesn't come from nowhere. You can check the battery status on the panel.

梅蕾尔：……万一检测结果出现了问题，也不是我们能修理的吧？
Merel: And if it does sound a warning? I doubt we'd be able to fix it on our own.

玛蒂尔达：我们只要换上备用气象鸽，再通知基金会就好了，会有人来处理的。
Matilda: In that case, replace it with a backup Dove and report the malfunction to headquarters. Someone will take care of it.

玛蒂尔达：备用装置都存放在瞭望塔地下的防空设施里。不过中央瞭望塔的地下区域正在封闭维护，所以那边的备用气象鸽都存放在塔顶。
Matilda: There are backup Doves stored in the air-raid shelters beneath the watchtowers, except the central one. That shelter is closed for maintenance, so its Doves are stored near the top.

玛蒂尔达：根据记录来看，气象鸽从没出过大问题，但巡查还是必不可少的。
Matilda: According to the maintenance logs, we shouldn't have any problems. But we still need to inspect each one of them.

梅蕾尔：所以只是例行公事？
Merel: Uh, so, it's just a routine check, then?

玛蒂尔达：听起来有些单调，但是它事关整个第一防线学校师生的安危。
Matilda: Yes. I know it sounds a little dull, but the safety of the entire campus depends on us.

玛蒂尔达：所以这项任务才会被交给我们——专业的纪律辅员，出色的调查员，优秀的毕业生。
Matilda: Now you understand why they turned to Matilda—their most reliable investigator and former monitor assistant—to bear such a great responsibility.

玛蒂尔达：嗯？
Matilda: Hello?

拉文德：玛蒂尔达小姐，你等到秩序协助小组的其他成员了吗？
Lavender: Ms. Matilda, have you met the other member of the Security Assistance Team?

玛蒂尔达：嗯哼，她现在就在我身边。
Matilda: Yes. She's here with me now.

玛蒂尔达：之前福格尔女士委托你寻找的走失学生找到了吗？
Matilda: Any luck finding the missing student Ms. Fogel talked about?

拉文德：我正要汇报这件事。我按照你的占卜结果去找了，但是教学楼和宿舍楼两个可能的地点都没有他的影子。
Lavender: Well, about that, I followed the instructions of your divination to the classroom building and dorm, but there was no sign of him at all.

拉文德：而且福格尔教员传来了新的消息，她清点过人数后，还发现了另一位没参加开学典礼的学生。
Lavender: And I received a new message from Ms. Fogel. She said there was another student missing during the attendance call at the opening ceremony.

05  愈演愈烈 / Getting Worse

瞭望塔校园传说
Legends of the School Watchtowers

[一张从书上撕下的末页纸张——已经过显形药剂处理，隐形墨水已失效]

下午我偷偷听见教员说漏了嘴，她说什么“学校里没有瞭望塔的时候……”这说明什么？那五座高塔果然不是普通建筑，而是出于某种特殊目的建造的！我们之前的猜测很有可能是真的！

你说的是哪一个？那五座塔是用来镇压幽灵的？还是关押疯掉的学生的？

都有可能！想想看，是什么原因让学校突然建造了这几座没有任何用处的塔，还禁止任何学生进入！

得了吧，说不定就是塔如其名，只是用来盯着我们的，上面安排了全天值班的老师，端着一把远程电击枪，就等你想溜出学校的时候，砰，一枪把你放倒

就算真有老师，我也不信他能二十四小时不睡觉，等哪天晚上我们找机会溜进去亲眼看看，不就什么秘密都知道了！

你疯了？万一里面真有什么能监控学生的机器，我们不是一下子就被抓住了

你就是胆子太小！到时候我们把脸一遮，出了事你先跑，我殿后，你就说去不去吧！

行吧……看在咱俩的关系的份上，我陪你去一趟就是了


[这张“秘密传讯”下方压着一张报告文件，上面有几行凌厉的钢笔字迹]

根据字迹比对，已确认撰写该内容的两名学生为五年级学生安德烈与奥利弗，我将亲自训斥他们，并给予他们禁闭一周的惩罚。鉴于近日学生之中大范围流传着关于瞭望塔的种种不实传闻，我提议增加警卫队于瞭望塔范围的巡逻频率，并增强对塔中暴雨气象稳定信鸽的监管措施，以免在鸽子屋专员检查时出现纰漏。
另外，关于我私下的失言，我确保其中未提到“暴雨”与第一次“暴雨”时的学校回溯事件，我将为此提交一份三千字的检讨书，认真反省我的管理失误。
[The last page torn from a book, treated with a visibility potion—the invisible ink now exposed.]

This afternoon I overheard the instructor slip up—she said something like "back when the school didn't have the watchtowers ..." What does that tell us? Those five towers are no ordinary structures; they were built for a reason! Our earlier suspicions just might be right!

Which one do you mean? The one about the towers being used to subdue ghosts? Or the one about locking up students who've gone mad?

Both! Just think—why would the school suddenly put up towers that serve no purpose and ban every student from setting foot inside?

Oh please. Maybe the name says it all—they're just there to watch us. The higher-ups have teachers on duty up there around the clock, holding a long-range lightning gun, just waiting for the moment you try to slip out ... and bang, you're down.

Even if there really are teachers on duty, I don't believe they can stay awake 24 hours a day. One of these nights we'll find our chance, sneak in, and figure out their secrets for ourselves!

Have you lost your mind? What if there are machines inside that can spy on students? We'd get caught in no time.

You're just a coward! We'll cover our faces. If something goes wrong, you run first, and I'll cover the rear. Just tell me—are you in or not?

Fine ... for the sake of our friendship, I'll go.


[A report lies beneath this "secret message," with a few sharp lines on the page.]

After comparing handwriting, we have confirmed that the two students responsible are fifth-years Andrea and Oliver. I will personally reprimand them and impose a one-week detention as punishment. Given the recent rumors circulating among students regarding the watchtowers, I propose increasing patrol frequency around the watchtower perimeter and strengthening oversight of the weather-stabilizing pigeons housed within to avoid any lapses during inspections from the Pax House.
Regarding my mistake, I can confirm that I did not mention the "Storm" or that reverse incident during the first "Storm." I will submit a three-thousand-word written apology and reflect seriously on my failures in student management.

06  出逃计划 · 1/2 / Escape Plan · 1/2

出逃计划
Escape Plan

乔治橡树
George the Oak

时间没有给古老的橡树新添多少枝条，数年光阴对它的寿命来说只是短暂的一瞬。
The passage of time has done little to change the face of the old oak. Years pass by as fleeting moments, but it remains as it ever was.

与过去相似的是，在乔治橡树下，同样聚集着一些小白鸽子似的孩子。
And just as ever, a flock of little white doves gathers beneath its ancient boughs.

艾米莉亚：这真的会有用吗，卢卡斯？
Emilia: Are you sure this is going to work, Lucas?

一个个子较小的男孩趴在地上，似乎在搜寻着什么。
A short boy crawls across the ground. He seems to be searching for something.

卢卡斯：不相信我的话，别跟我来就好了。
Lucas: You CAN leave if you don't trust me.

艾米莉亚：哼……
Emilia: Hmph!

艾米莉亚：我听不见校歌的声音了。马可说他会趁合唱团退场的时候溜出来。
Emilia: The school anthem's over. Mikey said he would sneak out when the choir was leaving the stage.

艾米莉亚：然后校长演讲会持续十五分钟，可我们现在还没有准备好……时间来得及吗？
Emilia: Then, it's time for the principal to give that same old speech that goes on and on. So, let's say 15 minutes, but we might need more time than that. Do you think we can make it?

被叫作卢卡斯的学生站起身，拍掉了手上的土。
Lucas stands up and brushes the dirt from his palms.

卢卡斯：你又不是第一次参加开学典礼，艾米莉亚，这个老头不多说半个小时是不会停下来的。
Lucas: C'mon Emilia, don't sweat it! You know the old man'll keep blabbing on for at least half an hour.

卢卡斯：我们肯定还有时间再布置一下，一旦召唤术阵布置完成，我们……再加上马可，一共三个人……
Lucas: We've still got plenty of time. Just think about it: once the summoning array's done, the three of us will be free.

卢卡斯：就可以去学校外面看看了！
Lucas: I mean out-of-the-school free.

艾米莉亚：……
Emilia: ...

这番话引来一阵紧张的吸气。
The little girl tenses visibly.

艾米莉亚：我们真的……能逃出去吗？
Emilia: Out of the school, really?

艾米莉亚：你知道现在外面在发生什么吗？教员说外面现在其实非常不安全，好像到处都在打仗。
Emilia: Do you know what it's like out there? The instructor said there's a war going on. It could be dangerous.

卢卡斯：那是他们要吓唬我们才这么说的！
Lucas: Are you kidding? They just said that to scare us.

艾米莉亚：到底为什么一定要在今天……
Emilia: But why are we doing it today of all days?

卢卡斯：就要在今天才行。现在全校的人都聚在开学典礼上，而且校园警卫也分了三分之二的人手去陪基金会的巡查员参观校园……听说这次来的可是委员呢。
Lucas: Because it's our last good chance for the whole semester. Everyone's at the ceremony, and the guards are busy either handling the crowds or showing the Inspection Team around. I heard there are some Committee members coming this time.

卢卡斯：嘿，这是你的计划，还记得吗？艾米莉亚，你一直很擅长做计划。
Lucas: So, will you calm down already? This was your plan, remember? Everything's gonna go just fine.

擅长计划的艾米莉亚沉默不语，她转头看向古老的乔治橡树。
Emilia—the planner between them—says nothing. She turns her head to look up at George the Oak.

古树默默注视眼前的一切，同样缄口不言。
The grandfatherly tree has nothing to offer but stern and noble silence.

橡树叶记忆着每一届在树下聚会的学生，随后这些记忆枯萎、被风吹落，成为后辈脚下松软的腐殖质。
Year after year, new cohorts gather beneath his bright, green leaves as they wither and dry, until they're plucked from his head by the wind and become the soft mulch on which the new class treads.

艾米莉亚：……？
Emilia: ...?

艾米莉亚：我听到有人过来了。
Emilia: Someone's coming.

卢卡斯：是马可？——他来了？我去接他。
Lucas: Could be Mikey. I'll go get him.

艾米莉亚：等等，让我再确认一下，我的神秘术还不是很稳定。
Emilia: Wait. Let me double-check. My arcane skill isn't totally reliable.

艾米莉亚：我听见的更像是女孩子的脚步声，一个小个子的女孩……听起来比马可要矮一些。
Emilia: Let me see. They sound like a girl's footsteps, a bit shorter than Mikey.

艾米莉亚：可是……可是为什么好像有两个人？难道是我听出了重影？
Emilia: Wait. Now I hear two of them coming this way. Is there something wrong with my arcane skill?

玛蒂尔达：抓到你们了！
Matilda: Got you!

小个子的女生从灌木中钻出来。
The star investigator herself pops out from the bushes.

卢卡斯：糟糕！是纪律辅员！
Lucas: Shoot! It's a monitor assistant.

玛蒂尔达：梅蕾尔，从另一边拦住他们！
Matilda: Merel, are you in position?

梅蕾尔：就位了！
Merel: I'm ready.

艾米莉亚：快、快跑！
Emilia: Ah, run!

逃学小队不是毕业生的对手，他们很快被捕，而后被扎扎实实地捆在橡树之下。
No match for the alumni, the two young students are soon caught and detained beneath the oak tree.

艾米莉亚&卢卡斯：……
Emilia & Lucas: ...

玛蒂尔达：又来了，不自量力的逃学小队——怎么每年都有这样的事！
Matilda: Another two overconfident truants. This happens every year.

玛蒂尔达：还好我们在合唱团里找到了你们的合伙人，不然真要让你们逃出去了。
Matilda: Fortunately, your little accomplice in the choir was exposed; otherwise, you would be running free outside the school right now.

卢卡斯：坏人！
Lucas: Urgh, you're a big jerk!

玛蒂尔达：——你，你说什么？你们是因为及时得到善良的玛蒂尔达的帮助才没有遭遇危险的！
Matilda: Wh-What? Don't you realize you could have wound up in danger if the kindhearted and brilliant Matilda hadn't stopped you?

梅蕾尔从他们刚刚布设术阵的地方站起身，脸上挂着一种十分复杂的表情。
Merel rises to her feet to examine their array, and a peculiar expression crosses her face.

那样子像是一个笨手笨脚的厨师，吃了一口自己做的搞砸了的菜。
Hapless, yet knowing, like a chef realizing too late what their recipe needed.

梅蕾尔：……这个术阵是没有效果的。
Merel: You know, this array wouldn't have worked anyway.

卢卡斯：什么？——你骗人，这是可以召唤狮鹫的术阵，而且是书里写的。
Lucas: What? Liar! I found it in a book. It said it could summon a gryphon.

梅蕾尔：《鸟目魔精习性与饲养指南》？
Merel: Uhhh, Avian Critter Behaviors and Feeding Methods?

卢卡斯：你……你怎么知道？
Lucas: What? How did you know?

梅蕾尔：我还知道这个术阵出现在第三章和第四章之间的空白位置上，是用隐形墨水画上去的。
Merel: You found this array on the blank page between chapter three and four, right? Drawn in invisible ink.

梅蕾尔：“一个可以用来召唤传说中的狮鹫的术阵，只分享给能够破解隐形墨水谜题的人。”
Merel: "Whosoever unlocks the secret hidden in invisible ink will discover an array which summons the legendary gryphon."

男孩不作声了，他明白，眼前的对手好像比自己强大得多。
The young boy's eyes go wide as he realizes just how far out of his depth he is.

艾米莉亚：那本书……是不是你也看过？你也破解了隐形墨水的秘密？
Emilia: You've read that book too, haven't you? And you found this array?

梅蕾尔：当然。
Merel: That's right.

艾米莉亚：……你以前也是这里的学生吗？
Emilia: You mean you went to this school?

梅蕾尔：嗯，你想说什么？
Merel: Of course. What are you trying to say, exactly?

艾米莉亚：所以毕业之后，我们就可以到外面去了，是这样吗？
Emilia: Then, you mean we really do get to go back into the world?

梅蕾尔：倒不如说想留在学校也不行了。
Merel: We couldn't stay here even if we wanted to.

艾米莉亚：既然以后都会知道，为什么现在什么都不对我们说呢？
Emilia: The instructors are hiding something. But if we'll find out one day anyway, why don't they just tell us now?

艾米莉亚：或者，你能……和我说说外面的世界在发生什么吗？我在毕业之前绝对会保密，不会告诉别人。
Emilia: Miss, why can't you just tell us what's happening out there? I swear we'll keep our mouths shut.

卢卡斯：不要问了，艾米莉亚。你看她们的样子，就知道她们都不会说的。
Lucas: Don't bother, Emilia. They're not gonna tell you anything.

卢卡斯：她们明明知道外面发生了什么，明明以前也和我们一样，但毕业之后就变得和那些讨厌的教员一样了。
Lucas: They know. But even if they used to be students like us, they're just like them now.

卢卡斯：一旦说到学校外面的事，就会摆出一副藏着秘密的臭脸。
Lucas: They'll ignore us, just like the instructors do.

梅蕾尔：我……
Merel: I ...

毕业生藏着秘密的脸涨得通红。
Her face flushes. Terrible secrets threaten to burst from her lips.

梅蕾尔：外面……外面到处都在打仗，我恨不得自己从未毕业！但打仗甚至不是最糟的……
Merel: War ... There's a war raging out there. Honestly, I wish I could stay here at the school where it's safe. And that's not even the worst part.

玛蒂尔达：……
Matilda: ...

教员：我听见声音了，他们在这里。快跟我过来。
Instructor: That must be them. This way. Hurry up!

稍迟一步赶到的教员在几个校园警卫的伴随下赶到现场，打断了尴尬的氛围。
An instructor arrives flanked by school security guards, disrupting their increasingly dangerous conversation.

教员：天呐，卢卡斯，还有你……艾米莉亚？真是让人想不到，你们到底想闹出什么名堂！
Instructor: Lucas, Emilia! What do you think you're doing out here? Unbelievable!

教员：你们要在禁闭室度过今天剩下的时间！立刻！
Instructor: You're both spending the rest of the day sitting in the guardhouse! Take them away!

艾米莉亚：不……不要，那里有好多虫子……
Emilia: No, no! There's worms in the walls of that place.

教员：那些沙米尔虫就是为了让你们这些捣蛋的学生长记性的。
Instructor: Exactly! Those Shamirs are there to give you troublemakers a lesson.

校园警卫：跟我们走吧，别耍花招，捣蛋鬼们。
School Security: Move it, you little terrors. No more tricks. I'm watching you.

卢卡斯：放开我！
Lucas: Let me go!

气急的教员平复了心情，对秩序协助小组的毕业生们露出了公式化的礼貌笑容。
The flustered instructor regains her composure and offers the Security Assistance Team a polite smile.

教员：谢谢二位的协助，我会负责让这些孩子理解规则与秩序的含义的。
Instructor: Thank you for your assistance, investigators. Rest assured, I'll be giving these two a much overdue lesson on following rules and procedures.

玛蒂尔达：……我们可以带这些学生去禁闭室。
Matilda: You are most welcome, Instructor. Of course, we could escort them to the guardhouse if you'd like.

教员：不必了，趁“天气还晴朗”，你们还有更重要的事情需要处理。
Instructor: No need for that. I'm aware you have more important tasks to do while "the skies are clear."

教员和安保擒住小白鸽的翅膀，把他们关回安全的笼子中。
She seizes the little doves by their wings and escorts them back to the safety of their cage.

秩序协助小组的两个毕业生还留在原地。她们对视一眼，算是勉强接受了眼前这个无法改变的现状。
Matilda and Merel stay in place, exchanging a look of resigned acceptance, powerless to change things as they are now.

玛蒂尔达：学生的年纪还太小了，而且很多学生都来自不同的时代，说出“暴雨”的秘密只会吓坏他们。
Matilda: These students are too young to handle the truth. They don't even know they're from different eras. Could you imagine what would happen if we told them about the "Storm"?

梅蕾尔：也许吧，至少他们能得到几年安全的校园生活……但这些惩罚措施还是太伤人了。
Merel: I do understand. I mean, I know the school's only trying to protect them, but their punishment still seems a bit much.

玛蒂尔达：哼～等伟大的玛蒂尔达在基金会身居高位，就肯定有办法改变这里的一切了！
Matilda: Mhm. Which is why I, the Great Matilda Bouanich, will be changing things as soon as I gain power in the Foundation!

玛蒂尔达：来不及等十四行诗和维尔汀过来了，我们先开始工作吧。
Matilda: But for now, let's keep our eyes on the mission, alright? There's no time to wait for Sonetto and Vertin.

玛蒂尔达：刚好这里离西北瞭望塔比较近，我们就从这座塔顶的气象鸽开始检查。
Matilda: The nearest watchtower is the one in the northwest. Let's start from there.

西北瞭望塔·顶层
Top Floor, Northwestern Watchtower

玛蒂尔达：这个就是暴雨气象稳定鸽。
Matilda: So, this is the Shelter Dove.

梅蕾尔谨慎地站在防爆门边，紧绷得像一件被冻上的湿大衣。
Merel stands beside the blast door, looking frozen stiff from head to toe.

玛蒂尔达：离那么远干嘛？过来，我教你怎么用校验器。
Matilda: What are you doing standing back there? Come on, I'll show you how to use the tester.

梅蕾尔：教我？你不也是第一次拿到校验器吗？
Merel: What? I thought you said you never used it before.

玛蒂尔达：你在小看伟大的玛蒂尔达·布翁尼吗？我当然已经掌握了使用方法！
Matilda: Perhaps not, but you underestimate Matilda the Magnificent! I already figured out how to use it well in advance.

梅蕾尔：好、好吧。
Merel: Oh, okay.

玛蒂尔达：过分谨慎的毕业生挪动脚步来到玛蒂尔达身边，接过了校验器。
Her stiff posture finally breaks as she walks over to accept the tester from Matilda.

玛蒂尔达：把校验器插入气象鸽基座旁边的插槽，拧动回路保险，之后按下插槽侧面的开关。
Matilda: Plug the tester in at the panel on the pedestal, turn the safety knob, and press the button on the side.

梅蕾尔：回路保险……是往这边拧吧。
Merel: The safety knob ... Am I turning it the right way?

她听到一阵细微的嗡鸣声。
A slight buzz emerges from the device.

玛蒂尔达：接着我们可以根据面板上的灯光来判断它工作是否正常。
Matilda: Okay. Now the lights on the panel will tell us whether the Dove is working.

玛蒂尔达：编号校验……正常，能源供应正常，庇护区范围校验……嗯，正常。太好了，一切正常。
Matilda: Verifying ID, green. Power status, green. Immunity area, also green. Good. Stellar. Superb!

玛蒂尔达将校验器取出。
She withdraws the tester with satisfaction.

玛蒂尔达：这样就好了，我们该去下一座瞭望塔了。
Matilda: That's all there is to it. Now we head to the next watchtower.

玛蒂尔达：对了，我之前就想问了，为什么你知道那些学生用来召唤狮鹫的术阵是没有用的？
Matilda: By the way, I'm quite curious: how did you know the gryphon array wouldn't work?

梅蕾尔：因为那是我在八年级时画上去的。
Merel: Because I was the one who drew it, back in eighth grade.

玛蒂尔达：……什么？这、这不是破坏公物吗？
Matilda: Ah, uh, what? But, but, but that's, that's vandalism!

基金会走廊
Hallway, St. Pavlov Foundation

十四行诗：……
Sonetto: ...

漫长的走廊像一道首尾相衔的谜语，盘绕着委员会大楼，与精心设计的阶梯系统一同连通各大楼层与房间。
The long corridor is like a riddle whose end connects to its beginning. It loops around the Committee Building, linking various floors and rooms with meticulously designed stairways.

第一助手的脚步慢了下来。
Somewhere within that winding riddle, Sonetto comes to a halt.

维尔汀：怎么了，十四行诗？
Vertin: Is there something wrong, Sonetto?

轻柔的说话声在静谧的空间中膨胀，基金会在“暴雨”前夕的忙碌丝毫没有波及此处。
The soft words sound louder and sharper in the empty hallway. The bustle of the Foundation's Pre-Storm activity hasn't yet reached this corridor.

十四行诗：我们刚刚经过了审理委员会的所在地，刻雷乌斯先生和其他委员这个时候应该就在那间房里。
Sonetto: That courtroom we just passed—Mr. Creius is in there answering the Committee's questions.

十四行诗：刻雷乌斯先生正在接受问询。我们当时前往别列格勒正是因为他提供的琥珀屋线索，我担心……
Sonetto: It was his information that led us to the Amber Room and to Bereggrad. I'm just concerned ...

维尔汀：提交顿河地区的行动报告之后，基金会就没有再过问这件事。
Vertin: The Foundation hasn't quizzed me on anything since I submitted my report on the Don Operation.

维尔汀：如果基金会想在这件事上调查我，那程序早就应该开始了。
Vertin: I would be in there right next to Creius if they wanted to investigate us as well.

十四行诗：……我明白了。
Sonetto: I see.

十四行诗压下忧虑。脚步声再度在廊柱间回响。
Sonetto suppresses her concerns. Footsteps echo over the marble again.

维尔汀：就是这里，我们进去吧。
Vertin: Here it is. Let's go.

基金会办公室
Foundation Office

门后是一间再普通不过的标准办公室，并无更多彰显个人喜好的摆饰。这或许意味着它的使用者才刚刚搬入不久。
The office behind the opening door is barren of any identity, a symptom both of the occupant's recent arrival and her curious nature.

房间的主人从桌面上一沓整齐堆砌的文件中抬起头。
The woman in question lifts her head from the neat stacks of files on her desk.

无名者：二位，很久没见面了。
Ms. Stranger: Long time no see, Timekeeper, Sonetto.

07  返校日 / Homecoming Day

？？？：今天真是个好日子。
Such a lovely day, isn't it?

？？？：叽叽喳喳的孩子们都要被关进学校了，你我也该告别了。
These noisy children are soon going to be locked behind the school gate, and I will say goodbye to you.

？？？：现在我只要闭上眼睛，就能和这场过于漫长的战争说再见。
This war will be over as long as I close my eyes. It has lasted far too long.

？？？：终于……
*sigh* Finally ...

你怎么还在这儿？去学校啊，去学着识字，不要再打打杀杀了。
What are you doing standing around? Aren't you heading to school? Go on then. Children your age are SUPPOSED to be learning and playing.

选项 / Choices

1. 我早就毕业了
1. I graduated long ago

2. 我会想念你的
2. I'll miss you

分支 / Branch 1

？？？：那就去上大学啊，读个硕士，博士。
College then. Get yourself a medical degree or a doctorate at least.

？？？：不用打仗的时间是很宝贵的，拿去充实你自己。
Haven't you realized how precious peace is after coming back from the battlefield? Make your life count.

？？？：别在这儿打扰我午睡了，做点什么。
I'm going to take a nap. Now, go and find something to keep yourself busy.

分支 / Branch 2

？？？：……别来这套。
Oh, please.

？？？：看看那棵树，它见证了一次又一次的叛逆，而且每年都要长高差不多两英寸。
Take a look at that tree. I think it must feed on the escape plans of students like you; how else could it grow two inches taller every year?

？？？：坏孩子总能在它下面找到归属感。
I imagine it might even serve as something of a secret hideout for them.

？？？：但很少有人在毕业后再回去看它。树上的每一道划痕都属于一个不同的人，没有人划下第二道。
But I wonder if anyone ever bothers to visit it again after they leave; likely not. A once beloved place all too quickly forgotten.

？？？：你知道为什么吗？
Do you know why?

？？？：因为他们感到恐惧与后悔。
Because they are too afraid to face the difficult truth.

？？？：他们意识到只有那棵树有资格一直留在这天堂一样的地方，而他们却把天堂的时光过得像地狱。
That they wasted the most carefree days of their lives trying to escape from paradise into hell, and now there's no power in heaven or earth that can send them back.

？？？：现在，回去看看吧，去证明你有勇气面对那棵树和童年的乐园。
You'll end up just as pathetic as them, unless you can go back there and prove your courage.

？？？：祝你像你的法国同窗一样勇敢，或者你要做胆小鬼？
You won't have anything like the fanfare your French classmate received—she was proud as a peacock when she walked through that gate. But you'll not chicken out, will you?

共同接续 / Shared continuation

返校日
Homecoming Day

08  完美置换 / Perfect Exchange

改良版隐形墨水配方
Improved Invisible Ink Formula

[一份字迹幼稚的笔记]

想办法用学校里易得的东西替换掉原本的材料

仪式祭礼——这种传统的供奉食物成分大多是面粉，用一把干意大利面大概差不多？

咆哮根——好在这份主要材料学校里就有，上周正好在湖边的树根旁发现了几株，使用盐封咒需要精磨苦盐，去食堂弄一些食盐来应该能代替

颤颤之齿——理论上是中小型杂食类神秘动物的臼齿，但从大小和灵性含量来看，医务室牙仙小姐收藏的神秘学家乳牙或许是最佳选择，这两天洗个冷水澡感冒一下就有理由去医务室了，不过应该直接向她求取还是偷偷拿一颗呢……
[A note in childish handwriting.]

Find a way to replace the ingredients with things that are easy to get from within the school.

Ritual Offering—This sacrificial food is mostly made of flour. Maybe a handful of dry pasta will do the trick?

Roaring Root—Fortunately, this main ingredient can be found right here at school. Spotted a few growing near the tree roots by the lake last week. The spell calls for Milled Magnesia—some salt from the canteen should work.

Trembling Tooth—Theoretically these should be molars from small to mid-sized omnivorous arcane animals, but judging by size and pneuma content, the baby teeth collected by Ms. Tooth Fairy at the clinic might be the best option. I could take cold showers for a couple of days, then if I get sick, I'd have an excuse to visit ... but should I ask her directly or just quietly steal one ...

09  黑箱研究员 / The Cube Researchers

黑箱研究员
The Cube Researchers

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

维尔汀：你看起来还很虚弱，无名者。
Vertin: You still look pale, Ms. Stranger.

无名者：谢谢你的关心，司辰。我现在已经恢复了大部分的工作能力。
Ms. Stranger: Well enough to get back to work. Thank you for your concern.

无名者：不过，在寒暄之前，我们不妨先解决这位女士的问题。
Ms. Stranger: Though, before we exchange any more pleasantries, shall we allow this researcher to finish her job first?

她看了一眼站在房间角落中的另一个人。
She glances over to the other woman standing in the corner.

技术员：司辰，你很准时……甚至可以说是分秒不差。
Technician: Hello, Timekeeper. You're as punctual as ever, right down to the tick.

维尔汀：你也是，女士。但我的准时通常只是巧合。
Vertin: In fact, you're early, miss. But I must confess, I was never all that timely before becoming the Timekeeper.

技术员：啊，幽默感。他们没和我说过司辰也会这一套。
Technician: Oh, wow, and a sense of humor too! You've really got it all, Ms. Vertin.

技术员：我是负责手提箱研究的技术人员，之一——这么有趣的神秘学道具当然不会只让我一人负责。相信你以前也见过我的其他同事。
Technician: I'll be the one conducting research into your Suitcase's technology this time. Well, it's not just me, of course; it'll go to the team. One researcher alone couldn't possibly understand a device of the Suitcase's magnitude, could they?

技术员：请问箱子中的住客都已经转移了吗，司辰？
Technician: So, has everyone moved out already?

维尔汀：是的，基金会为他们安排了临时住所。我自己也检查过，没有人遗漏在房间或荒原里。
Vertin: Yes. They're all in the Foundation's guest rooms. I've checked every nook and cranny to ensure no one was left behind.

每年手提箱检修的时间，今年正好撞上了“暴雨”。司辰小队也许可以放个假了。
This year's Suitcase inspection just so happens to fall during the "Storm." Perhaps Team Timekeeper will be able to take a vacation.

按照惯例，技术员上前一步，取走空荡荡的箱子。
Following protocol, the technician takes the emptied Suitcase.

技术员：非常感谢你的配合，我相信这一定能帮助我们对神奇的纺车有更多的了解。
Technician: Thank you for your cooperation. I'm sure this will help us learn more about that mysterious Spinning Wheel.

技术员毕恭毕敬地提着手提箱，却没有马上离开。似乎她还有些话要说。
She holds the Suitcase delicately, almost reverently. She stares down at it and then glances up again, as if weighing unspoken words on her tongue.

维尔汀：请问还有什么事吗？
Vertin: Hm? Anything else I can help you with?

技术员：噢……这无关箱子，是出于我的私人兴趣。
Technician: Um, Timekeeper, there's something I'd like to ask you about, kind of a private matter.

技术员：我想问的是关于琥珀屋的事……我听说，你在那里经受了一些时间错乱的困扰，对吗？
Technician: About the Amber Room. I heard you went through some kind of time distortion in there. Is that true?

维尔汀：没错，这件事我也写在提交给基金会的报告中了。
Vertin: Yes. It was exactly as I wrote in my report.

技术员：时间混乱……真希望我也能被安排进这个课题，还有什么问题比时间更有意思！
Technician: Time distortion ... If only I were assigned to research that! What could be more fun than studying time itself!

她想要充分发挥肢体语言的效用来表达心中的激动，但手中的手提箱让她不得不保持最大程度的克制。
Her visible excitement is contained—if just barely—by concern for the marvelous device she is holding.

技术员：不过，手提箱是一个有关空间的问题……噢……时间与空间，哪一个更神秘？
Technician: Not to knock working on the Suitcase—I suppose involves the study of space ... Space or time, ah, I wonder which is deeper.

技术员：你身上有很多的谜，司辰，或者说，你本身就是一个“问题”……唉，我不知道怎么表达。
Technician: You know, you're a real gatherer of mysteries, Timekeeper. Actually, you're kind of a mystery yourself. *sigh* I don't know what I'm saying.

技术员：抱歉，我该走了。请原谅我不太会说话的嘴。
Technician: Sorry for the blabbering. I should really go.

技术员：我大概会在一周之后送还手提箱，快的话也许只要三天，请注意规划好行程安排，司辰。
Technician: We'll get the Suitcase back to you in a few days. A week at most. Please plan your schedule accordingly. Goodbye, Timekeeper.

她轻轻点头，光滑的面具表面折射出晃眼的光芒，随后转身离开房间。
She nods, causing the light to reflect off her sleek mask, then makes her exit.

十四行诗：……
Sonetto: ...

无名者不知道在什么时候已经轻飘飘地来到了身边。
Ms. Stranger approaches gently.

无名者：科算中心总能找到这么有意思的人来为我们的知识添砖加瓦。
Ms. Stranger: I wonder if she even saw me or Ms. Sonetto. Laplace really does excel in finding "unique" talents.

维尔汀：我差不多已经习惯了。
Vertin: I'm used to it.

维尔汀：基金会已经给你安排新的工作了吗？我以为你还需要再休息一段时间。
Vertin: Has the Foundation assigned you a new job already? I thought you'd want to rest for a while longer.

无名者：我也在盼望一个假期，但这件事的决定权并不在我。
Ms. Stranger: I wish I could, but it's not my decision to make.

无名者：我还在接受治疗的时候，就收到了你在顿河地区的行动报告，以及有关你从伊戈尔手上获取的“奇迹”的简要信息。
Ms. Stranger: The Foundation received your report on the Don Operation while I was still stuck in bed. It mentioned that Igor gave you a "boon."

无名者：科算中心负责研究“奇迹”的小组需要熟悉重塑之手神秘术的人，于是我被派去帮了一些忙。
Ms. Stranger: Part of my work involves studying the Manus's arcane skills, so I was sent to Laplace to help.

无名者：项目的负责人告诉我，现在已经有了阶段性的成果，不过他们需要你的帮助才能继续进行下去。
Ms. Stranger: The project leader said they've made some progress already, but they'll need your help to continue.

无名者：这也是为什么我在这个时候要请你们过来一趟。
Ms. Stranger: Which is why I invited you here.

维尔汀：研究小组在基金会？
Vertin: Is the research team here at the Foundation?

无名者：嗯，基金会提前通知了研究负责人，她和她的助手已经带着仪器来到基金会了。
Ms. Stranger: Yes. The project leader and her assistant were notified in advance and brought their equipment here.

无名者：跟我来吧，她们想要一个开阔的空间，所以在后花园等你。
Ms. Stranger: Follow me. They asked for an open space, so we agreed to meet in the garden.

基金会南翼花园
Southern Garden, St. Pavlov Foundation

委员会大楼的南面排列着多栋功能不一的附属建筑，这些线条凛冽的白色屋宇共同环绕着一片开阔的绿地。
Cold white auxiliary buildings form an uneven enclosure around the pleasant green commons of the Foundation's southern annex.

塑造这片花园的园艺师一定是法国古典主义园林的忠实拥趸。这里像是一个更加紧密、精巧的凡尔赛宫苑。
The patterns of shrubbery and lace-like walkways resemble, in a deliberate way, the intricate gardens that surround the Palace of Versailles—albeit tightly compacted.

无名者：我无法想象有人的放松方式，是在这样的花园里走迷宫。
Ms. Stranger: I don't understand how one can relax while walking through a maze like this.

维尔汀：在树篱没有这么高的时候，这里的景色还算怡人。
Vertin: The view was much more enjoyable before the hedges grew into walls like this.

在几何花园中央的一块空地上，兀立着一台构造奇特的仪器。
At the center of the commons stands a peculiar instrument.

十四行诗：我看到科算中心的人了，那是……小春雀儿？
Sonetto: That should be them over there. Is that Eagle?

小春雀儿：各位好。
Eagle: Hi, Ms. Timekeeper, Ms. Sonetto, Ms. Stranger.

曾以童子军姿态示人的少女，本分地向所有出现在她面前的人点头致意。
The girl nods dutifully but bounds back with a bright smile.

小春雀儿：司辰，十四行诗小姐，无名者小姐。
Eagle: It's great to see you again.

维尔汀：小春雀儿，好久没有见到你了。看样子，你已经是科算中心的一员了。
Vertin: It's been a while, Eagle. I understand you've joined Laplace.

维尔汀：你也参与了这个项目吗？
Vertin: Will you be working on this project too?

小春雀儿：项目的主要负责人是我的老师，而我只是帮各位处理一些简单的问题。我的老师正在——
Eagle: Not exactly. The project leader is my tutor. I'm just here to help with a few things. She is ...

？？？：您好，司辰。
???: Hello, Timekeeper. It's truly an honor.

一名身着拉普拉斯制服的女子从仪器后探出身来，眼神中带着一丝了然的狡黠。
A woman steps out from behind the device. At a glance, she could be mistaken for any other Laplace researcher, save for the sharp and cunning look in her eyes.

玛格丽特：您读过虚构集小姐的那本小说，对吗？
Marguerite: I hope you've found the time to read Ms. Recoleta's novel.

玛格丽特：称呼我为玛格丽特即可。很高兴见到您。
Marguerite: Oh, you can call me Marguerite.

维尔汀：你好，玛格丽特研究员。
Vertin: It's a pleasure, Marguerite.

玛格丽特：你们来得正好，我和小春雀儿刚将“完全指向性虚拟监听仪”调试完成。我们马上可以开始实验。
Marguerite: Your timing's perfect. Eagle and I have just finished setting up the Omni-Vector Virtual Monitoring Device. We're ready to start the experiment at any time.

研究员站在仪器旁，满脸写着不怀好意的期待。
She presents the device to them with a look that reveals both anticipation and calculation in a single dangerous smile.

玛格丽特：请坐，司辰。我保证这会是一趟充满意外与未知的旅程。
Marguerite: Please, take a seat, Timekeeper. You're about to experience quite the adventure.

十四行诗：等等，玛格丽特研究员，请问你说的“完全指向性虚拟监听仪”到底是什么？
Sonetto: Wait, Ms. Marguerite. Would you mind explaining what this "Omni-Vector Virtual Monitoring Device" is before you start?

玛格丽特：全称“混沌系统全方向量化分析与空间度量感官虚拟监听仪器”。
Marguerite: It's a "Chaos-System Omni-Vector Quantitative Analysis and Spatial-Metric Virtual Sensory Monitoring Device," to be precise.

玛格丽特：对于这个小匣子，我们已知的初始系统信息非常有限，经过一段时间后，它将不再可预测。依据实验中的实际情况，我们必须——
Marguerite: Based on what we know about this cube, it'll go through unpredictable changes in the near future. So, to keep it under control, we must—

玛格丽特：哎呀，看诸位的表情，似乎并没有理解这台仪器的用途。
Marguerite: Ah. Judging by your faces, I think I haven't been clear enough.

无名者：也许我们可以把问题简化一点儿。
Ms. Stranger: Allow me to make things easier for everyone.

无名者：根据重塑之手的术式原理，研究小组逆向解析了用以稳固外壳的神秘术。由此，我们得以一窥盒中之物的真面目——
Ms. Stranger: Using the data I provided, the research team has decoded the arcane skill Manus Vindictae used to stabilize the shell of the "boon," allowing us to access what was held inside.

玛格丽特：以单一感官的粗暴方式——也就是听力。
Marguerite: It was trivial, really. All we needed to do was listen.

维尔汀：所以，借助这台仪器，我能够“听见”盒中的内容。
Vertin: So you're saying I can use this device to "hear" what's inside the cube.

玛格丽特：没错，虽然“监听”这个说法不是很准确，但是语言本身就是充满暗示性和不准确的。
Marguerite: Exactly. I know it isn't quite correct to call it a "monitoring device," but language itself is an exercise in uncertainty, wouldn't you say?

无名者：考虑到这个黑盒出自阿尔卡纳之手，我们更倾向于使用间接的观测手段。
Ms. Stranger: Given that the cube was created by Arcana, a less direct but safer method of exploration is probably for the best.

玛格丽特：顺带一提，趁着仪器调试的时候，我已经尝试过监听了。但在听到第一道声音的时候，我就昏了过去。
Marguerite: As it happens, I already attempted to listen to the device while setting up the system. As soon as the first blast of sound reached my ears, I was hit with a wave of dizziness and briefly fell unconscious.

玛格丽特：而这种感觉，与我“秘密拜访”您箱中湖泊时产生的眩晕感十分类似……
Marguerite: Symptoms that closely resemble what I experienced when you secretly "invited" me to the lake in your Suitcase.

玛格丽特：所以，司辰，我想让您来听听里面有什么，通过您来间接观察内部的状况。请放心，如有意外，小春雀儿已经提前在康复中心准备好了舒适的床铺。
Marguerite: It's likely, Vertin, that you are the only one able to remain conscious while listening to the device. And don't worry. Eagle has already booked a VIP room for you at the rehab center, just in case.

维尔汀：那就试试吧。
Vertin: I see. Well, I suppose there's only one way to find out.

靠近仪器，取下帽子，好让头能放进倒扣的半球形装置。
The hat is removed, allowing the head to fit more easily inside the dome.

玛格丽特：参照物参数顺利建立。可以开始观测了。
Marguerite: Virtual environmental model constructed. You may begin at any time.

玛格丽特：无名者小姐，麻烦你将潘多拉的小盒子放在托盘上……
Marguerite: Ms. Stranger, place the Pandora's box on the tray, if you would.

玛格丽特：……果然这……原始……有机的汇聚……
Marguerite: ... Just as ... the cube ... superposition ... ever-changing matter ...

玛格丽特：……量化分析……比较符合模拟……不对……
Marguerite: It seems feasible ... the system ... aligns well with our simulated ... Wait—

无名者：……司辰……似乎……
Ms. Stranger: ... Timekeeper ... seems ...

维尔汀：……？！
Vertin: ...?!

耳鸣停止了。同一时间，完全指向性虚拟监听仪在一阵危险的警报声中停止了运作。
The ringing in the ears fades, only to be replaced by the wailing of the device's alarm.

十四行诗：仪器……冒烟了，是不是发生了什么意外……
Sonetto: The machine is smoking. Did something go wrong?

十四行诗：司辰，你醒了。你感觉还好吗？
Sonetto: Timekeeper! Are you alright?

维尔汀：我没事，十四行诗。只是有一些耳鸣。
Vertin: I'm fine. My ears are ringing, but that's all.

帽子被重新戴回头上，遮住半边如同大梦初醒的脸。
The hat is put back on, concealing still-spinning eyes beneath its brim.

玛格丽特：如我所料，亲爱的司辰，您并没有产生任何不适——但这台机器可就不好说了。
Marguerite: So you were able to withstand whatever's inside the cube, as expected, but it looks like our monitoring device wasn't.

玛格丽特：监听仪需要承受的混沌信息比预计的上限要高出许多，以至于它彻底罢工了。按理来说，阿派朗的卷轴应该能发挥用处才对……
Marguerite: The overload of chaos data caused it to break down, though it should've been fine under the protection of the Apeiron scroll.

在导师沉浸于仪器的受损问题时，小助手正在一旁忠实地记录方才实验的情况。
Eagle diligently jots down notes as she provides a full assessment of the damage.

小春雀儿：司辰，在监听实验期间，你有听见什么吗？
Eagle: Did you hear anything during the experiment?

维尔汀：我没有听到很具体的内容，但是的确听到了一些声音。它难以形容，就像是……流沙一样。
Vertin: I did. But I don't quite know how to describe it. It was almost like the sound of sinking quicksand?

维尔汀：在以往，我似乎接触过类似的力量与存在。具体情形我无法确定，但它十分危险。
Vertin: I think I've made contact with similar powers before. I'm not sure what it is, but I know it could be very dangerous.

小春雀儿：无名者小姐说得没错。在弄清楚内容物之前，贸然打开外壳的风险确实很高……
Eagle: Then Ms. Stranger was right. It could be risky to break the shell before we find out what's inside.

玛格丽特：但在监听仪没有进展的时候，这也不失为一种可行的尝试。
Marguerite: It's still an option worth considering, since we've gotten nothing from the monitoring device.

富有“冒险精神”的研究员重新把自己的注意力汇聚过来。
She lets the undertone of excitement bleed into her voice intentionally as she focuses her attention on the artifact.

玛格丽特：我们要把盒子打开吗，司辰？
Marguerite: Well, Timekeeper? Shall we open it?

维尔汀：不，目前我认为打开它的风险太大。
Vertin: No. The risk is too high.

玛格丽特：真是可惜。那日后，如果您改变了主意——
Marguerite: Shame. Anyway, if you change your mind ...

苍白的研究员拿出一颗胶囊状的物品。
She produces a capsule-shaped item.

玛格丽特：在我们研制监听仪的同时，我们的网上邻居顾问先生已经率先炼制出了能够摧毁外壳的炼金药剂。
Marguerite: Use this. Our virtual neighbor came up with this capsule while we were working on the monitoring device.

玛格丽特：胶囊通过一道简单的咒文启动，预计在七秒内就能将外壳溶解。不过之后会发生什么我们就无法预测了。
Marguerite: It activates with a simple incantation. It'll take about seven seconds to dissolve the shell. After that, who knows what might happen.

玛格丽特：稍后我会教您如何念诵开启咒文，它只需要您具备第一防线学校九年级学生的咒语水平。
Marguerite: I'll teach you the incantation later. It's easy, as long as you passed the SPDM's ninth-grade arcane skills class.

选项 / Choices

1. 接受
1. Accept

2. 确认年级问题
2. Talk about ninth grade

分支 / Branch 1

维尔汀：谢谢，我会好好保管它。
Vertin: Thank you. I'll treat it with the utmost care.

玛格丽特：希望届时，您能将盒中所藏的秘密与我们共享。
Marguerite: Good. I look forward to hearing its secrets. If you decide to open it, that is.

分支 / Branch 2

维尔汀：其实我的九年级并没有在教室中度过。
Vertin: As it happens, I wasn't in class for most of ninth grade.

玛格丽特：那么——我相信您在担任司辰的过程中已经充分掌握了更高级的咒语知识。
Marguerite: Really? Either way, I'm sure being the Timekeeper has given you more than enough experience to handle it.

共同接续 / Shared continuation

玛格丽特微笑着移交了药剂。
Marguerite hands over the capsule with a practiced smile.

10  量化痛苦 / Quantifying Pain

基金会诊疗记录档案
The Foundation's Medical Record Archive

密级：高
患者代号：飞蛾
病情记录：
经检查，自南极行动中归来的患者因在长期卧底任务中频繁进行人格覆写，超出安全迭代频率，导致其脑内的趋光性定律已出现多处不稳定片段，或已影响部分覆写效果，可能提升患者人格崩溃概率。
患者脑电波可见快波增多，提示神经元代偿性过劳；脑内神经递质浓度出现失衡，多巴胺水平降至正常值60%，血清素合成效率下降28%；内侧前额叶皮层出现物理性损伤；脑组织边缘系统轻微水肿……
……
同时患者因于南极超负荷使用神秘术，长时间处于灵性枯竭状态，当前恢复情况不容乐观，近期需严格控制其神秘术使用频率，以防造成永久不可逆损伤。
在精神评估中，患者表现出轻微的自我认知障碍……
……
诊断结论：
患者大脑损伤整体处于可控范围，尽管本应在间谍任务结束后立即执行深度人格覆写，但鉴于其在本次深入重塑之手内部时获取了关键的敌方信息与知识，现决定采取非常规处理方案，暂不执行趋光性定律覆写，仅定期注射神经修复肽以维护患者大脑状态。在此期间需密切观察患者心理状态，确认其认知锚定结果。
Classification: High
Patient Codename: Moth
Medical Record:
Examination reveals that the patient, having returned from the Antarctic Operation, performed repeated identity overwrites throughout their long-term undercover assignment, exceeding the safe iteration threshold. The patient's Phototaxis now shows multiple unstable segments, which may affect overwrite efficacy and increase the risk of personality collapse.
Brainwave activity shows an increase in fast waves, indicating compensatory neuronal overload. Neurotransmitter concentrations are imbalanced: dopamine levels have dropped to 60% of the normal range, and serotonin synthesis efficiency has decreased by 28%. Physical damage is present in the medial prefrontal cortex, with mild edema in the limbic system ...
...
The patient also overextended her use of arcane skills during the Antarctic Operation, resulting in prolonged pneuma depletion. Recovery prospects are not encouraging. Strict limits must be placed on her arcane skill usage in the near term to prevent irreversible damage.
During psychological evaluation, the patient exhibited mild self-perception disorder ...
...
Diagnosis:
The patient's brain damage is largely within controllable limits. Although a deep personality overwrite should be performed upon completion of the undercover assignment, given the critical enemy intelligence and knowledge obtained during this infiltration of Manus Vindictae, an unconventional approach has been approved: the Phototaxis overwrite will be suspended for the time being. The patient will instead receive regular injections of neural repair peptides to maintain brain function. During this period, the patient's psychological state must be closely monitored to confirm the results of cognitive anchoring.

11  石鸽 · 1/2 / Stone Doves · 1/2

石鸽
Stone Doves

中央瞭望塔·顶层
Top Floor, Central Watchtower

梅蕾尔：唔哦……
Merel: Whoa!

石鸽子似休憩般落在它下方的基座上，向四周的空旷中投射柔和的光芒。
The large Shelter Dove casts a soft glow over its perch.

梅蕾尔：是我的心理作用，还是中央瞭望塔的鸽子确实比其他四座塔楼上的都要大？
Merel: Is it just me, or is this Dove bigger than the others?

玛蒂尔达：这不是你的错觉。
Matilda: It is much bigger than the other four.

玛蒂尔达：我们面前的这个是主气象鸽，它带来的免疫范围几乎能覆盖整个学校。
Matilda: And that is for a very good reason. This is the primary Dove.

玛蒂尔达：不过难免在四个角落里会有缺漏的地方，所以四座瞭望塔上会有小型气象鸽作为补充。
Matilda: It covers the largest area of the campus, while the other four cover the corners.

梅蕾尔：第一防线学校就是靠这些才躲过了一次次“暴雨”……
Merel: To think that the SPDM's been kept safe all this time by these things.

梅蕾尔上前一步，按照惯例，插入了手中的校验器。
Merel takes a step forward and, just as before, inserts the tester.

她又听见了仪器运作的轻微嗡鸣声，数秒后，室内复归平静。
The tester whirs to life, and then just as quickly, the sound fades, replaced by a triumphant hue.

梅蕾尔：三盏绿灯亮，校验结果正常。
Merel: All green. Shelter Dove's functional.

梅蕾尔：这样一来，所有的气象鸽就都检查过一遍了。
Merel: That's it. We've checked all of them.

梅蕾尔：我们现在是不是可以休息了，玛蒂尔达？
Merel: So, snack break, then?

玛蒂尔达：你在想什么，现在当然还不到能休息的时候。我们要保持巡逻状态，直到“暴雨”结束为止。
Matilda: Certainly not! We need to keep patrolling the grounds until the "Storm" is over and everyone is safe.

梅蕾尔：就这样绕着学校兜圈子，爬很高的塔楼……
Merel: Oh. So, what, we just keep circling around the school, going up and down the watchtowers?

玛蒂尔达：虽然我一开始也觉得这项工作有些太没有挑战了，不过……
Matilda: I know it is far from the sort of glorious mission that the Great Matilda Bouanich is capable of.

玛蒂尔达：我们身上肩负的是保护全体师生安全的责任！
Matilda: But just think, we are protecting all the staff and students of the school!

玛蒂尔达：在我们读书的时候，一定也有人在我们不知道的地方做这种无聊但重要的工作。
Matilda: Someone must have done the same for us when we were students.

梅蕾尔：好吧，我明白了。
Merel: Yeah. I get it.

梅蕾尔突然轻松地笑起来。
She chuckles to herself.

梅蕾尔：至少这份工作要比在战场上活命轻松，而且……这也比压缩军粮好吃多了。
Merel: *sigh* I shouldn't complain. I'd much rather do this than fight on the battlefield. And the snacks here are better than the MREs, that's for sure.

她从口袋里摸出两根巧克力能量棒，将其中一根递到玛蒂尔达面前。
She pulls two chocolate-flavored energy bars from her pocket and offers one to Matilda.

梅蕾尔：现在应该不会有教员来没收零食了。
Merel: Plus, we don't have to hide them from the instructors anymore.

玛蒂尔达：……谢、谢谢，确实不能饿着肚子工作。
Matilda: Oh, th-thank you. Um, I suppose it is inefficient to work on empty bellies.

玛蒂尔达接过校友递来的小零食。
After a moment of hesitation, she reaches out to take the bar.

梅蕾尔：这是我从90年代带过来的，这个黄油莓果口味的只在那几年有生产。它在我的房间里已经放了挺久，不过应该还能吃。
Merel: They're butter berry flavor, only produced in the 90s. I guess that makes them a little old, but they should taste fine.

梅蕾尔：外包装我已经换掉了，没有不应该出现在学校的年代信息。
Merel: Ignore the packaging. I swapped it out to avoid leaking time period info.

玛蒂尔达：你还挺细心的……
Matilda: Quite thoughtful of you.

玛蒂尔达：我们走吧，记得锁上平台的防爆门，以免学生溜到他们不该去的地方。
Matilda: Let's go. Remember to lock the blast door in case any students end up wandering around near these restricted areas.

玛蒂尔达：拉文德？
Matilda: Yes, Lavender?

拉文德：玛蒂尔达小姐，你现在能来一趟西南瞭望塔吗？
Lavender: Ms. Matilda, could you go to the southwestern watchtower right now?

玛蒂尔达：发生什么事了？
Matilda: What's wrong?

拉文德：我在学校入口附近巡逻的时候，看见一些陌生人在往西南瞭望塔的方向去。
Lavender: There are some strangers at the school gate that are staring in its direction.

Warehouse, Air Raid Shelter

经过历练的校级优等生已然可以在任何战斗中保持冷静。

13  库存告急 / Low on Supplies

一盘难求
Running Out of Disks

关于本时代传送软盘使用现状的简要说明

一、 基本情况
近期，总部配发给各部门的任务器材“传送软盘”的库存极度紧张。该器材为一次性消耗品，且单次传送人数十分有限，鉴于库存数量，目前仍实行紧急配给制度，主要保障一线战备及核心任务单位的必要需求，除特殊突发状况与重大需要外，不予批准使用申请。

二、 现状与原因
目前，“传送软盘”主要原材料的唯一已知产地位于战争核心冲突地区，开采作业已全面中止，且运输线路受阻。受此不可控因素影响，“传送软盘”现阶段产量大幅缩减，库存量持续下降，补充周期无法确定。

三、 结论
受外部原料供应中断的直接影响，“传送软盘”库存进入稀缺状态，科算中心正在着手研究替代材料。总部要求各部门严格执行配给标准，优化使用策略，务必审慎评估作战目标，将软盘预留在改变战局的关键节点使用，以应对当前及后续一段时期的供应压力。
A Brief Overview of Teleport Disk Usage in the Current Era

1. General Situation
Headquarters's teleport disk supply across all departments is critically low. As a single-use item with a limited capacity per teleportation, emergency rationing remains in effect based on current inventory. Priority is given to frontline and core mission units. Requests for additional supplies will not be approved outside of special emergencies or critical needs.

2. Cause and Background
The only known source of the primary materials for teleport disks is located in the core conflict zone of the ongoing war. Extraction operations have been fully suspended, and supply routes remain blocked. As a result of these uncontrollable factors, teleport disk production has dropped significantly, inventory continues to decline, and resupply remains uncertain.

3. Conclusion
Due to the disruption of material supplies, teleport disk stocks have entered a critical shortage. The Laplace Scientific Computing Center is actively researching substitute materials. Headquarters requires all departments to strictly adhere to rationing standards and optimize usage strategy. All combat objectives must be carefully evaluated, with disks reserved for critical junctures capable of turning the tide of battle, in order to manage current and future supply pressures.

14  毕业生们 · 1/2 / The Alumni · 1/2

毕业生们
