槲寄生：司辰小队目前不被允许离开基金会总部，对吗？
Druvis III: We won't be leaving headquarters, will we?

无名者：没错，不过我不适合在那样的氛围下告诉他们这个消息。
Ms. Stranger: No, but I didn't have the heart to rain on their parade.

无名者：你们将作为预备单元的成员，接替已经出发的第一梯队，留在基金会总部，保障总部的安全。
Ms. Stranger: Your job is to protect headquarters, now that the First Squad has left.

无名者：雨前调度中心不希望扰乱基金会原本的收容程序，总部的工作和支援第一防线学校同等重要。
Ms. Stranger: Dispatch doesn't want the emergency at the SPDM to interrupt the admission process. Both are equally important.

槲寄生：过去几个月，基金会的人员规模扩充到了前所未有的程度，应该不会像我加入的时候那样缺乏人手。
Druvis III: Hasn't the Foundation been recruiting more talent than ever in the past few months? I doubt manpower is a problem anymore.

槲寄生：司辰小队是否可以被允许外出，与维尔汀一同面对危险？
Druvis III: Is it in any way possible to reassign this duty to another unit so we can reinforce the Timekeeper?

无名者：我们确实在这个时代招募了很多人手，但正因为他们来自“这个时代”，所以大多都被认为是“不可用”的人。
Ms. Stranger: We have expanded our staff considerably, but many of the new members are from the current era. This is their first "Storm." Their reaction to it could be unpredictable.

无名者：他们尚未见证“暴雨”，这会给雨前收容工作带来很大的不稳定性。在“暴雨”期间，这些人反倒是需要付出精力去监管的对象。
Ms. Stranger: Unfortunately, we've determined it's best not to put them on any tasks in the lead-up to it. In fact, we've been keeping them under close surveillance.

无名者：尤其是他们中的许多人来自不久前还互相敌对的国家或阵营。
Ms. Stranger: It doesn't help that many of them still carry grudges from the war. Not all of them were on the same side.

无名者：第一梯队的成员都拥有与重塑之手作战的经验，只要他们抵达现场，一定可以控制住局势。
Ms. Stranger: Still, there's no need to worry for your friends. The First Squad are experienced in dealing with Manus Vindictae. The Timekeeper is in safe hands.

她顿了顿，侧耳聆听飞蛾传来的声音。
She pauses to listen to the moth beside her ear.

无名者：你可以想办法安抚那些孩子们的情绪吗？
Ms. Stranger: I don't suppose you can manage to pacify them?

槲寄生：他们向来不太服从管理。
Druvis III: They're hardly what I would call "obedient."

无名者：基金会通常也不会对神秘学家施与多少自由。
Ms. Stranger: Then they'll have to learn to live with it. The Foundation has rules for a reason.

槲寄生：……
Druvis III: ...

槲寄生：我可以尝试说服他们，只不过我不能保证会有多少效果。
Druvis III: I'll do my best. But I can't promise anything.

槲寄生：在我最初来到此地时，基金会就以研究的名义强制回收过维尔汀的手提箱。
Druvis III: The first time we came to the Foundation, they also took the Suitcase from Vertin under the guise of "research."

槲寄生：现在，相似的情况又一次发生，司辰小队里已经积累了许多不满的情绪。
Druvis III: I'm afraid there isn't much I can do to dissuade their suspicion, nor their dissatisfaction.

无名者：谢谢，有你的尝试就够了，槲寄生小姐。
Ms. Stranger: I'm grateful as long as you are willing to try, Ms. Druvis.

无名者：不久之后“剃刀”会和你们联络，他会交给你们合适的工作。
Ms. Stranger: "Razor" will contact you soon to give you further instructions.

在离去之前，无名者再次看了一眼司辰小队落脚处紧闭的房门。
Before she leaves, Ms. Stranger pauses at the door to Team Timekeeper's temporary lodgings.

无名者：很显然……你我都不太喜欢自己在这场雨中扮演的角色。
Ms. Stranger: Apparently, neither you nor I like the characters we're playing on this stage.

防空区·杂物间
Broom Closet, Air Raid Shelter

维尔汀：玛蒂尔达还好吗，十四行诗？
Vertin: How's Matilda doing?

十四行诗：没有非常严重的外伤，只不过我无法将她从昏迷状态中唤醒。
Sonetto: Her vitals are strong, and she doesn't appear to have any dangerous wounds, but she still hasn't come to.

十四行诗：她随身携带的水晶球破裂了，也许是它帮玛蒂尔达挡下了最致命的伤害……但她还是需要专业的医疗援助。
Sonetto: Her crystal ball is broken. I suspect it took the brunt of the damage. But we need to get her some professional medical help to know more.

维尔汀：牙仙女士带着纸信圈儿去英国分部了，她们会在那里就地避难。
Vertin: If only Ms. Tooth Fairy weren't at the UK branch with Flutterpage right now.

维尔汀：但学校里应该还有其他校医值守，我们可以先把玛蒂尔达带去安全的地方，再寻求校医的帮助。
Vertin: Still, there should be medical staff on-site at the school. Let's move Matilda somewhere safe and start looking.

梅蕾尔：维尔汀——
Merel: Vertin!

梅蕾尔灰头土脸地从角落中钻出来。
A dusty, disheveled Merel emerges from the corner.

梅蕾尔：我检查过了，防空设施内已经没有其他学生了。但之前的战斗让一些墙体坍塌了，堵住了通往其他地下设施的路。
Merel: I've checked the shelter. There aren't any other students or staff. Some walls have collapsed, though. We can't access the other underground facilities.

维尔汀：现在没时间清理残骸，我们只能走地面转移了。
Vertin: There's no time to clear debris. We'll have to move above ground.

梅蕾尔：我们不在这里等援军过来吗？
Merel: Aren't we going to wait for reinforcements?

维尔汀：援军到来还需要一段时间，在那之前，我想尽量多为学校提供一些帮助。
Vertin: No. The clock is ticking. We can't afford to wait.

被分配任务后，提前读过说明书的十四行诗低头思索着。
Sonetto, having already reviewed the manual from cover to cover after receiving their assignment, lowers her head in contemplation.

十四行诗：我们头顶的暴雨气象稳定鸽已经被破坏，备用气象鸽在没有基座的情况下只能维持十分钟左右。
Sonetto: This tower's Shelter Dove has been destroyed. And the backup can only keep us safe for ten minutes without a pedestal.

维尔汀：而我的手提箱不在身边，继续留在这里，很可能会让你们感染上暴雨症候。
Vertin: And to make matters worse, I don't have my Suitcase. If things go wrong, you could all be infected with the Storm Syndrome.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

梅蕾尔不自觉地望向躺在地上的“石鸽子”，相比数分钟之前，它的光芒已经黯淡了许多。
Merel can't help but look at the Shelter Dove lying on the ground. Though only a few minutes have passed, its glow has dimmed significantly.

梅蕾尔：那……我们现在应该往哪里去？
Merel: So, where should we go now?

维尔汀：中央瞭望塔是学校指定的应急避险设施。
Vertin: Normally, I'd suggest the central watchtower. It's where the instructors used to tell us to go in case of emergencies.

维尔汀：但我们不能带着玛蒂尔达和贝弗利穿越如此长距离的开阔地。
Vertin: But I don't imagine we'd make it that far with a comatose patient and a child.

维尔汀：我们可以先去图书馆，那是离我们最近，最稳定的建筑。
Vertin: Our best choice is the library. It's close by and "Storm"-proof.

维尔汀：里面也许能找到一些派得上用场的东西，也有可能找到学校里的其他人。
Vertin: With any luck, we'll find something useful, or at least some other people.

第一助手仍忧心忡忡。
Beneath her calm exterior, new worries surface in the chief assistant's mind.

十四行诗：如果图书馆已经被重塑之手占领了怎么办？
Sonetto: What if the Manus are already there?

维尔汀：敌人不多的话，可以试着解决他们。
实在难以应付就借道图书馆，继续向校医室转移——玛蒂尔达也需要医疗援助。
Vertin: We take them down. And if there are too many of them, we'll pass through it to the clinic. We need to take Matilda there anyway.

维尔汀：“暴雨”正在逼近，无论如何我们都不能继续留在原地了。
Vertin: The plan is settled then. We'd better get moving. The "Storm" waits for no one.

维尔汀：前往图书馆的路上可能还会遇到重塑之手的信徒，我可以负责照顾玛蒂尔达和贝弗利。
Vertin: If combat breaks out, I'll do what I can to protect Matilda and Beverly.

维尔汀：十四行诗，梅蕾尔，接下来的战斗就交给你们了。
Vertin: I'll trust you both to handle whatever stands in our way.

十四行诗：没问题。
Sonetto: Understood.

梅蕾尔：我、我能行的，交给我吧。
Merel: S-Sure. Just leave it to us.

维尔汀：叫上贝弗利，告诉他我们马上出发。
Vertin: Alright. Grab Beverly, and let's move.

学校小径
Path, SPDM

梅蕾尔：十四行诗——
Merel: Sonetto!

十四行诗：Regna sereno intenso ed infinito！
Sonetto: Regna   sereno   intenso   ed   infinito!

辉光从笔尖奔涌而出，随后凝聚于空中，又如利剑般坠落。
Light flows from the tip of her glasfeder, condenses, then cleaves through the air like a sword.

重塑·信徒：ㄜ——
Manus Believer: ㄜ ...

梅蕾尔：它倒下了。维尔汀，贝弗利，你们可以过来了。
Merel: *exhalation* Vertin, Beverly, all clear.

贝弗利：哇……
Beverly: Wow!

维尔汀：别怕，睁开眼，注意脚下。
Vertin: Stay calm and keep your eyes open. Watch your step.

梅蕾尔：维尔汀？你在看什么？
Merel: Vertin? What are you looking at?

维尔汀：……没事，先进图书馆再说。
Vertin: Nothing. Let's get inside.

维尔汀：前面就是图书馆的侧门了，快跑。
Vertin: The side entrance is right ahead. Keep going.

梅蕾尔：我去帮你们开门！
Merel: Let me get the door.

毕业生转身，刚要拔腿猛奔的一瞬间，图书馆的侧门豁然洞开。
Merel turns. Just as she readies herself to run, the library's side door swings open.

梅蕾尔：……？！
Merel: ...?!

拉文德：各位，快进来！
Lavender: Everyone, get in here. Quickly.

图书馆·走廊
Hallway, Library

大门在身后关上的一瞬间，门后等候多时的学生们一拥而上，合力搬来桌椅堵住大门。
The door is slammed shut, and a small cadre of students inside slide desks and chairs into place behind them to reinforce it.

贝弗利：莫斯，兰迪！你们都还活着！
Beverly: Moss! Randy! You're alright!

贝弗利奔向自己的同班同学们。
Beverly runs toward his classmates.

一排排书柜将图书馆分隔成了许多个小隔间，几乎每个隔间中都蜷缩有一身白色制服的孩子。
Rows of bookshelves divide the library into a series of compact alcoves, nearly every one of which is now filled with children in white uniforms.

梅蕾尔：原来这里还有这么多学生……
Merel: All these students ...

新任纪律辅员愣在原地，吃惊地看着被抱进图书馆、昏迷不醒的玛蒂尔达。
Lavender stands in shock as she watches the unconscious Matilda being carried into the library.

拉文德：这是……玛蒂尔达小姐怎么了？
Lavender: What-What happened to Ms. Matilda?

维尔汀：她受了伤，我们想先找一个安静的房间安置伤员，小心不要频繁地挪动她的头部。
Vertin: She's wounded. Could you find a quiet room and help me move her there? She may have suffered some trauma to her head, so we need to be very careful.

梅蕾尔：我记得图书馆的高级阅览室有沙发，还在吗？
Merel: I remember there were couches in the reading room. Are they still there?

拉文德：在的，需要我帮忙吗？
Lavender: They are. Do you remember where it is?

梅蕾尔：不用了，维尔汀，我来帮你抬玛蒂尔达。
Merel: Yeah. Vertin, let me help you put Matilda there.

维尔汀：好的。
Vertin: Alright.

拉文德：……
Lavender: ...

拉文德转向剩下的一名毕业生。
Lavender makes a sharp and precise turn to face Sonetto.

拉文德：十四行诗小姐，我是纪律辅员拉文德，九年级生。
Lavender: Ms. Sonetto, my name is Lavender, Ninth Grade Monitor Assistant. It's an honor to meet you.

十四行诗：你认识我？
Sonetto: An honor? You know about me?

拉文德：我相信这里有很多学生都认识你，过去我们经常能在巡礼上见到你。
Lavender: You're an SPDM legend! A lot of us know you from the school parade.

传奇般的优等生此时不知该如何回应。
Said legend finds herself struggling to accept the sudden burst of praise.

十四行诗：这是……我的荣幸，拉文德。这里的学生都是你带到图书馆来避难的？
Sonetto: I'm flattered. It's very nice to meet you, Lavender. Are you the one who led all these students here?

拉文德：嗯，爆炸发生后，我和其他的高年级生一起把参加典礼的学生们带了过来。
Lavender: That's right. I'd just gotten back to the ceremony when the explosions went off. The instructors told us ninth-graders to lead all the students here.

拉文德：我们受过应急情况培训，而且还是这里年纪最大的人，自然要照顾低年级的学生……不过……还好现在有毕业生在……
Lavender: We're the oldest students and the only ones who have received any real training, so it's our duty to protect the lower grades. But, honestly, thank goodness you showed up.

拉文德尽量让自己的语言有条理，但看得出来，她并没有准备好面对如此危急的情况。
It's clear she's attempting to seem more in control than she truly feels. The scale and danger of this crisis are far beyond anything she could have prepared for.

十四行诗：校园警卫和其他老师呢？
Sonetto: Where are the guards and your instructors now?

拉文德：没有见到他们，只有几个原本留在图书馆的老师，但他们现在……状态不太好。
Lavender: I don't know. They just ordered us to head to the library. A few of them came with us, but they're not doing so well.

拉文德：在组织学生就近避难的时候，他们在和重塑之手的作战中受伤了。
Lavender: They got hurt protecting us from the Manus on our way over.

十四行诗：你知道重塑之手？
Sonetto: You know about Manus Vindictae?

拉文德：嗯，还有……“暴雨”，作为纪律辅员，我被允许提前了解过一些。现在正在发生的就是“暴雨”，对吧？
Lavender: Yes, and the "Storm" too. I was told about it as a part of my role as monitor assistant. All these things that are happening means the "Storm" is about to arrive, doesn't it?

拉文德：我、我知道在学校里不能谈论这件事，但是已经发生了这种事……
Lavender: I-I understand that we're not allowed to talk about it on school grounds, but given the situation ...

十四行诗：没关系，知道发生了什么才能更好地规避风险。
Sonetto: I think we can make an exception. You need to be prepared for the possible dangers ahead.

拉文德：……这个时候能看见你真是太好了，你肯定能比我做得更好。你一定是基金会派来指挥学校应急避难的。
Lavender: Thank you so much, miss. You don't know how relieved I was when I saw you coming to our rescue. So, you were sent here by the Foundation, right?

拉文德：基金会一定已经接到警报了吧，还会有其他人来帮助我们吗？
Lavender: That means headquarters already knows what's going on. Will there be more reinforcements?

十四行诗：我们是先头队伍，更大规模的援军就在路上。等支援赶到学校，一定会没事的。
Sonetto: Yes, and they're already on their way. We'll be safe soon.

拉文德：那就……太好了，在这期间我们就听你的了。刚才送玛蒂尔达去休息的人是你的助手吗？
Lavender: Thank goodness! Phew. We'll follow your orders until then. By the way, who's that woman who was carrying Matilda? Is she your assistant?

十四行诗：……
Sonetto: ...

十四行诗：她是维尔汀，基金会的司辰，我是她的第一助手。
Sonetto: That was Vertin, the Foundation's Timekeeper. And, in fact, I'm her chief assistant.

拉文德有些不可思议地睁大眼睛。
Lavender's eyes open wide in disbelief.

拉文德：那是……维尔汀？那个维尔汀？逃学之后被开除的维尔汀？你居然成了她的助手——
Lavender: You mean that was Vertin? THE Vertin?! I heard she ran away from the school and got expelled. How did you come to be her assistant?

维尔汀：我确实是维尔汀，不过那不是开除。
Vertin: Yes, I'm the Vertin you've heard about. But I was never expelled.

拉文德：——？！
Lavender: —?!

拉文德：抱、抱歉，你是什么时候……
Lavender: S-Sorry. When did you ...?

维尔汀：没关系，那之后我确实不常回到学校了。
Vertin: It's more than alright. It's only natural that people would start rumors with me coming here so rarely.

拉文德没想到自己会一下子撞上如此多的“校园传奇”。
Lavender never imagined she'd run into so many "campus legends" all at once.

拉文德：我还不太明白……不，我明白了。我会尽全力协助你的，维尔……司辰。
Lavender: How could it be? Ah, never mind. I'll do whatever I can to help, Ver—Ms. Timekeeper.

维尔汀：叫我维尔汀就好，拉文德。
Vertin: Vertin is just fine, Lavender.

维尔汀：能请你再详细地和我说说图书馆的情况吗？
Vertin: Could you tell me more about the situation here? We need all the details.

拉文德：可以，不过这里低年级的学生太多了，请你们和我到自习室去吧。
Lavender: Of course, but it might be better to go somewhere that the other students can't hear us.

15  落石路 · 1/2 / Bumpy Roads · 1/2

落石路
Bumpy Roads

图书馆·自习室
Study Room, Library

拉文德：……我知道的大概就这么多。
Lavender: That's everything I know.

维尔汀：谢谢。
Vertin: Thank you.

一张从图书馆“借阅”的校园平面图摊开在桌面上。
A map of the campus "on loan" from the library is laid out over the table.

维尔汀：好消息是，大部分的学生都在图书馆内，暂时是安全的。但眼下还有很多坏消息。
Vertin: The good news is most of the students are accounted for within the library, and it's secure for now. But, that's basically where the good news ends.

维尔汀：老师和警卫人员都失踪了。如果一切顺利，他们只是按照避难程序撤向中央瞭望塔。但……我不认为他们会自愿抛下这里的学生。
Vertin: We haven't seen any signs of the instructors nor the school security. They may have retreated into the central watchtower, but if that's the case, I don't understand why they would send the children here.

维尔汀：我在安置玛蒂尔达的时候观察了一下校园内的情况，现在要前往中央瞭望塔已经非常困难。
Vertin: We've viewed what we can from the windows in the reading room. It seems impossible to reach the central watchtower safely.

维尔汀：光是开阔区域内就能看见很多重塑之手的信徒在游荡，我们很难穿过中央广场。
Vertin: The campus is crawling with Manus believers, and that's just the ones we can see. I doubt we could even make it as far as the central square.

维尔汀：而且我还观察到，校园四角的瞭望塔塔顶都遭到了破坏，那里的气象鸽平台应该已经失效。
Vertin: All four corner watchtowers are damaged, and I believe their Shelter Doves have been disabled.

维尔汀：图书馆内并没有可用的气象鸽。目前这里仍在免疫区内，只是因为中央瞭望塔的主气象鸽还在发挥作用。
Vertin: As for the library, it's only safe because the Dove in the central watchtower is still operational.

梅蕾尔：太好了，只要我们守住图书馆……
Merel: So we just bunker down here and wait for reinforcements, right?

维尔汀：……
Vertin: ...

维尔汀：但在从西南瞭望塔转移到图书馆的过程中，我发现有效免疫区正在以中央瞭望塔为核心收缩。
Vertin: I'm afraid we can't. The central tower's immunity range is shrinking.

梅蕾尔：什、什么？！
Merel: What?

维尔汀：按照收缩的速度计算，图书馆建筑有可能在“暴雨”落下前就暴露在免疫区之外。
Vertin: Unfortunately, if I'm correct, the library will be exposed before the "Storm" arrives.

十四行诗：可要带领学生们前往中央瞭望塔的话，需要先清理掉路上的敌人，凭借我们的力量……
Sonetto: Then we need to make our way to the central watchtower. But I'm not sure we have the strength to clear a path there.

十四行诗：图书馆结构比较复杂，建筑整体也很结实，依靠我们和高年级学生应该还能暂时抵挡住重塑之手的攻击，只不过……
Sonetto: We can take advantage of the library's layout to hold the line for a little longer, but ...

十四行诗：如果免疫区一直缩小，图书馆迟早会被“暴雨”影响。
Sonetto: If the major Dove's immunity range keeps shrinking, it won't make much difference.

梅蕾尔：那、那我们现在应该怎么做？
Merel: So, then, what do we do?

维尔汀：基金会的援军。
Vertin: We wait.

维尔汀：他们应该已经快到了，他们会带来足够多的平衡伞。
Vertin: The Foundation's reinforcements should be here any minute. They'll have brought enough Equilibrium Umbrellas for everyone.

车内
Inside the Car

五分钟前。
5 minutes earlier.

叶尔莫莱：抓稳了！
Yermolai: Hold on tight!

飞驰的汽车猛地向地面一扎。
The speeding armored vehicle collides with the ground.

帕拉维扬&洛佩拉：啊啊啊——
Paravyan & Lopera: Ah!

半秒后，全副武装的地面载具昂起车头，如同破浪的舰船般从一处深坑中冲出。
Its front lifts as it rises out of the pit like a ship cresting over a churning sea.

坐在后排的队员在安全带的束缚下依旧在颠簸中摔得东倒西歪。
他们望向车外，蜿蜒的山路不断延伸。
Even with their seat belts fastened tight, the impact jostles the occupants. As they regain composure, eyes shift to the winding mountain road ahead.

洛佩拉：为什么基金会的路这么烂！
Lopera: Why are these roads so bumpy?

叶尔莫莱：第一次走？这里的山路就这样，不管维护多少次，没多久就会被两边坡上滚下来的石头砸出新坑。
Yermolai: What, haven't driven around here much yet? You'll get used to it. Rocks slide down off the mountain from time to time.

帕拉维扬：我们离第一防线学校还有多远？
Paravyan: How far are we from the SPDM?

叶尔莫莱：按照经验，再开不到一刻钟就能到那座鸽子笼了。
Yermolai: Fifteen minutes, give or take.

洛佩拉：重塑之手到底是怎么混进学校的？那里的安保都没有检查出来吗？
Lopera: How did Manus Vindictae manage to infiltrate the school? Security didn't notice anything?

叶尔莫莱：学校那些只配了麻醉枪的安保可应付不来重塑之手。
Yermolai: Tsk. The guards are only equipped with tranq guns. They wouldn't have been much use against the Manus.

叶尔莫莱：这也不是重塑之手第一次袭击学校。
Yermolai: This isn't actually the first time they've attacked the SPDM.

叶尔莫莱：不过以前都只是用飞行魔精骚扰一下空域，这次竟然派来了作战能力更强的地面部队。
Yermolai: But this time they've sent an army instead of Olitiaus. Won't be quite so easy to deal with.

洛佩拉扭过身子，透过后窗玻璃瞥了一眼身后尾随的车队。
Lopera twists around to survey the convoy behind them.

洛佩拉：不用担心，我们的人手也不少。
Lopera: That won't be a problem. We're more than ready, aren't we?

帕拉维扬：“乌鸫”早些时候也被调到学校去了，我本来还以为她终于可以松口气了……希望她没事……可她怎么可能没事呢……
Paravyan: Blackbird was at the school before the attack. Just when I thought she might get to take a break. I hope she's alright. Well, she's probably completely freaked out, but I hope she's safe, at least.

叶尔莫莱：“警官”，别提前想那么多倒霉事，专注你自己当下的任务。
Yermolai: Quit your whinging, Beagle. Focus on the mission.

叶尔莫莱：……我倒是相信她肯定能行。
Yermolai: Our little birdie's gonna be just fine.

洛佩拉：“乌鸫”？你们的朋友？
Lopera: Is this Blackbird a friend of yours?

叶尔莫莱：没错，她最擅长的事就是在所有要人命的状况中活下来，就连南极和欧洲的战场都杀不死她。
Yermolai: Yeah. She might not think she's a soldier, but she's a survivor. If Antarctica and the Eastern Front couldn't kill her, she can handle a school.

帕拉维扬：不过在南极的时候，她也没有真正和要命的敌人交手过吧？
我听说充其量就是魔精、企鹅……哦，还有叛军。
Paravyan: But she's never really fought the Manus directly, has she? Only Critters and penguins in Antarctica. Oh, and then the rebels.

洛佩拉：叛军？
Lopera: The rebels?

叶尔莫莱：咳咳！
Yermolai: Ahem!

叶尔莫莱：有一件事我还没和你提过，“警官”，我在给她做战斗训练的时候发现她——
Yermolai: There's one thing you need to know, Beagle. Same thing I learned during her combat training—

“吱吱——！”
*screech*

帕拉维扬&洛佩拉：唔啊！
Paravyan & Lopera: Whoa!

叶尔莫莱踩下一脚急刹车。
The brakes squeal as Yermolai slams the armored car to a halt.

洛佩拉：又怎么了？！
Lopera: What now?

叶尔莫莱：……队长打信号停车了，我下车看看。
Yermolai: Captain signaled us to stop. Let me go check.

盘山路
Mountain Road

第一梯队指挥官的车辆停在前方不远处，奥斯本队长已经下车，正在用通讯器向后方车队下达指令。
First Squad captain's car is stopped a short distance ahead. Captain Osborn stands nearby, issuing commands over his communicator.

奥斯本队长：……各分队停车，呈Y型队列警戒。我们遇到了一些状况。
Captain Osborn: All units, pull over. Formation Y, stay alert. We have a situation.

奥斯本队长：神秘术支援小组，准备检查周遭环境。
Captain Osborn: Arcane Support, move up ahead.

奥斯本队长：保持警戒，所有人都确认一遍是否正确装备了便携式平衡伞，检查装置是否运作正常。
Captain Osborn: All team, check your wearable Equilibrium Umbrellas. Make sure they're functional.

他挂断通讯，视线落在叶尔莫莱身上。
一枚霍夫曼结在他腰间的便携式平衡伞中闪烁着微弱的光芒。
He hangs up and turns his attention to Yermolai. The Equilibrium Umbrella at his hip, fastened with a Hofmann Knot, is emitting a faint glow.

奥斯本队长：你发现了吗——
Captain Osborn: Did you notice?

叶尔莫莱：是，我也感觉到了。
Yermolai: Yeah, and I double-checked too.

男人的目光扫过路旁坡道的轮廓。
He gazes back over the rising hills surrounding them with intent.

叶尔莫莱：就算我不经常走基金会辖区里的公路，但我敢打包票，这绝对是我们三分钟之前刚刚拐过的弯。
Yermolai: I'm not claiming to be an expert on these roads, but I can tell you one thing: We turned that same goddamn corner three minutes ago.

叶尔莫莱：该死，连那颗长得像只死耗子的石头也还在这儿。
Yermolai: Right down to that daft rat-looking boulder.

叶尔莫莱：我们闯进迷障网里了？
Yermolai: Oh man! Think we could be trapped in a Maze Net?

奥斯本队长：不像，迷障网困不住我们这么大规模的车队。
Captain Osborn: Unlikely. A Maze Net couldn't cover a convoy so large.

奥斯本队长：……起码过去的迷障网做不到。
Captain Osborn: Well, at least of the ones we've encountered before.

叶尔莫莱：我可不想被困死在这种地方。
Yermolai: *sigh* Hate wasting time with these arcane props.

奥斯本队长：我觉得这是个好消息。
Captain Osborn: Take it as a good sign.

叶尔莫莱：你说什么？
Yermolai: Sorry?

奥斯本队长：重塑之手想要用诡计将我们困住，证明他们只想拖延时间。
Captain Osborn: If Manus Vindictae had the firepower, they wouldn't be trying to keep us distracted, which means we're a threat. They're trying to buy time.

奥斯本队长：只要等我们的神秘术专家破解了这个幻术还是别的什么类型的神秘术……
Captain Osborn: Once Arcane Support decodes this arcane skill, or whatever it is ...

叶尔莫莱：不，不对……
Yermolai: No, wait.

“猎犬”仰起头，观察着四周——但与其说是在张望，不如说是在嗅探空气中的危险。
He raises his head to scan their surroundings again. It seems as if he's trying to sniff the danger out from thin air.

叶尔莫莱：这不是重塑之手现在的风格，经历过真正的战争后，这些神秘学家比以往更危险了……
Yermolai: That can't be right. Manus Vindictae's grown way more aggressive since the war in Europe. They wouldn't back down from a fight.

叶尔莫莱：如果重塑之手不想让我们支援学校，大可以在离学校更近的地方布置术阵，那样也离他们的阵地更近。
Yermolai: If their only objective was to buy time, they would've set up closer to the school, somewhere they could reinforce quickly and keep us pinned down.

叶尔莫莱：但他们选择将术阵布置在半路上，而这个地方看起来是一个……***[基金会粗口]完美的伏击点！
Yermolai: So, why choose this spot? Looking around, this seems like a perfect place for ... a f**king ambush!

叶尔莫莱：快让所有人上车！
Yermolai: Everyone, get back to your vehicles! Now!

奥斯本队长：——咳！
Captain Osborn: *choke*

叶尔莫莱：奥斯本——该死的！
Yermolai: Captain! Damn it!

叶尔莫莱低身躲过一道朝他飞来的术法，扑向自己的载具。
He dives under an incoming incantation, using the momentum to roll toward the safety of his vehicle.

帕拉维扬：——叶尔莫莱，上车！
Paravyan: Yermolai, get in.

“猎犬”飞身钻入车内。
Yermolai wastes no time getting inside.

叶尔莫莱：敌人在坡道上，后撤！
Yermolai: Enemies on the slope. Fall back.

委员会大楼·广场
Square, Committee Building

基金会总部的收容程序仍在一丝不苟地执行着，但显然这份工作已经快要触及组织力所能承载的上限。
Despite the ongoing threats, the Foundation continues trudging through its Pre-Storm procedures as planned, but it has become obvious that their capacity is being stretched to its limits.

不少刚刚完成登记和身份核验的人逗留在委员会大楼前的广场中。两位特别行动小队的成员与茫然的人群擦身而过。
People are loitering outside the Committee Building, waiting for further direction. Two squadmates squeeze through the anxious crowd of refugees.

J：我觉得这不对，“剃刀”。
J: I'm gonna disagree with you there, "El Capitan."

J停下脚步，显然他对刚刚完成的巡逻任务颇为不满。
J comes to an abrupt stop as they end their patrol. His voice drips with contempt, though it isn't directed at his superior.

走在前面的“剃刀”察觉到大事不妙，但也无可奈何。新入伙的队员总是会有些牢骚，而作为队长的他有必要倾听。
"Razor" doesn't need to guess what's coming—another complaint from his newest recruit. As a captain, he has weathered them a thousand times and obliges with a sympathetic ear.

J：那些被拦在外面的人让我想起救济院门前等待施舍的人，可他们不会得到面包和庇护，而是被基金会踢出门外。
J: All those people out there ... makes me think of the folks that line up outside the soup kitchens, only there ain't any soup. No shelter either. Just a shove to send them on their way.

“剃刀”：基金会对收容对象有自己的判断标准……
"Razor": The Foundation can only admit so many people.

J：“判断标准”是他们是否有用，人的价值要在“暴雨”下被划分出三六九等。
J: So, what, they get to pick and choose who lives and who dies? We throw equality out of the window just because of the "Storm"?

J：我尝试去理解我妹妹的选择，但是……面对这样的情况，她会说什么？
J: I've tried to understand why my sister chose to join the Foundation, but what would she say if she saw this?

J：我很难改变对基金会最初的看法——这栋大楼里坐着的都是伪装成慈善家的冷血蜥蜴。
J: I can't shake the thought outta my head: those cold-hearted bastards sitting back in their glossy offices with comfy chairs, pretending to be philanthropists while they leave people to die.

“剃刀”：不要在总部里大声说这种话，老兄，就算是帮我个忙。你明白的，现在基金会对这些事很敏感。
"Razor": Do me a favor, J. Do your complaining in private. I don't want to have to be the one pulling rank and charging you with insubordination.

J：是，我知道。我们不能帮助世界上的所有人。我只是希望能说服自己：我所做的都是为了帮助更多无法自保的人。
J: Whatever. I know we can't help everyone, but you ain't convincing me that this is for the "greater good."

“剃刀”：我们……咳，我们面对的困境比当年在海特街的要大得多，也要复杂得多，你应该记得……
"Razor": We ... What we're dealing with here is more complex than things back on Haight Street. I'm sure you remember.

通讯器的响声将队长从这场他不擅长的尴尬谈话中拯救出来。
The ring of his communicator rescues "Razor" from an imminent clash of organizational discipline and street philosophy.

“剃刀”：……
"Razor": ...

“剃刀”：J，快到换岗时间了。我建议你去休息室坐一会儿，和陶德聊聊，喝点儿热茶。
"Razor": Your shift's almost over, J. Why don't you go grab something in the lounge? Maybe have a chat with Todd.

J：听起来你不打算和我一起？
J: You not coming with?

“剃刀”：我刚刚接到了新的指令。
"Razor": I've got new orders.

16  临时制品 / Temporary Product

便携式平衡伞使用说明
Wearable Equilibrium Umbrella User Manual

便携式平衡伞为平衡伞去掉伞面后的便携装置，仅保留了核心的霍夫曼结结构、转换器及调和器，配备腰戴式尼龙扣锁，轻便牢固、不易甩脱，便于武装人员在“暴雨”中作战时使用，减轻持伞负担，可大大提升战斗时的机动能力。

使用时请严格注意以下几点事项：
1.为保证霍夫曼结的稳定效果，本装置依然采用玻璃外壳，以保证良好的神秘术传导性能。本装置外壳虽已进行特殊加固，但仍无法应对强力冲击，请务必避免其遭受巨力击打或尖锐磕碰。
2.因研发时间仓促，平衡伞去掉伞面后的避雨效果尚未得到验证，本装置亦未进行大范围推广。使用时请务必保证每人每份随身佩戴，避免双人、多人使用同一装置，导致避雨范围不足酿成严重后果。
3.建议申请便携式平衡伞的武装单位主动申请备用数量的同时，申请一定数量的完整平衡伞，以便应对突发情况。
The Wearable Equilibrium Umbrella is a compact version of the standard Equilibrium Umbrella with the canopy removed. It retains the core Hofmann Knot structure, converter, and harmonizer, and is equipped with a waist-mounted nylon buckle—lightweight, durable, and secure against accidental loss. Designed for armed personnel operating in the "Storm," it reduces the burden of carrying a full umbrella while significantly improving combat mobility.

Please note the following precautions during use:
1. To ensure the stability of the Hofmann Knot, the device retains a glass casing to maintain optimal arcane conductivity. Although the casing has been specially reinforced, it cannot withstand heavy impacts. Avoid striking it with force or exposing it to sharp edges.
2. As development time was limited, the rain-shielding effectiveness of this canopy-free design remains unverified, and distribution has been limited accordingly. Each person must carry and wear their own unit at all times. Sharing a single device between two or more individuals risks insufficient coverage and may result in serious consequences.
3. Armed units are advised to request backup Wearable Equilibrium Umbrellas and a number of standard Equilibrium Umbrellas when submitting their application, in case of emergencies.

17  程序正义 / Rules Are Rules

程序正义
Rules Are Rules

基金会办公室
Foundation Office

行动队长Ⅰ：——车队失联了？
Operation Team Leader I: No comms from the First Squad?

“剃刀”进入办公室时恰好听到了这一句坏消息，他面具下的眉头皱起，看向室内。
"Razor" catches the bad news as he enters their makeshift command post. His brow furrows beneath his mask as he surveys the room.

不少与他相识、留守总部的行动队长都在房间里，召集他们的无名者则站在办公桌前。
Several familiar squad captains are present, as well as the one who summoned them—Ms. Stranger—now standing before a desk.

“剃刀”：啊，你们都在。
"Razor": Oh, everyone's here.

行动队长Ⅱ：老兄，来这边坐，我们才刚开始没多久。
Operation Team Leader II: Over here, "Razor." We've just started.

“剃刀”：别在乎我，继续吧——我想你说的车队是支援第一防线学校的队伍。
"Razor": Don't mind me. You were talking about the First Squad? They were the ones sent to the SPDM, right?

无名者：调度中心联系不上他们，暂时无法确认是车队真的遭遇了危险，还是只是被这一次“暴雨”影响了通讯。
Ms. Stranger: Dispatch has lost contact with them. We're not sure whether they're in danger or if communication has been jammed by the "Storm."

行动队长Ⅱ：是否要另派一支队伍去看看情况？
Operation Team Leader II: Will we send another unit?

无名者：不，调度中心认为不能在“暴雨”前再向外部输送武装力量。
Ms. Stranger: No. Dispatch has decided we can't risk sending anyone else with the "Storm" arriving so soon.

添油战术被否决。在座的行动队长足够资深，他们不会抗拒上级的决策。
Though the piecemeal tactic is ultimately rejected, the captains in attendance know better than to openly defy their superiors.

“剃刀”：我可以服从命令，可我仍然想了解调度中心做出这个判断的理由。
"Razor": Is there any clearer reason why they made that decision?

无名者：继续抽调人手外出只会削弱总部对局势的掌控力，频繁的人员调动也容易在这个时间点诱发恐慌。
Ms. Stranger: We can't risk leaving headquarters short-handed if an emergency breaks out. Besides, mobilizing another unit could easily spark panic among the people being processed.

无名者：调度中心认为等到“暴雨”结束之后再去探查情况更为稳妥。
Ms. Stranger: Dispatch has determined that if we cannot make contact, it'll be better to deploy after the "Storm" has passed.

行动队长Ⅱ：所以，我们聚在这里是为了什么，无名者小姐？
Operation Team Leader II: Then what are we here for, Ms. Stranger?

无名者：你们的行动小队会在“暴雨”结束之后立刻着手调查车队和第一防线学校的情况，各位需要提前做好准备。
Ms. Stranger: Your teams will be performing reconnaissance in force as soon as the "Storm" ends. You are to gather any intel about the status of the First Squad and SPDM as soon as possible. I need you to prepare your teams in advance.

无名者：但是，在你们的行动开始之前，车队失联一事不允许告知比你们职级更低的行动队员。
Ms. Stranger: But let me be clear. The purpose of this mission and the status of the SPDM and First Squad are on a need-to-know basis. You are not permitted to inform the lower ranks at this time.

无名者：调度中心担心你们小队中的一些新成员会有冲动的举动，尤其是一些……神秘学家。
Ms. Stranger: We have to take into account the possibility that some of your newer team members may act on impulse if they hear the news, especially the arcanists among them.

“剃刀”：在这个艰难的时局，人类和神秘学家需要学会并肩作战。
"Razor": That's fair. I'm not sure I could hold some of them back from rushing into the "Storm" if they thought they could help.

“剃刀”：我队伍里的新人如果知道这件事，确实会很麻烦。
"Razor": For the good of the operation, we'll keep our mouths shut. Human or arcanist, we need to prioritize our teams' well-being.

行动队长Ⅰ：奥斯本带的队伍不小，还配备了大量平衡伞，重塑之手要对付他们没那么容易。
Operation Team Leader I: Osborn's team will be fine. They have numbers, skills, and the latest gear. The Manus won't take them out—not without a fight.

行动队长Ⅰ：也许等到“暴雨”结束之后，我们会发现他们已经成了第一防线学校的英雄。
Operation Team Leader I: I bet they'll be crowd surfing above the cheering students by the time we get to the SPDM.

行动队长Ⅱ：对了，无名者小姐，你之前要我“照看”司辰小队成员状况。我勉强能说他们在自己的岗位上干得不错。
Operation Team Leader II: Ms. Stranger, I've kept an eye on Team Timekeeper as ordered. I wouldn't say they're the most disciplined group, but they're doing fine.

行动队长Ⅱ：……不过这要排除一个总以为自己在公海上的海盗，她已经在筹划带着其他人逃离基金会了。
Operation Team Leader II: Except the one that claims to be some kind of pirate-rockstar-come-freedom fighter. She's been selling the others on an escape plan.

无名者：……
Ms. Stranger: ...

无名者：“剃刀”，拜托你去按住星锑。你之前在爱琴海上为她提供过紧急救援，我想她对你也许不会那么抗拒。
Ms. Stranger: "Razor," could I ask you to speak to Regulus? You saved her life once on the Aegean. Perhaps she'll listen to you.

无名者：另外，这是给各个小队的任务指示。
Ms. Stranger: Here are your mission briefings: Keep them secret; keep them safe.

无名者将指令文件分发给各个队长。
She hands each of the assembled captains a file.

无名者：希望各位队长能按照调度中心的指示行动，现在你们可以去处理雨前最后的收尾工作了。
Ms. Stranger: Please follow Dispatch's instructions and report in before you make further decisions. You have your orders. Dismissed.

行动队长们领命离去，无名者也准备跟随众人走出房间。
The other captains take their leave, and Ms. Stranger prepares to follow.

“剃刀”：你要去哪里，无名者小姐？
"Razor": Wait, Ms. Stranger.

基金会大门登记处
Registration Desk, St. Pavlov Foundation

士兵Ⅰ：难道我要把这些东西都丢到外面去吗？
Soldier I: What? I have to leave my things behind?

一位身穿破旧军装的士兵挤到登记台前，他的身旁拖着一只硕大的包裹。
A soldier in a tattered uniform stands before the registration counter, a large bag set down beside him.

登记员：——那还能怎么办？
Registrar: Those are the rules, sir.

登记员：先生，我想军队系统已经用非常明确的方式通知过你们，不允许携带超过20公斤的个人物品，除非它们是军用装备。
Registrar: I believe you've been informed that each individual is allowed only a maximum of 20 kilograms. Any excess baggage must be discarded unless it qualifies as military equipment.

登记员：但很显然你包裹里的东西全都是毫无疑问的“个人物品”。
我不想和你争辩了，你知道你身后还有多少人吗？
Registrar: Since there is no military equipment in your baggage and ... You know what? I don't have time for this. Don't you see the line behind you?

士兵Ⅰ：你们这些坐办公室的，什么都不懂的是你们！
Soldier I: Do you have any idea what I've been through to get here?

士兵愤而指向前台桌面上的基金会标志。
He points toward the Foundation symbol adorning the counter with a grim stare.

士兵Ⅰ：过去三个月，我都在前线为这个标志和重塑之手拼命。
现在你们对我就是这个态度？！
Soldier I: I spent three months in the trenches fighting Manus Vindictae for this organization. Now you're asking me to throw away what's left of my life?

士兵Ⅰ：这里面是我要带给我弟弟的东西，连长说我们在这里签完那什么“合同”之后暂住一下就会离开，为什么非要我现在把它们扔了？
Soldier I: And it's not just my belongings. I brought things for my little brother, too. The commander said we'd be moving again after we sign this contract. I don't see why I have to throw it all away.

眼看一场新的冲突即将爆发，负责大门的基金会安保逐渐围了上来。
Foundation security make their way over to the counter, flanking the man on both sides.

基金会安保：请你保持冷静，先生，把超过规定限额之外的物品交出来。
Foundation Security: Calm down, sir. You'll have to discard any excess baggage before we can allow you entry. No exceptions.

基金会安保：你身后还有很多人要进入基金会签订“军事服务合约”，他们的时间也很宝贵。
Foundation Security: We need to get you all processed quickly so you can sign your military service contracts. There's a lot of paperwork to do.

士兵Ⅰ：……
Soldier I: ...

与他一同前来的另一名士兵走上前。
Another soldier who had arrived with him approaches.

士兵Ⅱ：听他的吧，亚瑟。
你知道的，基金会的这些人和我们镇上的办事员没有什么区别。
Soldier II: Just do as he says, Arthur. You know these bureaucrat types. They're no different here than any back home.

士兵Ⅱ：他们躲在后方，什么都不懂，只认准面前那些爬满格子和小字的纸。
你非要和这些人较劲就没意思了。
Soldier II: Heads are too clogged up with rules and regulations. There's no talking any sense into them. Might as well save yourself the bother.

在战友的劝说下，士兵才终于决定扔下自己的随身物品。
At his comrade's behest, the soldier finally parts with his belongings.

士兵Ⅰ：……哼。
Soldier I: Hmph.

大厅另一端，一名女士见证了这一幕。
From the other side of the hall, a tall and lithe woman has been watching the scene unfold.

槲寄生：……
Druvis III: ...

另一人靠近她身旁。
Someone else joins her.

无名者：你在这里做什么，槲寄生小姐？
Ms. Stranger: What are you doing here, Ms. Druvis?

槲寄生：我只是……想来看看。
Druvis III: Just looking around.

槲寄生：通常会在这个时候匆匆赶回来的只有少数外派人员，这是我第一次看见这么多军人进入总部。
Druvis III: I guess I find it odd seeing all these long lines. It's not normal to have so many people waiting to come in, especially so close to the "Storm."

槲寄生：他们换上基金会的制服，带上用来作战的武器，即将与自己的国家、时代还有亲人诀别。
Druvis III: Soon, they'll be putting on Foundation uniforms. And then, just like that, they'll be told to say goodbye to their family, friends, homelands—their entire era.

无名者：我们不应该在这里谈论这个话题，在这里做登记的人大多不知道时代的“问题”。
Ms. Stranger: Please, Ms. Druvis, it's best we don't talk about that here. Most of those waiting in line aren't aware of this "issue."

槲寄生：过去几个月里，基金会的士兵一直在与重塑之手战斗，他们真的不会从敌人口中得知“暴雨”的存在吗？
Druvis III: They've been fighting Manus Vindictae for months. Don't you think they might have learned something from our enemies?

无名者：不让本时代的人获知“暴雨”的存在是基金会的规定，这个规定与他们是否真的知情无关。
Ms. Stranger: The Foundation's policy forbids revealing information on the "Storm" to any personnel from the current era. It doesn't matter what they may or may not already know.

槲寄生：……
Druvis III: ...

无名者：怎么了？
Ms. Stranger: What is it?

槲寄生：你的语气……在讽刺吗？你在指责基金会政策的荒谬？
Druvis III: Was that sarcasm in your voice? You find the Foundation's policy absurd too, don't you?

无名者：我不会这么说。
Ms. Stranger: I wouldn't say that.

槲寄生：……维尔汀和我说过你的事，我知道你曾经强迫自己成为其他人，为重塑之手做过自己难以接受的事。
Druvis III: Vertin told me about you. You went under deep cover in Manus Vindictae, forced yourself to become someone you're not, did things you've come to regret ...

槲寄生：你为基金会付出了我无法想象的代价，我为你的过去感到惋惜。
Druvis III: You paid an unimaginable price to complete the Foundation's mission.

槲寄生：现在你回到了基金会，但你仍然说，你并不喜欢自己扮演的角色。
Druvis III: But even now, after you've returned home, you're dissatisfied with the character you're playing.

槲寄生：那么，你想成为的是什么人？
Druvis III: I can't help but wonder what the "you" you really want to be is like.

无名者：……
Ms. Stranger: ...

无名者：我不知道我想扮演谁，或者说我还没来得及去思考这件事。
Ms. Stranger: I don't know. I've never had the time to answer that question.

无名者：基金会给我的只有新的工作，没有新的角色。
Ms. Stranger: The Foundation only gave me a new role, not a new identity. They expected me to fall back into my old self.

槲寄生：……你是来确认我和司辰小队的其他人是否会做出出格举动的吗？
Druvis III: Are you here to check in on us? To make sure we aren't planning anything too radical?

槲寄生：这次不会再发生过去那样的事。
Druvis III: You can rest assured. It won't happen.

无名者：了解。我不会再担心这件事了，感谢你信任基金会的决策。
Ms. Stranger: I appreciate it. Then, let's put it behind us. Thank you for trusting the Foundation.

槲寄生：我不信任基金会，这一点至今也没有改变。我很难劝说自己相信基金会能带来真正的秩序与和平。
Druvis III: But I don't trust the Foundation. I've always doubted their ability to bring about their professed order and peace for all.

无名者：我不建议你对我说这些话，槲寄生女士。
Ms. Stranger: It might be best to end our conversation here, Ms. Druvis.

韦耶豪瑟女士没有接纳这份建言。
Druvis ignores the suggestion.

槲寄生：让一无所知的人进入基金会，之后依靠一场“暴雨”将他们和过去的一切切断，从此只能为基金会所用。
Druvis III: They bring these people in under false pretenses, knowing full well that after the "Storm" wipes out their era, they'll be isolated and moldable. And just like that, the Foundation gets a new generation of puppets.

槲寄生：如同植物被斩断根须，让树木成为“木材”……
Druvis III: Uprooted, shorn down, and processed, like trees turned into logs. No one ever asks them what they want to be. They never get that chance.

槲寄生：我留在基金会，是因为维尔汀邀请我，而我选择加入她的事业。
Druvis III: I work with the Foundation only because of Vertin. I am loyal to her, devoted to her cause. Not yours.

槲寄生：我不会因自己的选择而犹疑，但有多少人能理解自己在不知情的情况下做出的牺牲？
Druvis III: I can face the uncertainty of the future because I know what I'm fighting for. How would these people react if they found out the truth?

槲寄生：那些不愿意被胁迫的人，会不会渴望在世界上的其他地方另寻庇护之处？
Druvis III: Don't you think some of them would rather risk their lives to find another shelter than give up their freedom to the Foundation?

无名者：你想说人们会投向重塑之手……
Ms. Stranger: You mean they might want to join Manus Vindictae.

槲寄生：还有狮鹫军，或是我们不一定知晓其存在的庇护所，如同曾经阿派朗学派的小岛。
Druvis III: Or the Gryphon Legion, or some other shelter we don't know of, like the Apeiron island.

槲寄生：当庇护不再是一项独一无二的特权，基金会一贯的做法还能维系多久？
Druvis III: How long do you think the Foundation's policies would hold if they didn't present themselves as the only option?

槲寄生：——！
Druvis III: ...!

无名者：……全局警报？
Ms. Stranger: That's a stage one alert.

高亢的警报声从四面八方响起。
A high-pitched alarm drones over their voices, blaring out from all directions.

这一阵音调与节奏的组合太过罕见，以至于大多数人都无法第一时间做出反应。
The tone and rhythm are too rare a combination for most to immediately realize their meaning.

登记员：这——又出什么问题了？
Registrar: What? What's going on now?

士兵Ⅰ：炮击？还是防空警报？
Soldier I: An assault? Or a bombardment?

调查员Ⅱ：所有人，保持秩序，等待广播做进一步解释。
Male Investigator: Everyone, stay calm and wait for further instructions.

槲寄生：是“暴雨”引发的异常？还是除了第一防线学校，还有其他地方遭到袭击？
Druvis III: Could it be something related to the Storm Syndrome? Or another attack outside?

无名者：不，这是……
Ms. Stranger: Neither. A stage one alert means ...

情报人员面色苍白地转向身后委员会大楼的方向。
The color drains from her face as she turns toward the Committee Building.

无名者：这是基金会总部内发生严重危机的警报。
Ms. Stranger: A major crisis is happening inside headquarters.

无名者：槲寄生小姐，请你立刻与司辰小队汇合，根据应急广播的指示寻找安全地点。
Ms. Stranger: Ms. Druvis, please return to Team Timekeeper and lead them to the nearest shelter.

她飞快地离开了。
Ms. Stranger offers a wordless goodbye and rushes off.

槲寄生：……
Druvis III: ...

所有人员注意，基金会正遭遇敌对力量攻击，应急避险程序已启动。
Warning: Stage one alert. Unidentified intruders in headquarters. Emergency evacuation procedures initiated.

广播：请所有人遵循现场最高职级人员的指示，就近前往避险设施，并等待下一步指示。
Broadcast: All personnel are to obey ranking officers during evacuation and await further instructions in the emergency shelters.

广播：本次应急避险程序不是演习。重复一次——基金会正遭遇敌对力量攻击……
Broadcast: This is not a drill. Repeat: Stage one alert. Unidentified intruders in headquarters.

阿尔卡纳：和我料想的一样……
Arcana: Well, well. It is of little surprise—

阿尔卡纳：这座不苟言笑的白色堡垒，不欢迎不请自来的客人。
Arcana: That the white fortress doth not extend its courtesy to an unbidden guest.

阿尔卡纳：可我不喜欢被拒之门外的感觉。
Arcana: Yet I shall brook no refusal.

基金会南翼花园
Southern Garden, St. Pavlov Foundation

火渡船：力气真大。
Ustrina: A most impressive display of strength.

绀紫腕：看起来他们被吓坏了。
İlmek: Look at them run. Perhaps I overdid things.

绀紫腕：唉，我原本不想采用这种方式的。
İlmek: *sigh* I wish I didn't have to be so rough with them.

阿尔卡纳：……
Arcana: ...

火渡船：……引导之人。
Ustrina: Guiding One.

在场的追随者们见证他们的神明缓缓走入委员会大楼。
The followers watch as their deity approaches the Committee Building in slow, deft steps.

平：去第一防线学校的基金会车队怎么样了？
Taira: How has our ambush against the Foundation's Response Squad proceeded?

格鲁托夫：他们不会回援了。
Krutov: They've been taken care of.

平：很好，那我们不用浪费时间了。
Taira: Hm. Impressive. Then I need only point my blade forward.

格鲁托夫：呵……但愿你的刀真的能起到作用。
Krutov: Heh, bringing a knife to a gunfight, you are a hoot.

格鲁托夫将视线从纹路扭曲的蟹壳上移走，别过头去面对自己的军队。
Krutov turns his gaze from the shell covered in warped patterns and toward his troops.

格鲁托夫：各突击小组，快速进入委员会大楼，封锁所有出口，消灭任何有抵抗意图的敌人。
Krutov: Assault squads, lock down the first floor of the Committee Building. Eliminate any resistance.

格鲁托夫：我们要在今天摧毁这座装满虚伪和傲慢的高塔！
Krutov: We'll demolish their tower and rip the guts from these arrogant hypocrites!

重塑士兵Ⅰ：——是！
Manus Soldier I: Yes, sir!

基金会走廊
Hallway, St. Pavlov Foundation

基金会职员Ⅰ：快、快跑……
Foundation Staff Member I: Run!

基金会职员Ⅱ：重塑之手——我看到他们了！
Foundation Staff Member II: It's Manus Vindictae! I saw them!

无名者：……
Ms. Stranger: ...

白色的影子从她身边擦过，最初的枪弹与术法交锋的声音已经响彻拥挤的走廊。
A white figure brushes past her as the sounds of gunshots and incantations reverberate through the crowded corridor.

耳旁的脚步声越来越稀疏。该走了。她不该继续逆着人潮向前了。
The footsteps grow sparser. It's time to go. She shouldn't push against the flow of the crowd anymore.

？？？：是你……
???: You ...

阿尔卡纳：无名者循声抬起头。
Arcana: Ms. Stranger raises her head.

阿尔卡纳：……格蕾丝，被人类牢笼囚禁的飞蛾。
Arcana: Grace, a moth sealed inside a human's cage.

无名者：阿尔卡纳，不，这怎么可能……
Ms. Stranger: Arcana? No, this can't be happening.

阿尔卡纳：不必紧张，我来拜访基金会只是想带走一个答案。
Arcana: You need not fear. I come only seeking an answer. We have no dark designs.

阿尔卡纳：我很喜欢你的名字，也记得你的奉献，可惜你没有选择留在我身边。
Arcana: To speak thy name doth please me still, and I do not forget the deeds thou once didst for us, grieved though I am by thy betrayal.

阿尔卡纳：靠近些，我要送你一份迟来的送别礼。
Arcana: Come hither, child. For I carry a gift that should have long ago been bestowed upon thee.

无名者：——！
Ms. Stranger: ...!

Don't be so shy to show it off. If that boneheaded lunatic doesn't bother to hide her face, you shouldn't either.

But I wasn't rewarded with immortality for all my pains.

It seems unfair that a privilege like that should be given to such a troublemaker.

This whole damned world is coming to an end!

19  应急策略 · 1/2 / Emergency Measures · 1/2

应急策略
Emergency Measures

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

审理委员会
Courtroom

广播：所有人员注意，基金会正遭遇敌对力量攻击，应急避险程序已启动。
Broadcast: Warning: Stage one alert. Unidentified intruders in headquarters. Emergency evacuation procedures initiated.

广播：请所有人遵循现场最高职级人员的指示，就近前往避险设施，并等待下一步指示。
Broadcast: All personnel are to obey ranking officers during evacuation and await further instructions in the emergency shelters.

广播：本次应急避险程序不是演习。重复一次——基金会正遭遇敌对力量攻击……
Broadcast: This is not a drill. Repeat: Stage one alert. Unidentified intruders in headquarters.

特别检察官：请各位保持镇静，审理程序仍未结束。
Special Tribunal Officer: Quiet in the court. The trial will not be interrupted.

特别检察官：突发情况会由基金会的防卫力量应对，我们所处的楼层正受到保护。
Special Tribunal Officer: The Foundation's security forces are more than capable of handling whatever emergency is occurring outside. This tribunal is perfectly safe.

伊里内伊：检察官，陪审席的各位都在基金会身居要职，集中在此处恐怕不是一个好做法。
Irinei: Your Honor, might I suggest a recess until the end of this crisis? There are a number of critical personnel gathered here.

伊里内伊：普通职员虽然接受过演习训练，但面临真正的危机时仍然容易陷入混乱。他们需要在现场看见他们的领导人。
Irinei: I have no doubt our security forces are capable, but without senior leadership to direct them, chaos is inevitable.

佩德拉：我们应该立即指挥所有人撤离到安全地点并等待救援。你拥有现场的最高职权，检察官。我们在等待你的指示。
Pedra: We must evacuate to the nearest emergency shelter immediately and wait for rescue. As the ranking officer, Your Honor, you are in command of the evacuation.

特别检察官：……合理的判断。
Special Tribunal Officer: You're right.

特别检察官：我宣布审理程序中止，在此期间，受审人刻雷乌斯将被限制人身自由，陪审员可以回到岗位组织避险——
Special Tribunal Officer: In that case, I declare a recess until the end of this present crisis. During this period, the defendant shall remain in custody. Other personnel will return to their positions and—

刻雷乌斯：我们在放弃阵地。
Creius: You're going to get people killed!

特别检察官：刻雷乌斯，审理程序中止后，你的发言仍然会作为呈堂证供，注意你的言辞。
Special Tribunal Officer: Mr. Creius, I would remind you that any statements you make will be on the record when the court returns to session.

刻雷乌斯：我正是在理解这一点的前提下这么说的，尊敬的检察官。没有时间再走“避险程序”了。
Creius: I know, but I'll say it anyway. Because I know the truth is on my side, Your Honor.

刻雷乌斯：重塑之手发动的绝对不会是一次小规模的袭击。他们甚至冒雨而来。
Creius: Listen to me, everyone. Forget the procedures. If the Manus are brazen enough to attack us at our headquarters while the "Storm" is imminent, then they must have come with overwhelming force.

刻雷乌斯：基金会应该尽可能动员所有人，利用委员会大楼坚固的建筑结构和主场优势，展开巷战，争取时间，支撑到雨停后外围部队回援。
Creius: We'll need all personnel and every asset to resist this assault. If we can concentrate our defenses here, the long and narrow corridors of the Committee Building can work to our advantage.

刻雷乌斯：这样我们中的一部分人才能活下来。
Creius: This will be a fight for our lives.

特别检察官：你认为重塑之手的袭击力量远超基金会避险程序能应对的限度？
Special Tribunal Officer: Mr. Creius, what evidence do you have that this "assault" is beyond what is already accounted for in our emergency procedures?

伊里内伊：你是夜巡特遣出身，不应该欠缺神秘学常识。传送术阵不可能一次性输送足以摧毁基金会总部防御的军队。
Irinei: Indeed. You're a former Vigil, Creius; you should be well aware that there is simply no teleportation array with the capacity to move such a force into our security perimeter undetected.

刻雷乌斯：我们至今没有调查清楚299高地的问题——重塑之手如何在不被察觉的前提下几乎挖空了一座小山，神不知鬼不觉地向其中输送大军。
Creius: Have you already forgotten our investigation into Hill 299? They hollowed it out and transferred ten thousand soldiers inside without being noticed.

刻雷乌斯：顿河地区的战报也说明，重塑之手的确掌握某种隐秘性极强的传送链，起码足以让一整支军队保持机动。
Creius: We cannot rule out that Manus Vindictae now possesses some form of stealthy, high-capacity teleport array. We all but confirmed it following their movement patterns during the Don Operation.

刻雷乌斯：当下发生的危机也许已经超出了所有的应急预案。我们应该抛下常规和成见，让所有人加入作战。
Creius: There's a decent chance this assault is stronger than our protocols can handle. We have to mobilize everyone—anyone who can stand needs to be a part of this fight.

佩德拉：你是要让我们的文职人员也拿起枪吗？他们没有受过军事训练，这不也是让他们白白丢掉性命？
Pedra: Including our civilian staff? They don't know the first thing about combat! This plan of yours could only result in needless death.

刻雷乌斯：他们可以制造路障，转移和救治伤员，保证联络……做什么都行。
Creius: I'm not asking for them to pick up a rifle. They'll set up barricades, run messages, care for the wounded—anything they can do to help.

刻雷乌斯：还在基金会签订“军事服务合约”的军人也可以投入战斗。他们是老练的士兵，但愿他们已经自发完成了组织。
Creius: We have a hall full of soldiers, veterans of this era's battle against Manus Vindictae, all prepared to sign our military service contract. They could be the key to organizing a counterattack, if they aren't already in the fight.

特别检察官：刻雷乌斯，本庭中的所有人，包括我，此刻都没有权力推行你的策略。我们只是避难的一员。
Special Tribunal Officer: Even if we agreed to execute your plan, no one present has the authority to command it.

广播中传来一阵噪音，随后从中传出了平静的女声——这声音与预先录制的警报截然不同。
There's static over the loudspeaker, then a calm female voice, so calm it seems as if it must be a pre-recorded warning.

特别广播：基金会会长办公室通告：应急避险程序解除——基金会即刻进入战时状态。
Special Broadcast: Attention: This is an official Presidential Office broadcast. Emergency evacuation procedures are canceled. All combat personnel to battle stations.

特别广播：所有遇险人员做好抵抗准备，以合乎理性的方式保护自己的生存权利。
Special Broadcast: Non-combatants in distress, prepare to resist within safe limits.

特别广播：雨前调度中心将临时接管各单位指挥权，无法得到指令的人员请自行组织抵抗，过程中对基金会财产造成的破坏不予追责。
Special Broadcast: The Pre-Storm Dispatch Center takes command of all units, effective immediately. Any personnel unable to reach safety or receive direct orders are permitted to resist by any means available. There will be no liability assigned for any damage to headquarters sustained during resistance efforts.

特别广播：愿和平与我们同在。
Special Broadcast: May the peace be with us.

战时状态——这在总部是前所未有的情况，每个人都行动了起来。
Battle stations—an unprecedented situation for headquarters. Across the sprawling complex, staff begin to organize.

特别检察官：愿和平与我们同在……
Special Tribunal Officer: May the peace be with us.

特别检察官第一个离场以作表率，但刻雷乌斯的脚步比她更快，抢先一步离开了审判他的“法庭”。
The Special Tribunal Officer aims to be the first to the door, but Creius is faster, eager to take the opportunity to leave.

耳边响起几乎可以刺破耳膜的细微尖啸——最后一切都在一阵浪涛声中消失殆尽。
A tiny yet shrill screech rings in Ms. Stranger's ears. In the end, however, it's drowned out by a wave of other noises.

基金会房间
Room, St. Pavlov Foundation

无名者：呃——
Ms. Stranger: Ugh!

耳孔中似乎在流血，她用手去擦拭，所幸只是汗水。
It feels as though her ears are bleeding. Fortunately, when she raises a hand to check, she finds it to be only sweat.

无名者：……我是怎么到这里来的？
Ms. Stranger: How did I get here?

她仰起头，从刚才开始的耳鸣终于稍有缓解的迹象。
She tilts her head back. It offers some relief from the tinnitus.

现在充塞感官的是几条走廊之外的嘶吼、枪响与神秘术射弹爆裂的脆响。
Now, her senses are overwhelmed by the cries coming from outside the corridor, gunshots, and incantations.

无名者：阿尔卡纳……祂没杀了我，这是……
Ms. Stranger: Arcana didn't kill me. What did she ...?

她按压太阳穴，感受着——有某种巨大而空洞的东西在她的颅骨下膨胀，将她的思想裹入无光的黑夜。
She presses her temple. She feels it—something massive yet hollow swelling inside her skull, enveloping her thoughts in the blackest of nights.

？？？：无名者小姐，你醒了。
???: Ms. Stranger! You're awake.

她感觉到有人在搀扶自己。
Someone reaches out to help her up.

小春雀儿：你感觉还好吗？有没有很疼的地方？
Eagle: Are you okay? Any pain?

无名者：小春雀儿？
Ms. Stranger: Ah, Eagle?

无名者：我没事……也许吧。我们是怎么到这里来的？
Ms. Stranger: Yes, I'm fine, I think. But how did we end up here?

小春雀儿：袭击发生之后，我和老师就走散了。重塑之手的士兵已经占领了基金会的第一层，我没来得及赶到避难的场所。
Eagle: I got separated from Ms. Marguerite in the assault. The Manus captured the first floor before I could reach the emergency shelter.

小春雀儿：我在走廊里发现了昏迷的你，就和另一个职员一同把你带到了这儿来。
Eagle: I was running along the hallway and found you passed out on the floor. Luckily, a guard saw us and helped me move you here.

小春雀儿：这里暂时是安全的，但我不确定能持续多久。

小春雀儿：他……

小春雀儿看向房间里的一具尸体，一个死去的重塑之手士兵被压在他身下。

一张略有破损的面具刚好被甩在无名者脚边。

Ms. Stranger: Somewhere—anywhere else. It's too dangerous to stay here.

无名者：他们应该只是急于进攻，没有来得及搜索每一个房间。
Ms. Stranger: If they've secured the first floor, then they've likely already begun searching it room by room for survivors.

小春雀儿：可出门之后可能很快会碰到重塑之手，我们应该对付不了——
Eagle: But there are so many of them out there. We can—

小春雀儿忽然注意到无名者手上的面具——不知道她是什么时候拿起来的。
She spots the mask in Ms. Stranger's hand.

小春雀儿：请你不要戴上它，无名者小姐。

无名者：我可以用它避开普通敌人的攻击。我要为你找一条离开这里的路。
Ms. Stranger: It'll deceive the Manus soldiers. I'll use it to find a way out for us.

无名者：基金会总部有能力抵抗重塑之手的突袭，不是所有地方都会沦陷，我们或许能找到其他更安全的地方。
Ms. Stranger: Don't worry. The Foundation's headquarters was designed as a military fortress. There are plenty of hidden shelters around. We'll find a safe place.

小春雀儿：可是，只有我们两个人的话……是不是留在这里等待救援会比较好？
Eagle: But once we're out in the open, it'll be just the two of us against all of them. The Scout Code would say that we should hold in place and wait for rescue.

无名者：基金会能调动的武力应该都投入了战斗，我不确定他们有没有余力在沦陷区展开搜救。

无名者：重塑之手随时可能开始搜查他们占领的楼层，我们一定不能留在这里。

Ms. Stranger: I can't shake the feeling that I'm to blame. I must have missed something, some detail that could have helped us foresee this attack.

无名者：我希望我能在错误中救下一个人，至少做到一些事。
Ms. Stranger: I need to make up for this mistake, even if all I can do is save one life.

无名者：……不能再浪费时间了。
Ms. Stranger: We need to go. Now.

小春雀儿：你一定要戴上它吗？
Eagle: Are you really going to put it on?

玛格丽特：X在徽章上进行过附魔处理，它能够散发出特殊的神秘术辉光频段。遗憾的是，在一场战斗中，轰轰吼叫匣变成了一堆碎片。

Marguerite: Thankfully, despite most of these checkered doormats being about as useful as a flat tire, they do carry monitoring devices capable of picking up those traces, so ...

J：等等，你的意思是，你一直在这个小孩身上安装着追踪器？
