乌尔里希：现状？目前科算中心还在坚持抵抗，冷周六研究员想到了一个阻止重塑之手继续逼近的方法。
Ulrich: Right now? We're doing all we can to hold out. Researcher Hissabeth has formulated a plan to stop the Manus, though.

哑谜：一个方法？没错，她正在准备引爆“天王星4号”火箭发动机的烈火蜥蜴燃料罐，好让我们一起和这个悲惨的世界说再见。
Enigma: That's right. Ask the lady on the line how she feels about getting blasted into the sky by an exploding "Fire Salamander" fuel tank from the Uranus-4 rocket.

指针：她坚称科算中心的安全门足以抵抗爆炸，也相信重火力甚至能让她冲到基金会捞出她的组员。我会说服她的。尽量。
Pointer: Hissabeth seems confident that Laplace's security doors are strong enough to withstand the explosion and that her firepower should provide just enough cover to allow the Foundation to rescue her team. I'll speak with her, if she's still inclined to listen.

玛格丽特：看起来拉普拉斯科算中心一切正常。
Marguerite: In summation, things are about as expected there.

研究员不知道什么时候凑了过来，在她们身后的储存柜中挑挑选选。
At some point, Marguerite makes her way over. She peruses the specimens in the storage cabinets behind them.

玛格丽特：我想那些天才怪人们应该不会有什么事，只要冷周六没有真的把总部从内部爆破的话。
Marguerite: Those lab geeks should be just fine as long as Hissabeth gives up her plan.

玛格丽特：嗯？这还真不好说……
Marguerite: Hm. Hissabeth? Give up? Listen to yourself.

最终，挑剔的研究员什么好东西也没看上，失望地离开了。
Ultimately, nothing on these shelves suits the cold-eyed woman.

无名者：……
Ms. Stranger: ...

无名者：我想我所知的重塑之手的情报现在可以直接开放给你们了。战时状态，我们不该讲究太多。
Ms. Stranger: It may be best for me to share my intel on Manus Vindictae with all of you. Rules and regulations would only hold us back at this moment.

槲寄生：这正是我的期望，很高兴能得到你的帮助。
Druvis III: You read my mind, Ms. Stranger. I think we deserve to know.

无名者：我会给你们列出一个名单——关于重塑之手的高级人员和他们的基本信息。
Ms. Stranger: Allow me to start with a list of the Manus's high-level figures and their basic information.

无名者：他们不一定全都会出现，但万一遇上了……请小心谨慎地行动。
Ms. Stranger: We can't be sure just how many of them are part of this attack, but if you run into any of them, please be extra careful.

20  “新朋旧友” / "New Faces, Old Friends"

重塑之手干部名单
Manus Vindictae Leaders

[一份字迹飘逸的手写记录，纸张上沾有微不可察的鳞粉]

平
本体为寄居于他人面部的螃蟹，神秘术力量可以控制寄居之人的动作，观察到寄居之人死亡后对其无明显影响。需时刻留意螃蟹。

歌唱家
能够用歌声操控（加强？）他人的情感，其声音有极强的机动性，且能使用和风有关的神秘术，神秘术传播范围极广，较难防范。

火渡船
神秘术媒介疑似为影子，可以使用镰刀造成精神层面的伤害，受害者均未见明显外伤及痛苦表现。注意躲避镰刀，若有精神防御类神秘术最佳。

假想敌
外表以巨大的拼接玩偶示人，神秘术为将某种情绪、信念或一段意识植入他人大脑，常常用于植入恐惧。应对思路同火渡船。

绀紫腕
未发现其使用神秘术，但观察其力量与耐力远超常人。需避免正面近身接触。

普莱玛
擅长催眠、刑讯，性格残暴。其母阿尼姆斯曾任职重塑之手使徒，已于南极事件中死亡。
……

余下部分干部未有机会进行深入接触，已将疑似干部的人员外貌特征描述于附录。
[A flowing handwritten note; faint scale powder clings to the paper.]

Taira
His true form is a crab that latches onto the faces of others. Through his arcane skills, he can control the movements of his host. The death of the host does not harm him. Always be wary of the crab.

Lyra
She can control—or amplify?—emotions through song. Her voice is highly mobile, and her wind-based arcane skills are wide-reaching and difficult to guard against.

Ustrina
The medium of her arcane skills is possibly a shadow in the shape of a sickle. She can use it to inflict mental damage, though her victims show no visible physical wounds or signs of pain. Evade the sickle, and use mental defense arcane skills if possible.

Hypothetik
He takes the form of a giant stitched puppet. His arcane skills allow him to implant emotions, beliefs, or fragments of consciousness into the minds of others—most commonly fear. Counter him the same way you would against Ustrina.

İlmek
No arcane skills have been observed, but her strength and endurance far exceed ordinary limits. Avoid engaging her up close.

Prima
Skilled in hypnosis and torture, and known for her brutal nature. Her mother, Animus, was a Manus Apostle and died during the Antarctic incident.

...

No deeper contact has been made with the remaining leaders. Physical descriptions of suspected leaders are recorded in the appendix.

21  歧路 · 1/2 / A Hard Choice · 1/2

歧路
A Hard Choice

洛佩拉：搭把手，帮我把帕拉维扬拖过来——注意别走出我的烟雾！
Lopera: Help me get Paravyan over here, and remember to keep yourself in my smoke at all times.

士兵们压低身体，将一具沉重的躯体拖到掩体后方。
They crouch low and drag Paravyan behind cover.

从坩埚中涌出的炼金术迷雾为受困的第一梯队提供了临时的掩护。
Alchemical smoke flows out from her crucible, obscuring them further, at least temporarily.

洛佩拉：噢……他这个样子看起来不太好。
Lopera: Oh no. He doesn't look good.

帕拉维扬：……
Paravyan: ...

帕拉维扬：放开我，我们完蛋了，我们……我们全都会死在这……
Paravyan: Just leave me. We're doomed. We ... We're all gonna die here.

洛佩拉：你在说什么呢，精神点！
Lopera: What are you yammering about! Toughen up, soldier!

帕拉维扬：我不想再参加什么战斗了，无论输赢都没有意义。这个世界要完蛋了……
Paravyan: I don't want to fight anymore. It's all meaningless. The world's ending anyway.

帕拉维扬：基金会，重塑之手，还是别的什么东西，都算了吧……让我躺在这儿，我起不来了……
Paravyan: The Foundation, Manus Vindictae, wh-whatever. Just leave me be. I can't get back on my feet again.

洛佩拉：……你没受很重的伤啊，怎么会这样？
Lopera: Come on, all you've got are a few bruises. What happened?

叶尔莫莱：让一让！
Yermolai: Make way!

“猎犬”挤了过来，顺便一脚把身旁的一具黑色的尸体踢下山坡。
Yermolai rushes over, kicking a black-clothed corpse down the hill along the way.

他低下身子，把垂头丧气的帕拉维扬翻了个身。
He kneels and looks Paravyan over.

洛佩拉：等等……他的伞上有个大洞！
Lopera: Wait. His Umbrella's broken.

他低头解开帕拉维扬腰间的扣具，为他换上新的平衡伞。
Head lowered, Yermolai unfastens the buckle at Paravyan's waist and swaps the damaged Umbrella for a new one.

叶尔莫莱：我就猜到是这样。
Yermolai: I knew it.

叶尔莫莱：这小子没注意到自己的伞已经坏了，和没有伞的人一样受到了时代病的影响。
Yermolai: He didn't notice the damage to his Umbrella, and now he's infected with the Storm Syndrome.

叶尔莫莱：我遇到的好几个人有类似的情况——外表看不出来什么变化，脑袋已经被失败主义和绝望情绪给灌满。
Yermolai: There's a few others like him. Look fine on the outside, but their insides are wallowing in total despair.

叶尔莫莱：这和1914年的那次“暴雨”造成的战争狂热正好相反。
Yermolai: It's like the exact opposite of the war craze from before the 1914 "Storm."

洛佩拉：你的新平衡伞哪里来的？
Lopera: Where did you get another Umbrella?

叶尔莫莱：他的原主人已经用不上了，但帕拉维扬也许还有救。
Yermolai: From someone who doesn't need it anymore. Better used on Beagle, I'd say.

洛佩拉：……
Lopera: ...

叶尔莫莱：一时半会儿也好不了，先让他躺在这儿。
Yermolai: He'll take a while to recover. Just leave him be.

洛佩拉：找到突围的办法了吗？
Lopera: Have we found a way out?

叶尔莫莱：没有，神秘术支援小组的人还在尝试寻找破解术阵的方法。
Yermolai: No. Arcane Support's still working on it.

洛佩拉：我可以——
Lopera: I can—

叶尔莫莱：每个人都有要做的事。你是神秘学家，但没受过术阵专家那样的训练就别瞎掺和，这种事我一个人类都懂。
Yermolai: Listen, soldier. We all have our own duties, you included. You're not an array expert. Doesn't take an arcanist to know that.

叶尔莫莱：奥斯本已经倒下了，按照职级序列，我现在就是临时指挥。
Yermolai: Osborn's down, so I'm the ranking officer here. Understood?

叶尔莫莱：之前布置你和帕拉维扬去做的事完成没有？
Yermolai: Have you and Beagle completed the mission Osborn gave you?

洛佩拉：已经在收尾的阶段，不过就是那个时候帕拉维扬变得很不配合……
Lopera: We almost did, until he suddenly refused to cooperate.

叶尔莫莱：还能继续吗？需要多久？
Yermolai: Can you finish it? How much longer will it take?

洛佩拉：可以，我的炼金术迷雾还能撑几分钟，再给我这么些时间就够了。
Lopera: Yes, sir. In about three more minutes, before my arcane smoke lifts.

叶尔莫莱：那好，我会带人掩护你……准备好之后给我信号。
Yermolai: Got it. We'll cover you in the meantime. Let me know when you're ready.

洛佩拉：——我准备好了！
Lopera: All set!

叶尔莫莱：全体撤退！
Yermolai: All units, fall back!

被打退的士兵们跌跌撞撞地向更狭窄的防线退缩。
Soldiers stagger back toward their shrinking defensive line.

重塑·门徒：ㄨ……！！
Manus Disciple: ㄨ ...!!

重塑之手乘胜追击，眼看就要占领原本基金会方防守的阵地。
The Manus disciples follow up with another attack, threatening to overrun their previous positions.

洛佩拉：唔啊——
Lopera: Whoa!

布置在阵地上的炼金术炸弹接连起爆，洛佩拉和重塑之手的士兵一同摔在坡道上。
There is a chain explosion of alchemical mines, sending both Lopera and the Manus disciples rolling down the slope.

帕拉维扬：洛佩拉，你还好吗？
Paravyan: Lopera! Are you alright?

从时代病中挣脱的帕拉维扬从山坡上滑下，在浓烟中搜寻炼金术师的身影。
Paravyan, now recovered from the Storm Syndrome, slides down the hill and searches for Lopera through the smoke.

洛佩拉从一阵泥泞中爬起来。
Lopera staggers out of the mud and rises to her feet.

洛佩拉：我没事，平衡伞也没事。只是这爆炸的威力……我总是猜不准。你这么快就清醒了？
Lopera: I'm fine. My Umbrella, too. I just still can't control the explosion precisely. How are you holding up?

帕拉维扬：抱歉，我不会再掉链子了……让我来掩护你。
Paravyan: Better now. Sorry I let you down back there, but I'm not afraid anymore. I'll cover you.

在爆炸掀起的浓烟中，影影绰绰地出现了更多敌人的影子，如同黑色的潮水。
More hostiles appear from the dense smoke of the explosion, encroaching on them like a ring of growing shadows.

重塑·门徒：进……攻！ㄠ——
Manus Disciple: Attack! ㄠ!

洛佩拉：重塑之手到底埋伏了多少人？
Lopera: How many of them are there?

她掏出手枪，重新躲回掩体。
She draws her gun and ducks behind cover.

洛佩拉：别过来，帕拉维扬，敌人冲上来了！
Lopera: Stay put, Paravyan. They're charging.

帕拉维扬：——我看到他们了！
Paravyan: Copy that. I see them.

洛佩拉：……该死的！
Lopera: Damn it!

洛佩拉：帕拉维扬？
Lopera: Paravyan?

洛佩拉：嗯？这是……
Lopera: Hmm? What?

一些过分熟悉的符文在土地上闪烁着辉光。
All-too-familiar runes radiate along the dirt around her.

洛佩拉：不、不会吧——
Lopera: N-No way.

洛佩拉：——唔！
Lopera: Ugh!

她刚想站起，却再一次摔在地上。
Try as she might to stand up, she's knocked back down.

无数呐喊声在耳内混溶为尖锐的啼鸣。
A cacophony of shouts mixes into sharp bird calls in her ears.

迫近的脚步声向她扑来。
Footsteps draw near.

身旁的声音Ⅰ：我看到了，还有一个活的。我去看看。
Voice I: There's another one over there. I'll go take a look.

身旁的声音Ⅱ：小心。
Voice II: Be careful.

身旁的声音Ⅰ：这是……一个女孩儿？中尉，快过来看看。
Voice I: Hey. I've got a girl? Lieutenant, you need to see this.

洛佩拉：谁……？
Lopera: Who's ...?

她不喜欢在危险临近时束手无策的感觉。
She rages against her own helplessness.

洛佩拉摸索着抓起摔在一旁的左轮手枪，准备最后一搏。
Lopera scrambles for her revolver, just barely managing to find it on the ground. She breathes out and prepares to make her last stand.

洛佩拉：你……
Lopera: You ...

身旁的声音Ⅱ：别开枪！
Voice II: Don't shoot!

洛佩拉：——！
Lopera: ...!

她感觉自己被整个抱了起来。
She feels herself being pulled into an embrace.

莫莉德尔：洛佩拉！
Moldir: Lopera!

洛佩拉：莫莉……怎么会，是你？
Lopera: Molly, is that really you?

莫莉德尔：别动，我马上带你去安全的地方……
Moldir: Hang in there. I'll get you somewhere safe.

伊戈尔：……咳。
Igor: *cough*

托勒密：您还好吗，父亲？
Ptolemy: Are you alright, Father?

将军审视着战场。
Igor surveys the battlefield.

伊戈尔：重塑之手短时间无法再发动攻击了。
Igor: Manus Vindictae won't be able to launch another attack for some time.

伊戈尔：托勒密，你去联系基金会的人，告诉他们，我们不是敌人。
Igor: Ptolemy, we must reach the convoy's commander. Let them know we are not their enemy.

托勒密：遵命。
Ptolemy: Yes, Father.

狮鹫军的出现带来的混乱很快平息了——眼下所有人都需面对更多亟待解决的问题。
The chaos following the Gryphon Legion's arrival settles into an uneasy pause. There are more pressing matters at hand.

托勒密：和您想的一样，基金会的人很快就冷静下来了，没有闹出人命。
Ptolemy: The Foundation convoy has calmed down, as you predicted. We've avoided any unnecessary casualties.

伊戈尔：是我们救了他们，这点他们不会看不出来。他们的指挥官是谁？
Igor: We saved their lives. They have the sense to see that. Who is their commanding officer?

托勒密：他们的临时指挥官叫叶尔莫莱，还算识相，没有费多少口舌就愿意和我们合作。
Ptolemy: Their commander was killed, Father. The ranking officer is called Yermolai. He seems able enough. It wasn't difficult to persuade him that we were here to help.

托勒密：从装备来看，这些人应该是基金会的应急反应部队。重塑之手差点一口把他们都吃了。
Ptolemy: They're from the Foundation's QRF. The Manus ambush nearly wiped them out.

托勒密：我去把叶尔莫莱叫来，还有……洛佩拉想见您。
Ptolemy: I'll bring their officer here. And Father, Lopera wants to see you.

托勒密敬礼，转身离开。
Ptolemy salutes, turns, and leaves.

莫莉德尔：洛佩拉在莫莉德尔的监护下，从另一个方向走来。
Moldir escorts Lopera over to her father.

莫莉德尔：洛佩拉，刚才的爆炸可能给你造成了内伤。我们之后还有时间谈谈，你一定要现在——
Moldir: You might have an internal injury of some kind from that explosion, Pera. We'll have time to talk, but for now you need to—

洛佩拉：不要管我。
Lopera: Get away from me.

莫莉德尔：……
Moldir: ...

洛佩拉转向狮鹫军的领袖。
Lopera turns to face her father.

洛佩拉：……你为什么会来？
Lopera: What are you doing here?

伊戈尔：尽我所能。
Igor: Trying to help.

伊戈尔：我本应该抵达基金会总部的，但重塑之手的神秘术干扰让我的传送目的地发生了一些错误。
Igor: Our intention was to arrive directly at the Foundation's headquarters. But it seems whatever arcane skills the Manus have employed are interfering with my own.

伊戈尔：不过，这样我刚好可以救下你们。
Igor: You were fortunate. It seems we arrived without a moment to spare.

洛佩拉：救下我们，然后呢？想要带着我们去进攻基金会总部吗？
Lopera: And what? You're going to force me to join your assault on the Foundation?

伊戈尔：我的本意是阻止基金会遭受毁灭性的打击。
Igor: Our goal is to protect the Foundation, not destroy it.

洛佩拉：什么？
Lopera: Huh?

老将军沉默片刻，撇开了话题。
Igor lets the statement rest and moves on.

伊戈尔：你们有足够派发给每一个人的平衡伞吗？
Igor: Do you have enough Equilibrium Umbrellas for everyone?

洛佩拉：把丢失的车辆找回来就有了。
Lopera: We should, as long as our missing vehicles are intact.

伊戈尔：那就是没有。
Igor: Then the answer is no.

伊戈尔：你们想要在最后抽签决定，有谁拿不到伞吗？
Igor: What do you plan to do, Lopera? Draw lots for the remaining devices?

洛佩拉：我们要去的是第一防线学校。那里有免疫区，把学校的敌人驱逐之后我们就能在原地避雨。
Lopera: Our orders were to reach the SPDM. They have an immunity area, as long as we can manage to secure the school.

伊戈尔：你们被困住了。你们能保证自己在“暴雨”落下前的短时间内突破重塑之手的包围吗？
Igor: Can you be certain that you will secure the immunity area before the "Storm" arrives?

伊戈尔：只有我能带你们离开这里。
Igor: There is no choice in the matter. You will come with us to our shelter.

洛佩拉：离开？
Lopera: Your shelter?

伊戈尔：我要对跟我来的士兵负责。
Igor: I must put my soldiers first.

伊戈尔：狮鹫军没有足够的免疫设备，我们是在没有任何掩护的情况下在“暴雨”前展开机动的。
Igor: We don't have enough Umbrellas for the entire legion. My men risked their lives to follow me here.

洛佩拉：没有……你疯了？你带来的这些人，他们都没有免疫“暴雨”的装置？
Lopera: What? Are you out of your mind? You mean they're all exposed to the Storm Syndrome right now?

洛佩拉：难道莫莉也是？
Lopera: Don't tell me that includes Molly!

莫莉德尔：……只要能在回溯发生之前回到庇护所，那一切就还来得及。
Moldir: It's alright, Pera. We'll be fine as long as we get back to the shelter before the reversion starts.

莫莉德尔：愿意跟随父亲过来的人也都是出于自愿。请相信我，洛佩拉，我们原本是想提供一些帮助。
Moldir: We volunteered for this operation. Please, Pera, we're trying to help you.

洛佩拉：你们怎么会知道基金会正在遭遇什么？
Lopera: How did you know the Foundation was under attack?

莫莉德尔：我们获取了一些情报，但它们来得太迟，错过了最佳时机……
Moldir: We have our sources, but the intel came too late. We missed our window.

莫莉德尔：洛佩拉，下一次传送不一定能带我们抵达正确的目的地，我们失去了支援基金会总部的机会。
Moldir: We're unable to teleport there, which means there's nothing we can do to reinforce the Foundation. We're out of options, and I have no intention of leaving you here to die.

洛佩拉：我绝不会——
Lopera: I won't go.

叶尔莫莱：好了，洛佩拉，拜托你安静一下，这里该由我负责谈话。
Yermolai: Lopera, I'll remind you that I'm your acting commander.

临时指挥官被托勒密带了过来。他看着莫莉德尔带走愤怒的女孩，随后转向狮鹫军的领袖。
He watches Lopera struggling in Moldir's grip, then turns to Igor.

叶尔莫莱：你的副手告诉我，不会有基金会的援军了？
Yermolai: Your deputy said that headquarters couldn't reinforce us. Why is that?

伊戈尔：不会了。基金会总部正在面临比这更加严重的危机。
Igor: The situation there is much worse.

叶尔莫莱：我倒是盼望着这一天，但不是该死的今天。到底发生了什么？
Yermolai: Tch. I've had dreams of burning that place down, but not today, not now. What's happened?

伊戈尔：现在没有时间详细解释。
Igor: I do not have the time to brief you.

伊戈尔：在“回溯”发生之前，我的力量只够再开启一次通道，以突破术阵的迷惑。
Igor: I am only able to open my gateway once more before the reversion begins. I can take what's left of your squad to safety.

伊戈尔：而这一次，我只能选择回到狮鹫军的庇护所，这是我在重塑之手的干扰下确定能抵达的目的地。
Igor: We will fall back to our shelter. It is the only safe location I can reach without risking Manus interference.

伊戈尔：狮鹫军可以协助你们的队伍尝试突破重塑之手的围困，直到“暴雨”落下前的最后一刻。
Igor: For now, I have ordered my legion to assist in decoding this array.

伊戈尔：到那时，你只剩两个选择：与我们一同离开，或者继续留在重塑之手的陷阱中，让你的队伍被“暴雨”回溯。
Igor: But if we can't break through before the "Storm" arrives, you'll have no choice but to leave with us.

叶尔莫莱：……而前者意味着我们全都会被打上“叛逃”的标记，成为和你一样的基金会通缉犯。
Yermolai: Then we'll be marked as "traitors," wanted by the Foundation. Just like you.

老将军望向基金会的方向，并未否认这一后果。
Igor looks out in the direction of the Foundation. He doesn't argue the point.

叶尔莫莱：老天，我可不想下次和老同事见面是被他们用枪指着。
Yermolai: I may not be the fondest of them, but I'm not exactly looking to have their guns pointed at me either.

伊戈尔：现在不是说这个的时候，敌人的下一波攻势就要来了。
Igor: Your complaints can wait. The Manus are preparing another attack.

会长办公室
President's Office

饱满的宁静充斥着基金会总部大楼最顶层的房间。
The top floor of Foundation headquarters remains tranquil despite the chaos below.

低楼层战斗的震颤传到顶部时，刚好可以撼动会长办公室内的一粒灰尘。
By the time the tremors of explosions and incantations reach it, they do little more than stir the dust in the president's office.

秘书长：……愿和平与我们同在。
Secretary General: May the peace be with us.

会长：祂来了？
President: Is she here?

秘书长：已经目击到阿尔卡纳进入了委员会大楼，按照现在我方抵抗的有效性，祂预计会在三十分钟后抵达最高层。
Secretary General: Arcana has been confirmed to be within the Committee Building. Based on the strength of her forces, I estimate she'll reach us within 30 minutes.

会长：最近的部队要多久才能回援？
President: How long until reinforcements arrive?

秘书长：地面部队需要九十分钟——回援意味着他们要在行军途中穿越“暴雨”，所以只有少数配备平衡伞的队伍能够紧急机动。
Secretary General: The nearest ground units are 90 minutes away. Unfortunately, they'll need to brave the "Storm" to reach us. They're sending whatever troops they can equip with Equilibrium Umbrellas.

秘书长：部分飞行巫师正在赶回来的路上。他们的速度更快，但数量不足，对室内战斗的作用也不大。
Secretary General: The Flying Witches are also en route. They should arrive soon, but they don't have the numbers to change our situation. And their air advantage won't be of much use indoors.

会长：为基金会总部的人员开放所有楼层和工具的使用权限，尽可能为所有人提供可以用于抵抗的武装。
President: Grant all personnel at headquarters access to restricted areas and any available weapons. I want everyone armed.

会长：这样应该能多拖延三十分钟。
President: That should buy us another 30 minutes.

秘书长：我是否可以理解为，基金会无力凭借自己的力量解决现在的危机？
Secretary General: Do you believe the Foundation is no longer capable of solving this crisis on its own?

白衣人抛出了一个明知答案的问题。
She poses the question despite already knowing the answer.

会长：是。
President: Yes.

秘书长：请问司辰现在情况如何？
Secretary General: What's the Timekeeper's status?

会长：联系不到她。基金会和第一防线学校之间的通讯在一小时前就已经中断。
President: We can't reach her. We lost communication with the SPDM over an hour ago.

几只白鸽——光洁得不似俗物——冷漠地停在会长办公桌的边缘。
Several doves, almost ethereal in their luster, perch calmly on the edge of the president's desk.

基金会会长握起一只鸽子，轻轻抚摸。
He picks one up and strokes it.

会长：要多久才能等到鸽子屋的回音？
President: How long until the Pax House responds?

秘书长：很快。
Secretary General: Soon.

秘书长：哲人王已经知道祂来了。
Secretary General: The Philosopher King is aware of her arrival.

会长：这样就足够了。
President: Then that's all we need.

会长将白鸽举在窗边。
The president holds the dove to the window.

会长：愿和平与我们同在。
President: May the peace be with us.

22  花的密语 / Whisper of Flowers

花香幽远
Fragrance from Afar

[一朵随“风”而来的花，花心飘散出蜜的香味与柔和的女声]

普莱玛，伊戈尔与他的狮鹫军救下了基金会的支援车队，希望你能尽可能把他们拖在这里，但也仅仅是拖住他们的脚步即可。
不用好奇，我的确没有答应参与这场行动，我也依然反对去袭击学校，无谓的斗争与流血对神秘学家的未来并无益处……但，我毕竟还要履行我的职责，无论如何，我们都要为祂的大业尽心竭力。好了，我只负责传递这则消息，不必来寻找我，我很快就会离开。

[芬芳的幽香和飘渺的声音一同消失了]
[A flower carried on the "wind." From its heart drifts the scent of honey and the soft voice of a woman.]

Prima, Igor, and his Gryphon Legion have rescued the Foundation's support convoy. I need you to stall them for as long as possible—though slowing them down is all that's required.
Don't ask. I never agreed to take part in this operation, and I still oppose the attack on the school. Pointless fighting and bloodshed do no good for the future of arcanists ... But I still have my duty to fulfill. Whatever comes, we must give everything we have to Her cause. That's all—I'm only here to deliver this message. Don't come looking for me. I'll be gone soon.

[The sweet fragrance and the faint voice fade together.]

23  秘密通讯 / Secret Communication

阅后即焚
Burn After Reading

[一支突然开始发热的羽毛笔被从军装大衣口袋中拿出，搁置于一张白纸之上，羽笔凭空而动，写下一行行带有烧焦痕迹的字迹]

第一防线学校刚刚受到重塑之手的袭击，我即将前往援助。事发突然，又值基金会扩大收容之时，目前总部内人员混杂，我怀疑重塑之手的计划并不单纯。如你方有额外相关情报，恳请共享必要信息，万分感谢。距离“暴雨”已不足3小时，务必谨慎行事。

[当将军阅读完最后一个字，羽笔的光芒黯淡下来，所有字迹一同燃烧，将白纸瞬间吞噬，连一丝灰烬也没有剩下]
[A quill grows hot without warning. Drawn from a uniform coat pocket and set upon blank paper, it begins to move on its own, leaving lines of scorched writing.]

SPDM is under attack by Manus Vindictae. I'm on my way to assist. The attack came without warning. Amid the Foundation's containment expansion, headquarters is crowded with unfamiliar personnel. I suspect Manus Vindictae's plans run deeper than this. Share any relevant intelligence if you have it. Fewer than three hours remain until the "Storm." Stay cautious.

[As the admiral reads the final word, the quill's glow dims. The writing catches fire along with the paper, consuming it in an instant, leaving not even ash.]

24  变形术学徒 / The Shapeshifter

变形术学徒
The Shapeshifter

图书馆·藏书区
Book Stacks, Library

梅蕾尔：你好，我要借这本书。
Merel: Excuse me, miss. May I borrow this book?

管理员：《五种隐形药水的简易配置指南》？同学，你真的要借这本书吗？你们马上就要期末考了，这本书和你的考试没有关系吧？
School Librarian: Five Easy Recipes to Brew an Invisibility Potion? Are you sure you've picked the right book, dear? The final exams are coming up, and I doubt this appears anywhere on your syllabus.

梅蕾尔：……是没有关系，但是……
Merel: No, it doesn't, but ...

她缩着肩膀。
She hunches her shoulders.

梅蕾尔：但是我也看不下去要考的书。我已经看过了，我……尽力了。
Merel: I just want something different to read. I've gone through every book on the syllabus, or at least stared at them until my brain gave up.

梅蕾尔：反正我都会考砸的。
Merel: I'll probably fail. Maybe that's for the best.

玛蒂尔达：嘿，梅蕾尔。
Matilda: Merel!

书柜边传来一声呼喊。梅蕾尔回头，看见一个金发女孩势不可当地朝自己走来。
A noise from the nearby bookshelves grabs Merel's attention. She turns to see another girl approaching her, beaming with confidence.

梅蕾尔：……？！
Merel: ...?!

梅蕾尔：你……你是？
Merel: S-Sorry. Do I know you?

玛蒂尔达：我是玛蒂尔达·布翁尼，你一定听过我的名字。
Matilda: I am Matilda Bouanich. Though you have doubtlessly heard of me.

梅蕾尔：你好，玛蒂尔达。你怎么知道我叫什么？
Merel: Um, okay. How did you know my name?

玛蒂尔达：玛蒂尔达会记住自己的每一个同学，所以你也要好好记住我的名字，这样当你遇到困难的时候就知道该找谁了。
Matilda: Why, I remembered it, of course. I remember everyone's name. You should learn to do the same, starting with mine. That way, you'll know who to turn to if you need any help.

玛蒂尔达：你是今年刚入学的新生，对吧？
Matilda: You're one of the new students, isn't that right?

梅蕾尔：是的。
Merel: Yeah.

玛蒂尔达：我知道你的考试成绩很差，但是没关系，不是所有人都像我一样在入学之前就学会了课本上的内容，过阵子就会好了。
Matilda: I know you've been struggling, but not everyone arrives having mastered the entire syllabus as I have. You'll get the hang of it in no time. Trust me.

梅蕾尔：如果是这样就好了。你也是来借书的吗？你手上的书是……《变形术导论与术法演变研究》？
Merel: I guess so. Are you borrowing books too? What did you get? An Introduction to Shapeshifting and Its Practice Through Time?

那是一本厚如砖石的严肃著作，梅蕾尔都不用翻开书页，就知道这本书的每一页纸都塞满密密麻麻的小字。
An intimidating book, thick as a brick. Merel doesn't need to open it to know every page will be filled with walls of teeny-tiny text.

梅蕾尔：你想学习变形术吗，玛蒂尔达？
Merel: Are you trying to learn to shapeshift, Matilda?

玛蒂尔达：我只是随便看看。
Matilda: I just want to get a taste of it, that's all.

梅蕾尔：随便看看？那这本书也许太厚了，我觉得《笨蛋也能学会的招潮蟹变形术》比这本书更适合入门，我看过，推荐给你。
Merel: But that book's huge. Err, how about Fiddler Crab Shapeshifting for Dummies? I've read it. It's simple and fun, perfect for beginners like us.

玛蒂尔达：可是——那本书的标题实在太冒犯了！我才不是什么“笨蛋”呢，我不会去看那本书。
Matilda: Excuse me?! I've never read a more insulting title! Matilda Bouanich is no "dummy," and she absolutely will not read a book that is meant for one!

玛蒂尔达：既然要学习变形术，玛蒂尔达就要看最权威的著作，这样才不会像你们一样——在把自己变成湿乎乎的大螃蟹之后无法复原。
Matilda: If I am to learn shapeshifting, I will do it properly and learn from the best! I will not end up as some sorry, soggy, crabby little thing crawling around on the floor.

玛蒂尔达：我知道一些真正的笨蛋也偷偷尝试了书里的方法，最后他们都不得不挥舞着钳子，去敲校医务室的门。
Matilda: Besides, we've already had quite enough dummies knocking on the infirmary's door with claws instead of hands.

玛蒂尔达：听说十四行诗也借过这本书，但是她没有变成螃蟹，不知道她是不是完全学会了……要找个什么理由去问她呢？
Matilda: Though, I did hear Sonetto once borrowed that book. But I simply cannot imagine her turning into a crab. Hmm. I wonder if she succeeded? There must be a way I can find out.

玛蒂尔达：不、不管了，玛蒂尔达才不需要变形术这种小把戏，布翁尼家的水晶占卜就是最厉害的神秘术。
Matilda: N-Never mind. Why would Matilda concern herself with such petty tricks? We Bouaniches have already mastered something far more powerful—command of the crystal orbs.

优等生自顾自地走开了，手中仍然拿着厚厚的《变形术导论与术法演变研究》。
Matilda walks away, hefting a copy of An Introduction to Shapeshifting and Its Practice Through Time.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

图书馆·阅览室
Reading Room, Library

她手中拿着一本从图书馆走廊上捡到的书。
She holds a book she picked up from the library corridor.

梅蕾尔：其实一开始听说要回学校的时候，我没有很开心。我只是觉得，有一份简单的活干也不错。
Merel: To be honest, I wasn't exactly thrilled when they told me I'd be sent here. But I guess there are worse places I could've been stationed, so I didn't complain.

梅蕾尔：我不怀念学校的广播，230伏的电网，不见天日的禁闭室。但是……
Merel: I can't say I've missed the school announcements, or the electric fences and detention rooms. Not even for a second.

毕业生把书本放在玛蒂尔达枕边。
She places the book beside Matilda's pillow.

梅蕾尔：……我还挺喜欢图书馆的，这里是我唯一喜欢的地方了。
Merel: But I've always liked the library. It's probably the only part of this school that I did.

玛蒂尔达：……
Matilda: ...

昔日的同窗仍昏迷不醒。
She's alive but hasn't stirred at all since they brought her in.

梅蕾尔：我会做好我们的工作的，玛蒂尔达，倒不是为了这座学校……
Merel: I will see this through, Matilda. Not for the sake of the school, though—

艾米莉亚：既然以后都会知道，为什么现在什么都不对我们说呢？
Emilia: The instructors are hiding something. But if we'll find out one day anyway, why don't they just tell us now?

艾米莉亚：或者，你能……和我说说外面的世界在发生什么吗？我在毕业之前绝对会保密，不会告诉别人。
Emilia: Miss, why can't you just tell us what's happening out there? I swear we'll keep our mouths shut.

卢卡斯：不要问了，艾米莉亚。你看她们的样子，就知道她们都不会说的。
Lucas: Don't bother, Emilia. They're not gonna tell you anything.

卢卡斯：她们明明知道外面发生了什么，明明以前也和我们一样，但毕业之后就变得和那些讨厌的教员一样了。
Lucas: They know. But even if they used to be students like us, they're just like them now.

梅蕾尔：……是为了被困在这里的朋友，还有和过去的我们一样的学生。
Merel: —But for our friends and for the students who remind me of who we once were.

梅蕾尔：总会有和平到来的那一天，等这里的学生毕业之后，他们看到的世界也许会比我们看到的更好。
Merel: This war will end someday. And when it does, I hope they'll live in a kinder world than the one we grew up in.

身后响起轻盈的脚步声，梅蕾尔转过身。
Merel, hearing light footsteps echo behind her, turns around.

拉文德：姐姐，玛蒂尔达小姐还好吗？
Lavender: Merel, is Ms. Bouanich going to be okay?

梅蕾尔：我还是叫不醒她，可能是敌人神秘术的影响。
Merel: *sigh* She still hasn't regained consciousness. It might have something to do with the arcane skill that hit her.

梅蕾尔：不过她的身体没有什么大碍，我给她做了一个伤势评估，情况不坏。或许康复中心的医生能有办法治好她。
Merel: But her vitals are stable, and the casualty assessment didn't show anything serious. The rehab center should have her back on her feet in no time.

拉文德：伤势……评估？
Lavender: Casualty assessment?

梅蕾尔：是……我在夜巡特遣的时候学的，还好没有忘记。
Merel: Yeah. It's a process I learned back at the Vigiles Bureau. Just glad I haven't forgotten it.

拉文德：夜巡特遣——你在毕业之后去了夜巡特遣吗？这可真厉害。
Lavender: The Vigiles Bureau ... Was that your first job after school? You're incredible.

梅蕾尔：我可算不上是什么厉害的人，在这里读书的时候成绩一直很差。
Merel: I doubt my instructor back then would agree. I didn't do very well in the exams.

拉文德：不，玛蒂尔达小姐告诉我，考试成绩不是一切。你一定能做到其他人做不到的事，所以才能被夜巡特遣选上。
Lavender: Ms. Bouanich once told me that grades aren't everything. You must have had other strengths the Vigiles Bureau valued.

在拉文德敬佩的目光下，真实情况有些不太好被说出口——
Lavender's reverent gaze makes it hard for Merel to tell her the truth.

梅蕾尔因为毕业成绩太差没有机构愿意接收，只有急缺人手的刻雷乌斯找上门来。
How could she confess that her grades were so bad that nobody except Creius, who was so desperate he'd accept any graduate with a pulse, would take her in?

拉文德：……我也快毕业了，到时候我是不是也能像你一样加入夜巡特遣，或者像玛蒂尔达小姐那样成为调查员？
Lavender: When I graduate, I hope I can join the Vigiles like you or be an investigator, like Ms. Bouanich. Do you think I stand a chance?

梅蕾尔：当然可以，不过你不想在基金会总部就职吗？当文员会比外勤人员更安全。
Merel: Of course! But you should really consider working at the Foundation's headquarters. It's much safer behind a desk than out in the field.

拉文德：我不喜欢坐办公室，而且……
Lavender: I don't want to be stuck indoors. Besides ...

拉文德：玛蒂尔达小姐和十四行诗小姐都选择了成为外派调查员，还有你……你们一定是选择了一件比在总部任职更有意义的事。
Lavender: Ms. Bouanich and Ms. Sonetto chose to be field operatives, and so did you. You all must've seen a reason to do it.

拉文德：虽然我不知道外派调查员、夜巡特遣的执夜人，还有特别行动小队队员有什么区别，但听起来都像是我想去做的事。
Lavender: I don't really know all the details of what investigators, Vigil officers, or soldiers actually do out there, but I know it's what I want to do.

梅蕾尔：其实，有些时候这些职位的分工不是那么明晰……
Merel: They aren't all that different, really. Sometimes you'll find their duties overlap.

十四行诗：——梅蕾尔？
Sonetto: Merel?

橘发毕业生站在阅览室的门边。
Sonetto stands in the reading room's doorway.

梅蕾尔：我在，玛蒂尔达状况还好。有其他事吗，十四行诗？
Merel: Over here! Matilda's condition hasn't changed. Is there something you need?

十四行诗：司辰希望你来一下自习室。
Sonetto: The Timekeeper wants a word with you in the study room.

梅蕾尔：好的，这就来。
Merel: Sure. I'm on my way.

拉文德：那我去看看其他学生有没有闯祸。
Lavender: I better go check on the other students again—see how they've been behaving.

拉文德：有些年纪小的学生说想从书里找到对付“暴雨”的办法……但图书馆的书里是不会有这种东西的。
Lavender: Some of the younger ones think they might find a solution to the impending "Storm" in the books. But I figure if the answer really was in a book, someone would have found it already.

拉文德离开阅览室，进入走廊，从许多围成圆圈的学生旁穿过。
Lavender exits the room and reenters the crowd of nervous students in the library corridor.

图书馆·自习室
Study Room, Library

一片凌乱的自习室里，凝重的氛围让梅蕾尔产生一种事态不妙的直觉。
Despite the mess, the study room's atmosphere feels solemn. Merel can't help but sense the tension.

梅蕾尔：维尔汀……是有坏消息吗？
Merel: You wanted to see me? What's wrong?

视线从窗外收回。
She looks away from the window.

维尔汀：救援车队早就该抵达学校了，但现在还是不见踪影。学校与总部的通讯也被彻底切断。
Vertin: The convoy should've arrived by now, but there's no sign of them. I haven't been able to reach headquarters either.

维尔汀：我想……我们应该做好最坏的打算了。
Vertin: I'm afraid we have to prepare for the worst.

梅蕾尔默默走到桌边，看向之前摆在桌面上的地图。
Merel approaches the table, looking over the map that they've laid out depicting the SPDM campus.

梅蕾尔：这是你标记的免疫区收缩速度？
Merel: Is this the contraction rate of the immunity area?

维尔汀：是的，在最近的十五分钟，免疫区的半径收缩了三十米。这比我们一开始预想的速度还要快不少。
Vertin: Yes. Its radius has reduced by 30 meters in the last 15 minutes. It's shrinking even faster than I'd anticipated.

维尔汀：按照这个速度，回溯发生的前十分钟，整个图书馆都会处在免疫区之外。
Vertin: At this rate, the library will be exposed ten minutes before the "Storm" arrives.

梅蕾尔：图书馆里还有这么多学生……
Merel: But, there are so many students.

维尔汀：我们需要在事态无法挽回前做点什么。所以，我决定试一试寻找“奇迹”中可能存在的庇护。
Vertin: That's why I called you here. We don't have the luxury of waiting for rescue anymore. I've decided to see whether the "boon" can be of any use.

十四行诗：……
Sonetto: ...

从第一助手叹气的样子来看，她已经劝过了。
The resigned silence conveys her feelings on the matter.

梅蕾尔：“奇迹”？
Merel: "Boon"?

维尔汀：这是在顿河调查时，伊戈尔交给我的东西。他称呼它为“奇迹”。
Vertin: It's an item Igor gave me during the Don Operation.

维尔汀：这颗立方体中或许存在着某种能在“暴雨”中提供庇护的力量，但……
Vertin: It's possible that it could generate an immunity zone. But ...

十四行诗：但也有可能，其中封存的是难以预料的毁灭，毕竟……它是阿尔卡纳给予伊戈尔的东西。
Sonetto: But we have almost no idea what's inside it. It could save us or destroy us. It was a gift from Arcana. Let's not forget that.

梅蕾尔：阿尔卡纳？！那不是很危险吗？
Merel: Arcana?! Then, shouldn't we stay as far away from it as possible?

十四行诗：非常危险，就像故事里的“潘多拉魔盒”。打开它，就会释放出瘟疫和灾难……
Sonetto: I think so too. It really is like Pandora's box. Once opened, there'll be no undoing the consequences.

维尔汀：但也可能藏着最后的希望。
Vertin: Even so, it may be our last hope to save everyone here.

伊戈尔：“我想，你才是带领人们走出洪水的救世主。它真正的主人是你。”
Igor: "You are the true savior destined to lead us from the flood. Take it."

维尔汀：伊戈尔说过，我是这份“奇迹”的主人，我不知道他为何如此笃定。
Vertin: Igor believed that I was meant to have it. He was absolutely certain, though I still don't understand why.

维尔汀：如果我真的能够利用它，眼下就能找到从“暴雨”中保护学生们的办法。
Vertin: But if I can make it work, it could shield us all from the "Storm." I'd be saving not just you two, but all the students here.

维尔汀：就像在南极的时候，伊戈尔曾用它让自己的军队躲过了一次时代回溯。
Vertin: Igor managed to protect his entire army from the "Flood" in Antarctica, and I believe he did it with the power of this cube.

梅蕾尔：可是这一切都只是猜想和假设吧，如果……如果它只是一个大玩具呢？
Merel: But that's hardly for certain. What if we open it and find out there's nothing inside?

维尔汀：那我们只剩下唯一的选择——带领图书馆的所有人一起转移到中央瞭望塔。前提是重塑之手没有破坏那里的防护措施。
Vertin: Then we attempt to reach the central watchtower, assuming the Manus hasn't destroyed the Dove there too.

十四行诗：我怀疑重塑之手不会放过这个学校的中心设施。何况，凭我们的力量，没有办法在转移路上照顾所有学生和伤员……
Sonetto: They'll hit us with everything they have out there. And even if we somehow avoided them, how could we manage to get all these children out safely?

言外之意让梅蕾尔的手心失去了温度。
The implication leaves Merel's palms cold.

梅蕾尔：我……我想我明白这是什么意思了。
Merel: There really aren't any options left, then.

“奇迹”再一次出现在手心，向四周发散神秘莫测的光芒。
The "boon" appears in her hands once more and casts its mysterious, unpredictable glow across the room.

维尔汀：玛格丽特小姐交给我的炼金药剂已经安装到位，只要念诵咒语就可以摧毁它的外壳……
Vertin: I've already applied the alchemical compound Ms. Marguerite gave me. She said it will begin to dismantle the shell as soon as the incantation is recited.

时间。需要一些时间平复紧张的情绪。
Time. She needs time to calm her nerves.

十四行诗&梅蕾尔：……
Sonetto & Merel: ...

维尔汀：洞开铅铸之谜。
Vertin: Unlock   the   leaden   secret.

混乱平息后，一扇空洞、黑暗的门扉孤傲地出现在人的视线中。
The momentary chaos ends, and an empty, black gate manifests ahead.

似乎有什么超凡之物从中向四周辐射，逐渐将周遭的一切凡俗之物覆上一层古老而神秘的色彩。
An extraordinary aura radiates out from it, painting its surroundings in colors too mystical and ancient for description.

十四行诗：这是……一扇门？“奇迹”中封存着一道入口？
Sonetto: A gate? The "boon" is an entrance?

维尔汀：……我好像听到了什么声音，有人在说话。
Vertin: I hear something. A voice. Someone's calling out.

第一助手和梅蕾尔对视了一眼，从对方脸上看见了相似的疑惑。
Sonetto and Merel exchange a look of suspicion.

梅蕾尔：我完全没有听见声音。
Merel: I don't hear anything at all.

十四行诗：我听见了一些风的声音，像风吹起了地上的尘土，但没有听见人声。
Sonetto: There's only wind, blowing across the ground. No voices.

维尔汀：这声音在呼唤我们进去……我，还有你，十四行诗。
Vertin: It's calling out to us. I hear my name and yours, Sonetto.

十四行诗：我和你？这……“奇迹”出自阿尔卡纳之手，这很可能是针对你的陷阱，司辰。
Sonetto: Timekeeper, it's entirely possible that this is a trap. Stepping in there could be exactly what Arcana wants.

维尔汀：可是这个声音听起来就像是……我自己。
Vertin: But the voice ... It sounds just like mine.

梅蕾尔：从门里传来了你自己的声音？而且只有你一个人能听到？
Merel: Sorry, you're saying the voice coming from that gate is yours, and only you can hear it?

维尔汀：我知道这一切听起来都非常不可思议……
Vertin: I know how impossible it must sound.

维尔汀：但我想试一试。
Vertin: But I want to see for myself. I have to try.

十四行诗：……
Sonetto: ...

十四行诗：我知道了。请让我与你同行，司辰。
Sonetto: Then, please, at least allow me to accompany you.

维尔汀：嗯。梅蕾尔，如果我们没能及时从这扇门里回来，学生们就交给你了。
Vertin: Of course. Merel, can I ask you to wait here? Someone has to look after the students if we don't make it back.

维尔汀：在免疫区收缩到图书馆的那一刻，你就要组织好学生前往中央瞭望塔。
Vertin: You have to get them to the central tower before the library is exposed to the "Storm."

梅蕾尔：我……我刚才倒是想到了另一个办法。
Merel: I-I have another idea.

毕业生颤颤巍巍举起了手，像一名想要在课堂讨论中提出见解的学生。这是她过去绝对不会做的事。
Merel lifts a trembling hand. It feels as if she's back in school again, but volunteering ... That is a new sensation.

梅蕾尔：我可以去中央瞭望塔，把还在生效的气象鸽带回来。
Merel: I'll go to the central tower myself and bring back the Dove installed there.

十四行诗：你一个人？这太危险了，图书馆外面的校园很可能都被重塑之手占领了。
Sonetto: On your own? That's far too risky. The Manus are everywhere. They've likely taken control of the entire campus.

梅蕾尔：我是这么想的——现在学校里游荡的重塑之手大多是不太聪明的类型。
Merel: Just listen. The Manus we've encountered so far have been manageable, tough but dumb. They haven't sent anything I can't handle.

梅蕾尔：只有我一个人的话，行动会灵活很多，更容易躲开它们的攻击。
Merel: Acting alone might even be an advantage. I'll be much quicker and quieter without trying to move all these kids with me too.

梅蕾尔：我可以用变形术一口气冲到中央瞭望塔……在这个过程中，还能把围绕图书馆的敌人引走，保证学生们的安全。
Merel: If I shapeshift on the way, I might even be able to draw some enemies away from the library.

梅蕾尔：你们搞清楚门后的秘密，我去把“鸽子”带回来。虽然离开底座会加剧鸽子的能源消耗，但撑过回溯的那一瞬间应该是够的！
Merel: So, go on. Check what's behind the gate. I'll head for the Dove. It probably won't last long off its pedestal, but what's left inside should be enough to get us through the "Storm."

十四行诗：万一气象鸽的能源不足以撑过“暴雨”，或者你在路上遇见更大的危险……这太看运气了。
Sonetto: And if the Dove runs out of energy before the "Storm" arrives? Or what if something happens to you on the way? The stakes are too high. We can't afford to leave this to chance.

梅蕾尔：但、但是……说到底，现在没有人确定门里是不是真的有办法，你们也需要运气，不是吗？
Merel: We're already betting on luck, aren't we? And just waiting isn't going to improve our odds any more. What if you come back empty-handed? What then?

大脑内的混乱让说出口的语言也在打结。
The chaos in her mind causes the words to come out knotted and unfocused.

梅蕾尔：我是想，把我们的运气都加在一起！让我也分担一部分“暴雨”的危险……
Merel: No, we need to stack the cards, or-or spread the risk or something like that! Do you see what I mean?

梅蕾尔：如果你们回不来，我一个人肯定没有办法把学生们安全地带到中央瞭望塔。
Merel: And to be honest, if you don't make it back, I doubt I could get everyone to safety by myself.

梅蕾尔：可让我去把气象鸽带回来，我们就有了两手准备，学生也有了双份的生存率。
Merel: But going out alone? That I can do. This way, we double their chances of survival.

梅蕾尔：我的队长总会在作战之前做两手准备，他教过我们如何制定备用计划。
Merel: "Always have a plan to fall back on." Creius is always saying that to me.

梅蕾尔：这就是……我想到的备用计划！
Merel: And this time, I've got one.

梅蕾尔说完最后一句话，随后大口喘气。
She punctuates her speech with a deep gasp of breath.

十四行诗：……
Sonetto: ...

维尔汀：……你比我想的还要勇敢，梅蕾尔。
Vertin: I admire your courage, Merel, truly.

维尔汀：我相信你，也请你相信我。
Vertin: Go then. I trust you. And I need you to trust me, too.

维尔汀：“暴雨”前的最后一刻，我们都要平安地回到图书馆，在这里前往新的时代。
Vertin: We meet here again before the "Storm." And whatever happens, we move forward together.

梅蕾尔：嗯，约好了。
Merel: Mhm. I promise.

少女们约定好最终会合的地点，彼此交换着笃信的目光。
Having agreed upon a rendezvous location, they exchange a final glance—a moment of utmost trust.

维尔汀：我们出发吧，十四行诗。
Vertin: It's time. Let's go, Sonetto.

黑暗的门扉接纳了两名一无所知的探寻者。
The dark entrance welcomes them in.

在一阵奇异的低鸣之后，门扉与神秘的立方体都消失不见。
A strange rumbling follows. Then, both gate and cube vanish altogether.

梅蕾尔：她们……不见了。
Merel: They're gone.

下一秒，拉文德闯入自习室。
A moment later, Lavender bursts into the study room.

拉文德：姐姐，这里……发生什么了？维尔汀小姐和十四行诗小姐呢？
Lavender: Merel? What's going on? Where did Ms. Vertin and Ms. Sonetto go?

梅蕾尔：别担心，她们正在想办法让我们渡过这次“暴雨”。
Merel: They've gone to find a way to get us all through the "Storm" safely.

梅蕾尔：我……也有一些事情要做。拉文德，等会儿我会试着到中央瞭望塔去，找一些能帮助你们的东西。
Merel: I ... I'm going to leave soon, too, Lavender. I need to get to the central watchtower and see if I can find something that might help us there.

拉文德：你一个人去吗？
Lavender: By yourself?

梅蕾尔：别担心我，我知道怎么引开在图书馆外游荡的敌人。
Merel: Y-Yeah. Don't worry. I know a way to slip past the enemies outside.

拉文德：它们看起来倒是也没有攻击图书馆的意图……万一有一两个进入了图书馆，我可以和其他高年级的学生对付他们。
Lavender: Those Manus monsters have just been hanging around so far. They don't seem to be trying to get in. But even if a few try, the senior students and I should be able to manage.

拉文德：如果……数量不太多的话，我们可以做到的。
Lavender: We'll be alright here, as long as they don't all come charging in at once. You go ahead.

梅蕾尔：好的，那图书馆就先交给你们了。
Merel: I couldn't ask for more. I'll be back as soon as I can.

拉文德深吸一口气，用力点了点头。
Lavender takes a deep breath and offers a solemn nod.

十秒后，一只黑色小鸟笨拙地飞出图书馆向北的窗户。
Ten seconds pass. Then, a little blackbird clumsily flies from the library's north-facing window.

25  荆棘树 · 1/2 / Thorny Thicket · 1/2

荆棘树
Thorny Thicket

基金会办公室
Foundation Office

士兵Ⅰ：快走，你疯了吗？！你不能一直待在桌子底下！
Soldier I: Have you lost your mind? Get out from under there, now.

登记员：我、我……
Registrar: I ... I ...

他浑身发抖地裹在灰白格子里，手中捧着一沓纸张，不愿意离开他占据的角落。
He trembles in his checkered uniform, frozen in a corner. The paleness of his face is rivaled only by his white knuckles as he grips a stack of papers for dear life.

士兵Ⅰ：别管这些该死的住房登记表了！死人是不用交付工作的，快点转移！
Soldier I: Forget your bloody paperwork. Just get somewhere safe.

登记员：外面，他们……
Registrar: Those things out there ... Are-Are they ...

士兵Ⅰ：他们什么他们，不就是敌人打过来了，这种事每天都可能发生。
Soldier I: Yeah. Nowhere's safe anymore. Welcome to reality, sunshine.

士兵Ⅰ：快动起来，和我一起把这张该死的桌子抬走！我们要去帮其他人布设路障。
Soldier I: Now get on your feet and help me move this table outside. We need to barricade this place.

登记员：可是那些……我不知道，我们要怎么对付外面那些重塑之手？
Registrar: Wh-What about those monsters? What do we do about them?

士兵Ⅰ：把子弹嵌到对方的头壳里，什么怪物都会死。
Soldier I: We put a bullet through their heads, if we're lucky.

士兵面对着一张毫无血色的脸，忽然意识到了什么。
The soldier pauses, realizing in that moment the reason for the young man's terror.

士兵Ⅰ：……你不会从来没亲眼见过重塑之手吧？
Soldier I: For Pete's sake, don't tell me this is your first time seeing the Manus up close.

在总部工作多年的文职人员抬起可怜兮兮的目光，随后点了点头。
The terrified young man nods. Years of experience and long tenure in the Foundation's bureaucracy count for little in this moment.

士兵Ⅰ：哈——这可真是太有意思了！我天天和那些怪物拼死拼活，基金会里还有人连他们的面都没见过！
Soldier I: Well, I'll be damned. I fight these things every day, and half of you checkerheads haven't even seen one in the flesh!

士兵Ⅰ：你要真是个怂包，就自己躲在这儿向天祈祷吧！我要去找我的战友了。
Soldier I: Listen, if all you're going to do is stand there shaking, then I'm leaving without you. I've got to try to find my team.

登记员：等……等等！别丢下我！
Registrar: No, wait, wait! Please, don't leave me here.

士兵Ⅰ：唉，你到底想怎样？
Soldier I: Alright, then what are you going to do?

基金会职员终于舍得离开房间的角落。
The registrar finally pries himself up from the corner.

登记员：……我和你走。我们把这张桌子搬出去。
Registrar: I'll come with you. Let's move this table outside.

委员会大楼·四层
Fourth Floor, Committee Building

士兵Ⅰ：——来，再使把劲儿！
Soldier I: Just a little more.

登记员：唔——
Registrar: Ugh!

房间外，身穿文员制服的灰白格子们如受惊的飞禽，上下摆动着如羽翼般的衣摆，抱着各自的目的仓皇逃窜。
Outside the office, staff dressed in checkered uniforms flit around like a startled flock of birds, their "wings" flapping without any clear direction.

伊里内伊：非战斗人员向高层转移，离开主阶梯，不要妨碍军队调度。
Irinei: Non-combatant staff must evacuate to higher floors immediately. Clear the main stairwell—military use only.

基金会职员Ⅰ：别、别往下跳，朋友，这可是四楼啊。
Foundation Staff Member I: No, no. Don't jump. This is the fourth floor.

基金会职员Ⅱ：都给我——让开，那边的，当兵的，给我一把枪！
Foundation Staff Member II: Out of the way. Soldier, find me a weapon.

伊里内伊：够了，还没轮到你来拼命，快走，别拖其他人后腿。
Irinei: Enough. We don't need file clerks wasting ammunition. Just find somewhere safe with the rest of your colleagues.

在四散纷飞的文件中，几名特别行动小队的成员镇定地站立着。
Amid the flurry of documents stand several operation team members, calm and composed.

行动队长Ⅰ：刻雷乌斯，你自由了。
Operation Team Leader I: Looks like you're free for now, Creius.

刻雷乌斯：谈不上，审理还会继续——前提是我们都能活下来。
Creius: For the moment, yes. The trial will start up again once this all blows over, assuming we're still alive.

刻雷乌斯：我的队员呢？
Creius: Where's my squad?

行动队长Ⅰ：他们在总部遇袭之前都被派出去了，不在总部。现在我们也联系不到委员会大楼外的人。
Operation Team Leader I: They're with the Quick Reaction Force that was sent to the SPDM. We've lost contact with all our operatives in the field.

刻雷乌斯：……看来“Ⅻ”小队在这里只剩下我了。
Creius: I suppose that makes me the sole member of the "XII," then.

行动队长Ⅱ：还有一些小队也失联了，像是“剃刀”。我们一直没见到他们，恐怕他们被困在了一楼……
Operation Team Leader II: We've attempted to reach "Razor" and the others, but so far we've got nothing. They may have gotten cut off downstairs.

刻雷乌斯：援军还有多久能到？
Creius: What's the ETA on reinforcements?

康斯坦丁：十五分钟。
Constantine: Fifteen minutes.

基金会副会长出现在行动队员们身旁——她仍然留在靠近前线的楼层，没有转移。
Constantine hasn't left her post yet, but the front line creeps closer to her door each moment.

康斯坦丁：你们去告诉所有在作战的人，空中部队已经在路上，最快十五分钟就能抵达总部。
Constantine: Inform everyone you can reach: air support is on its way.

刻雷乌斯：……副会长，队长级别的成员需要知道真正的预计到达时间。
Creius: With respect, ma'am, you ought to at least be giving your captains the hard truth.

康斯坦丁：一小时……或者更久。但士兵们需要希望。
Constantine: Forces are an hour out, maybe more. Until then, we hold the line. Understood?

刻雷乌斯：了解。支援预计抵达时间：60分钟。
Creius: Understood. Reinforcement ETA: 60 minutes.

Z：女士，有一些共融院的人在就近的办公室里，他们希望尽快与你进行简短的会谈。
Z: Ma'am, we have representatives from the House of Integratus waiting. They request a brief meeting.

康斯坦丁：我马上去见他们。特别行动小队的各位——保重。
Constantine: I'm on my way. Best of luck, soldiers.

Z：……祝愿你们都能平安归来。
Z: Godspeed, officers.

政治家们离去了。
With that, the gathered politicians hurry away.

行动队长Ⅱ：好吧，现在我们的计划是逐层向上有序转移，保证有生力量不要被过度消耗或是暴露在雨中。
Operation Team Leader II: Plan's simple. We let them push up, layering out a defense in depth, giving ground as necessary to conserve ammo and avoid exposure to the "Storm."

行动队长Ⅱ：直到所有非军事人员撤到顶层。如果那时危机还没有过去……至少我们已经争取过了。
Operation Team Leader II: Priority for now is to buy time for all non-combatant staff to reach the top floor. If reinforcements don't come in time to save our necks, then at least we'll die standing.

他拍了拍刻雷乌斯的肩膀。
He pats Creius's shoulder.

行动队长Ⅱ：调度中心在重新集结各行动小队，基金会的行动队伍会负责普通士兵对付不了的神秘学家。你要加入我们吗，刻雷乌斯？
Operation Team Leader II: The Dispatch Center will be coordinating our teams to focus on the Manus's arcanist troops. You coming with us, Creius?

刻雷乌斯：当然，就算“Ⅻ”小队只剩下我一个人，也是一份力量。
Creius: The "XII" Squad always stands ready, even if I'm the only one left.

刻雷乌斯：开始行动吧。
Creius: Let's move out.

特别行动小队逆着向上撤离的人流，走下了通往交战区的阶梯。
Their team moves through fleeing bureaucrats and takes the stairs down toward the ever-nearing battle.

委员会大楼·三层
Third Floor, Committee Building

基金会安保：低头！
Foundation Security: Get down!

致命的术法被厚重的混凝土拦下，掩体之后，刻雷乌斯低着身子快速走过。
Thick cement manages to block a fatal incantation.

他来到指挥官身边。
Crouching low behind cover, Creius makes his way to the commander's side.

刻雷乌斯：我是特别行动队的，这里战况如何？
Creius: We're from Special Operations. What's the situation here?

基金会安保：二层刚刚沦陷，重塑之手的先头部队已经开始从主楼梯进攻三层。
Foundation Security: We've lost the second floor. The Manus are pushing up the main stairs.

基金会安保：我不敢确定敌方主力到底有多强，我们可能守不住第三层。
Foundation Security: We've got nothing reliable on their numbers or composition, sir. At this rate, I doubt we can hold the third floor for long.

基金会安保：调度中心说会有援军，他们还要多久才能到？
Foundation Security: Dispatch told us reinforcements are coming. What's their ETA?

刻雷乌斯：……15分钟。再坚持一会，把这个消息告诉其他人。
Creius: Um. We need you to hold on for 15 minutes. Understood, soldier? Spread the word.

基金会安保：15分钟……行吧！
Foundation Security: Fifteen minutes. We can manage that.

基金会的防线被继续压缩，防卫者们为了15分钟之后的期望维持着摇摇欲坠的士气。
The defenders steel themselves and fight with renewed vigor. Their flagging morale is centering on a single clear goal: 15 minutes, hold the line. That's it.

房间另一头，一抹黑色跃过远端的掩体，交战双方的先锋已接近到几乎能互相看清对方的表情。
At the far end of the room, a Manus soldier leaps out of cover. Both sides are close enough to make out each other's expressions.

格鲁托夫：保持移动，不要被他们的主力部队拖住。
Krutov: They're stalling for time. Push through.

格鲁托夫：我们有人数优势，他们不可能守住每一道防线，第二和第三队，你们去找出守备薄弱的部分！
Krutov: We have numbers on them. They won't hold. Units Two and Three, probe for any weak points in their defenses.

重塑士兵Ⅰ：是。
Manus Soldier I: Yes, sir.

格鲁托夫的士兵以远超一般神秘学家的纪律性稳步推进。
Krutov's soldiers push forward with a discipline far exceeding that of normal arcanists.

格鲁托夫：基金会已经反应过来，开始有组织抵抗了。我们推进的速度比预想中慢得多……
Krutov: We're behind schedule. These Foundation dogs have finally found their footing.

格鲁托夫：其他人在做什么？绀紫腕去哪里了？她应该继续在前线清障！
Krutov: Where is İlmek? She was supposed to be leading the vanguard. Late? Or simply proving herself useless again?

格鲁托夫：又怎么了？
Krutov: What is it now?

震撼感的到来并不在原本的战术规划中，但是格鲁托夫已经习惯了——与他合作的狂热神秘学家总是自行其是。
The tremors were not part of the plan, but Krutov has grown used to his fanatical arcanist troops acting of their own accord.

格鲁托夫：这样用神秘术到处乱轰会继续拖累我们的进攻节奏！
Krutov: Whose arcane skill was that? Imbeciles! If there can be anything said for humans, at least they can follow a plan.

恼怒的指挥官离开掩体，马上有两名术士靠近他，为他展开屏障。
He storms out of cover, two arcanists leaping with him to serve as his bodyguards.

格鲁托夫走到靠近外围走廊的位置，打算亲眼看看震撼的来源。
Krutov arrives at the outermost corridor, planning to find the source of the tremors for himself.

格鲁托夫：到底又是谁——
Krutov: Whoever's doing this, I will—

格鲁托夫：——！
Krutov: ...!

粗壮的枝条如巨人的手指一般，穿透了委员会大楼的外墙。
A thick branch smashes through the Committee Building's wall.

士兵Ⅰ：这、这是——
Soldier I: Wha—huh?

槲寄生：诸位，不要害怕这些枝条与荆棘，它们会为你们提供掩护。
Druvis III: No need to be alarmed, soldiers. This thicket will provide you cover.

J：来吧，大兵们，来体验一下和神秘学家并肩作战！——等等，虚构集？你一个文职人员跟上来干嘛？
J: We figured you guys could use a couple arcanists on your side. Pretty sweet, huh? Hold on. Recoleta? What're you doing here?

虚构集：刚开始的时候，我只是想记录下这场高尚而冲动的援助，成为它的见证者。
Recoleta: At first, I only planned to observe and record the noble deeds and heroes of this great fight.

虚构集：但现在我改变主意了。
Recoleta: But I've had a change of heart.

虚构集：我应该亲自参与这场一往无前、不顾一切的冒险！请让我协助你们！
Recoleta: I will join you in this fearless all-or-nothing adventure. I'll fight, just like everyone else. Please, let me stand by your side.

APPLe：——所有人，注意我用光线照射的方向，请尽快避开那里。
APPLe: Caution, everyone. Please clear a space around the light beam as quickly as possible.

士兵Ⅱ：光线？
Soldier II: Light beam?

士兵Ⅱ：等等，外头好像有一个大——特别大的东西直飞过来了！
Soldier II: Wait, there's something flying straight at us. Missile incoming.

士兵Ⅰ：你听到那水果说的了，动起来！
Soldier I: You heard the apple. Move it!

诺谛卡：——呜啊！
Nautika: Ah!

诺谛卡：我撞到了正确的位置吗？没有人受伤吧？！
Nautika: Did I ... Did I land right? Everyone okay?

玛尔纱：我从来——没有这么飞过，诺谛卡！
Marsha: That was unlike any transport I've ever ridden before, Nautika.

骑士从高速掠空的兴奋感中回过神来。
Marsha suppresses the momentary exhilaration of the flight and focuses her mind on the battle.

玛尔纱：窗边的人都避开了，我们配合得很好。按照我刚才和你说的办法去做，你还记得吗？
Marsha: Don't worry. No injuries. Everyone moved away from the window. Excellent timing, Mr. APPLe. Now, do you remember what I said?

诺谛卡：尽量扰乱敌人的瞄准——我还记得。
Nautika: Y-Yes, disrupt the enemy's coordination.

重塑士兵Ⅰ：是奇美拉……来自南极的奇美拉！他们说的是真的——
Manus Soldier I: A Chimera. It's the one from Antarctica. Then the rumors were true.

格鲁托夫：不要恐慌，蠢货，这个怪物不过是看起来很大而已。
Krutov: If I hear another word from you, coward, I will personally put an end to you. That beast there is nothing more than an easy target.

格鲁托夫：杀了她！
Krutov: Now take her down!

重塑之手的攻势开始受到制衡，基金会的士兵与职员纷纷夺回了有利防御阵地。
Manus Vindictae's assault begins to slow as the Foundation's troops establish strong points along their lines.

基金会安保：谢谢你们，我们从来没想过会得到如此……强大的支援。
Foundation Security: We can't thank you enough. You really saved our bacon back there.

槲寄生：大树内部有通向下层的通道，你们可以借用它来转移人员和物品。
Druvis III: There's a path down to the lower floor under this tree trunk. We can use it to move between floors behind enemy lines.

基金会安保：太好了，我们可以派人下去开辟敌人后方的战线，或者找来一些能用的武器。
Foundation Security: Excellent news. Now we can send down teams to look for weapons and ammo.

玛尔纱：说到武器，我们还从科算中心带来了不少装备。诺谛卡？
Marsha: On that subject, we've brought supplies from Laplace. Nautika?

诺谛卡：哦，对！我有很多东西可以给你们。
Nautika: Oh, right! I've got everything here.

诺谛卡从背上抖落了几大只银色的包裹，一只小铁盒随着众多包裹一同落地。
Nautika shakes several silver packages from her back. A tin box clatters to the ground alongside them.

小铁盒：咳……咳！
Tin Box: Ahem. Ahem.

安保人员捡起这只会说话的小铁盒。
He picks up the talking tin box.

基金会安保：这是什么？
Foundation Security: What's this?

小铁盒：你好——不管你是谁，这是一则预先录制的有声说明书。
Tin Box: Hi there, whoever you are, this is a prerecorded instructional message from X.

小铁盒：接下来我将快速为你们讲解这些装备的作用，首先是“半自动掩体构筑强力臂”……
Tin Box: Now, allow me to walk you through the specifications of the equipment. First up: the semi-automatic construction arm.

战斗的余震沿着建筑结构传递到了较低的楼层。
The aftershocks of the battle for the third floor reverberate through the building, shaking both the higher and lower levels.

无名者：槲寄生小姐他们已经成功支援了高层的战斗，我的伪装也完成了……该开始我的任务了。
Ms. Stranger: Ms. Druvis has checked in. They've made it upstairs and joined the fight. I'm ready to set out for my mission as well.

无名者：我会去重塑之手的占领区寻找他们进入基金会的入口，运气好的话，我能找到办法破坏它。
Ms. Stranger: I'll be going behind enemy lines in an effort to find out how they breached our security. With any luck, I might find a way to close it and isolate their forces here.

无名者：玛格丽特研究员，你留在这里——
Ms. Stranger: Researcher Marguerite, I'll leave the rest to you here.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：是的，这里终于只剩下我们两个人了。
Lorentz Butterfly: Not so fast. It's finally just the two of us. A good chance to speak face-to-face, don't you think?

同行的研究员出声打断了她的计划。
The words momentarily take her away from thoughts of the battle ahead.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：我很高兴我们不用再玩拯救世界的戏码，能够聊点正事——自加入黑盒研究小组开始，我就一直在等待着这一刻。
Lorentz Butterfly: Let's set aside this little "saving the world" matter for the moment and talk about what really matters. I've been waiting for this since the day I joined the "boon" research team.

无名者：如果你是对我与重塑之手曾经的联系有顾忌的话——
Ms. Stranger: If this is about my past with the Manus ...

洛伦兹蝴蝶：不，不。我并不在意那些。
Lorentz Butterfly: No, no. I have no interest in that.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：无名者小姐，你对“回环实验室”这个名字，有任何印象吗？
Lorentz Butterfly: Does the name "Loop Laboratory" ring a bell?

无名者：……你知道琥珀屋。
Ms. Stranger: You know about the Amber Room.

她面上褪去了一层血色。
The color drains from her face.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：第二次“暴雨”期间，芝诺与基金会秘密组建的回环实验室诞生，“琥珀屋”是它的另一个名字。
Lorentz Butterfly: The secret lab built after the second "Storm" in collaboration between Zeno and the Foundation. Yes, I know about the Amber Room.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：芝诺寻找到那些“暴雨”后身份有异的士兵，伪造了他们的“逃兵”证明，再用寂静小队将他们转移至实验室。
Lorentz Butterfly: I also know that, for years, Zeno has been tracking down soldiers reversed by the "Storm," labeling them as "deserters," and sending the Hush Troopers to ferry them to that very laboratory.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：灌体实验是否成功我们尚未得知，但回环实验室基于灌体的原理，研究出了一项新的技术——
Lorentz Butterfly: And I know that using the same theory and reasoning behind the vessel ensoulment experiment, they developed a new technique.

无名者：……趋光性定律。
Ms. Stranger: Phototaxis.

无名者：一项镌刻在受训者大脑皮层与皮肤表面的复杂神秘术，无数条咒语共同组成了名为“趋光性定律”的控制系统。
Ms. Stranger: A complex technique used to engrave a subject's skin and cerebral cortex with countless arcane incantations ...

无名者：它能够保证受训者绝对的优秀、坚定……以及忠诚。
Ms. Stranger: Enhancing their performance, resilience, and guaranteeing their unwavering loyalty.

那些快乐、恐惧、悲伤、感动，像是剧毒的违禁物，在大脑中蠢蠢欲动。
Joy, terror, pain—the emotions stir in her mind like a deadly poison.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：重视亲近之人的塞西莉，柔情的凯拉，喜爱标本的格蕾丝……基金会一直在用趋光性定律让你去扮演他人的身份。
Lorentz Butterfly: Caring Cecilie, sweet Kayla, Grace the Specimen Enthusiast ... The Foundation has always kept you moving from one identity to another.

无名者：我……曾经成为她们，利用她们的身份，做了许多……我不愿回忆的事情。
Ms. Stranger: There are many things I regret doing under those stolen names.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：你不是她们。这些年来，我一直在追踪回环实验室的动向。许多受训者都在多次转换身份后崩溃，无法继续执行任务。
Lorentz Butterfly: But somehow your true self was never lost, not like with the others. I've been keeping my eye on that laboratory and its subjects. Most collapsed under the weight of their identities. But not you.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：毫不夸张地说，你是回环实验室交给副会长康斯坦丁的，最优秀的那件“消耗品”。
Lorentz Butterfly: It's no exaggeration to say you are the laboratory's finest work, a perfect specimen for Constantine's needs.

无名者：够了，玛格丽特小姐。请直接告诉我，我身上究竟有什么你想要的。
Ms. Stranger: That's quite enough. What do you want to say?

洛伦兹蝴蝶：这些年来，我一直没能找到的，唯有这项实验成果的承载者——
Lorentz Butterfly: There's a puzzle I've been looking to solve all these years. And only someone who survived that system can give me its final piece.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：那正是你，经过重重试炼与改造的无名者小姐。
Lorentz Butterfly: You, Ms. Stranger, are the key to solving this mystery.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：让我看看你的大脑，看看它在被“暴雨”冲刷后，是否能让你重新成为你自己。
Lorentz Butterfly: I'd like to examine your brain to see whether it can rebuild your original self after the "Storm" washes it away.

静默在二者之间蔓延。本该一触即发的争执却在一声轻笑之中瓦解。
A prolonged silence. But what breaks it isn't dispute, isn't indignation or concern. It's a light, almost sarcastic chuckle.

无名者：……
Ms. Stranger: ...

无名者：我体内的趋光性定律已经不存在了。
Ms. Stranger: I'm afraid you'll be disappointed, Ms. Marguerite. The Phototaxis is no longer in me.

罕见的，极少沮丧的研究员微妙地叹了口气。
Lorentz Butterfly is caught at a rare loss.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：……这该死的好运气。我再次与谜底擦肩而过了。无名者小姐，请务必告诉我，是谁做的？
Lorentz Butterfly: Luck mocks me once again. But if that's so, then tell me: Who removed it?

无名者：阿尔卡纳。就在袭击发生时，我与祂见面的那一刻。
Ms. Stranger: Arcana. She freed me from it in our confrontation earlier.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：看来阿尔卡纳对你青睐有加。基金会真不该就这样把你召回。
Lorentz Butterfly: She does seem quite taken by you, doesn't she? Her little pet. Maybe the Foundation should have let you stay by her side.

无名者：我明白阿尔卡纳对我这么做的理由，祂想看我像失去牵引绳的狗一样乱窜，或者情绪崩溃走上绝路，以此取乐。
Ms. Stranger: It wasn't an act of kindness. She takes pleasure in the thought of me losing my mind, a mad dog off her leash, or collapsing once there's nothing left to hold me together.

无名者：所以祂没有当场把我杀死。
Ms. Stranger: She let me live only to savor my misery.

无名者：但我确实得到了自由……那在我脑海里逐渐膨胀的空洞感。
Ms. Stranger: I suppose I should feel lucky to have regained my freedom, but it has come at a price—an emptiness that gnaws at me every waking moment.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：我很好奇，你现在所做的事情，和先前受制于基金会命令的时候相比，有什么本质区别呢？
Lorentz Butterfly: So, you have your freedom, yet you're still acting as if you're under the Foundation's control. Why is that, I wonder?

无名者：我对曾经冒用的名字心怀愧疚，也憎恨基金会的政治……但我有我的选择。
Ms. Stranger: I hate what they made me do—stealing people's names and lives. The guilt of it sits in my chest. But today, my choice is my own.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：这就很有意思了。你的意思是，此刻你将基金会的利益放得比自己的生命还要高，遵循的是自身的自由意志。
Lorentz Butterfly: You are a strange one, Ms. Stranger. You mean to say you're risking your life for the Foundation by choice?

洛伦兹蝴蝶：既然观察已经不可能继续，我可以助你最快捷地完成你的选择与心愿……
Lorentz Butterfly: All the same, you've satisfied my curiosity, in a way. I suppose I should offer you something in return.

研究员微笑着递出一只飞蛾无比熟悉的面具。
She smiles and hands over a mask that Ms. Stranger is all too familiar with.

洛伦兹蝴蝶：这是我捡来的。“友情提醒”，没有了趋光性定律的保护，它有可能对你造成不可逆转的污染……与侵蚀。
Lorentz Butterfly: A friendly warning: this may corrupt you, and without the Phototaxis's protection, its effects could be permanent.

无名者：……感谢你的帮助，玛格丽特研究员。
Ms. Stranger: Your warning is appreciated, truly, Researcher Marguerite.

26  神奇对话 / An Unexpected Conversation

《纽约新闻报》节选
Excerpt from the New York Times

前日深夜，位于市郊西侧的橡木树林发生大范围火灾，由于预警不及，林中地形复杂且可燃物繁多，抢险困难，火势持续直至清晨时分才得以扑灭。
     于当日黄昏时分进入森林的十三名人员全部遇难。死亡人员中已确认身份的六人，包括韦耶豪瑟夫妇，与其交易者汤姆森·乔伊斯、雪莱·杰拉德、凯利·基思，施工负责人罗素·詹姆斯。其余人员身份均在进一步核实中。

     因近年来的木材供应风波，原本根基深重的韦耶豪瑟在前些日子宣告破产。韦耶豪瑟夫妇将基础版起死回生剂的配方，有偿捐赠与纽约玛利亚疗养院、俄亥俄州圣心疗养所与长老会医疗中心三方后，又将名下的所有林业用地对外抛售。
     火情发生当晚，韦耶豪瑟夫妇与购买者三人带领工作小组进入森林，预备开始铲除作业，随后遇难。经市警署排查与分析，火情现场的残留有神秘学家活动与少量汽油的痕迹，已可确定为人为纵火案件。更多关于犯罪嫌疑人的信息仍在进一步调查当中。

     报道记者：詹姆斯·威尔斯/多丽·约翰逊
Around midnight two days ago, a large fire broke out in the oak forest on the western outskirts. The delay of the warning and the complex terrains full of inflammables in the forest resulted in a fire that lasted a whole night till dawn and 13 deaths.
The victims entered the forest at dusk. Identities of 6 of them have been confirmed: the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers Tomson Joyce, Shelley Gerald, Kelly Keith, and the project manager Russell James. The others' are still being verified.

The Weyerhaeuser used to be a leader in the wood industry, yet it went bankrupt not long ago due to the wood supply crisis these years. After donating the formula of the Resurrection Potion (basic version) to the Maria Sanatorium in New York, the Sacred Heart Sanatorium in Ohio, and the Presbyterian Church Health Center on certain conditions, the Weyerhaeusers sold all of their forestry lands.
On the night of the fire, the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers, and the workers went into the forest to start the disafforestation project before they died. The police has found traces of arcanist's activities and a small amount of gasoline at the scene, which shows the case is an arson attack. More investigations are needed to identify the suspect.

Reporter: James Welles/Dorey Johnson

27  意见相左 / Disagreement

《纽约新闻报》节选
Excerpt from the New York Times

前日深夜，位于市郊西侧的橡木树林发生大范围火灾，由于预警不及，林中地形复杂且可燃物繁多，抢险困难，火势持续直至清晨时分才得以扑灭。
     于当日黄昏时分进入森林的十三名人员全部遇难。死亡人员中已确认身份的六人，包括韦耶豪瑟夫妇，与其交易者汤姆森·乔伊斯、雪莱·杰拉德、凯利·基思，施工负责人罗素·詹姆斯。其余人员身份均在进一步核实中。

     因近年来的木材供应风波，原本根基深重的韦耶豪瑟在前些日子宣告破产。韦耶豪瑟夫妇将基础版起死回生剂的配方，有偿捐赠与纽约玛利亚疗养院、俄亥俄州圣心疗养所与长老会医疗中心三方后，又将名下的所有林业用地对外抛售。
     火情发生当晚，韦耶豪瑟夫妇与购买者三人带领工作小组进入森林，预备开始铲除作业，随后遇难。经市警署排查与分析，火情现场的残留有神秘学家活动与少量汽油的痕迹，已可确定为人为纵火案件。更多关于犯罪嫌疑人的信息仍在进一步调查当中。

     报道记者：詹姆斯·威尔斯/多丽·约翰逊
Around midnight two days ago, a large fire broke out in the oak forest on the western outskirts. The delay of the warning and the complex terrains full of inflammables in the forest resulted in a fire that lasted a whole night till dawn and 13 deaths.
The victims entered the forest at dusk. Identities of 6 of them have been confirmed: the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers Tomson Joyce, Shelley Gerald, Kelly Keith, and the project manager Russell James. The others' are still being verified.

The Weyerhaeuser used to be a leader in the wood industry, yet it went bankrupt not long ago due to the wood supply crisis these years. After donating the formula of the Resurrection Potion (basic version) to the Maria Sanatorium in New York, the Sacred Heart Sanatorium in Ohio, and the Presbyterian Church Health Center on certain conditions, the Weyerhaeusers sold all of their forestry lands.
On the night of the fire, the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers, and the workers went into the forest to start the disafforestation project before they died. The police has found traces of arcanist's activities and a small amount of gasoline at the scene, which shows the case is an arson attack. More investigations are needed to identify the suspect.

Reporter: James Welles/Dorey Johnson

基金会走廊

Regulus dispels her optical stealth with a frown.

APPLe: Captain, I find it rather unlikely that the leader of the Manus Orchestra would simply be hiding in a room waiting for us to wander into them.

APPLe：或者，根本就没有所谓的乐团，也没有指挥。
APPLe: Assuming they have an orchestra at all and that there's someone leading it.

Regulus: Fair point, Mr. APPLe, but I'm not letting any sensible conclusions hold me back, not after all that clanging and clattering battered my poor ears on the "Free Breeze."

星锑：但一个乐团一定会有掌握节奏的指挥，不然那些听不懂人话的家伙就白白长成乐器的模样了。
Regulus: Besides, my dear chief mate, why else would they have become all musical instrument-y if there weren't some barmy conductor leading it all?

星锑：它一定就躲在重塑之手防线的后方。
Regulus: Therefore, ipso facto, Bob's your uncle, this conductor must be operating from behind enemy lines.

APPLe：可是，我们就算能找到它，那要怎么制伏对方？
APPLe: And if I may ask, Captain, what will we do if we actually find this conductor?

星锑：你忘了X送来的“无比黏口香糖发射器”了吗？
Regulus: Simple. That's when we bust out the "Gluey Gooey Gum Gun" our dear old X sent us.

APPLe：我认为这个时候我们应该快点离开，船长！

有人降落在不远处。在确认敌人所在的方向不再有威胁后，她转过头来。

红弩箭：巧了，原来是你在这儿。

Lilya: Ha! Then "they" clearly don't know how fast my brand-new Red 38 flies or who they're talking about.

红弩箭：真没想到，重塑之手捅了基金会的窝，天上还全是爱咬人的奥利图欧，真是够惹人烦的。
Lilya: So, what, we take our eyes off this place for ten minutes and the Manus are all over you? Look at all these Olitiaus buzzing like flies on a corpse. Makes my skin crawl.

红弩箭：那么，回见，欠我的人情记得还——不准用胡椒博士。
Lilya: Whatever, catch you later, rockstar. But don't forget. You owe me one. And I mean something harder than Dr. Papper.

星锑：什么？你用这么劲爆的方式登场之后，马上就要走了？难道你不和我们一起去痛揍重塑之手吗？
Regulus: That's it? You just swoop in, save the day, and fly off? Why not stick around and give these Manus stooges a bit of what for?

红弩箭：嘿，你不会是指望我在室内像台球一样撞来撞去吧？
Lilya: Oh, I will. But I'm not wasting my time charging around on foot.

红弩箭：我要去收拾委员会大楼外面的那些人。我看见重塑之手把一些大家伙抬出来了，他们也许想炮轰大楼。
Lilya: Plenty of stooges to deal with outside the Committee Building. Spotted them taking out some heavy machinery on my way in. Things are about to get wild.

红弩箭：你快回自己人那儿去，别再一个人溜达了，我不可能一直盯着你。
Lilya: You two should get moving. Let the Foundation know what's up. And no more wandering off. I don't babysit.

飞行女巫跨上新型的飞行扫帚，飞越廊柱，冲向天空。
Lilya mounts her new broom and takes off into the sky again.

星锑：——等等，你真的不愿意用胡椒博士代替酒精吗？！

副会长办公室
Vice President's Office

康斯坦丁：比预料中的要快。但即使她的中队全数抵达，也不足以扭转局势。

Z: You don't seem at all concerned. Is there something I don't know about?

火龙燃素燃烧弹从空中坠落，虽然为数不多，但也足够给重塑之手的增援部队造成混乱。
Pyrodrake Phlogiston Incendiaries rain down on the Manus. Though few in number, they're enough to throw the enemy's reserves into a panic.

Ritualist: I would have had the array ready for those Flying Witches if you idiots hadn't kept getting in my way.

飞蛾轻轻扇动翅膀，从无名者的指尖离开，安静平稳地覆在仪式术士的脸上。

玛尔纱：诺谛卡，你的位置太危险了！

诺谛卡：别担心，他们没法真正伤到我。

重塑士兵Ⅰ：该死的！

基金会安保：“超强破坏力追踪弹力球”！

Foundation Security: You've got to give them credit. Those Laplace brainiacs sure know how to build some solid toys.
