基金会安保：每次扔出去的时候都要大声喊出弹力球的名字，喊得越大声破坏力越大——这是那个小录音盒说的。
Foundation Security: The higher the volume, the greater the damage. That's what the guy in the tin box said.

士兵Ⅰ：行吧，那就当是苦中作乐好了。给我也来一个。
Soldier I: Alright, can't hurt to have a little fun. Let me try one.

基金会安保与签下“军事服务合约”的士兵们躲在掩体后，继续搜寻能用的道具。
The security staff and soldiers fall back behind cover and continue making use of their new "toys."

战线的另一边，袭击的指挥官在占领的临时掩体后观察着局势。
Meanwhile, from the other end of the battleground, Krutov observes the escalating counterattack.

格鲁托夫：那个奇美拉……普通术法攻击没有效果，除非能命中她的脑袋……啧，还有个碍事的德鲁伊一直在掩护她。
Krutov: Their Chimera is immune to our incantations. Nothing a swift bullet to the head couldn't solve, if that druid weren't so incessant.

格鲁托夫：真是难缠，室内拉锯根本不适合我的部队发挥。重型火力怎么还没有就位？
Krutov: We don't have the forces for close-quarters combat. Where the hell is our heavy firepower?

将领后退一步，以求获得更好的指挥视野，同时也在为战术性撤退做准备。
He steps back a bit, attempting to get a wider field of view. His troops are on the edge of breaking. The unthinkable occurs: A tactical retreat may be necessary.

之后，他看见祂踱步而来。
But then, Arcana appears.

阿尔卡纳：……
Arcana: ...

格鲁托夫：——！
Krutov: ...!

一股难以言喻的力量令格鲁托夫不自觉地退避一旁。
An indescribable force compels Krutov to step aside.

黑与白的对抗暂停，一切都笼罩在不可理喻的离奇默契之中。
The battle of black and white comes to an eerie pause. All present seem to be paralyzed with awe and terror.

阿尔卡纳：多么宏伟而美丽的造物，诺谛卡，我喜欢你新的身躯——即使它是你窃取的力量。
Arcana: It is a wondrous form thou hast claimed, Nautika. I cannot help but behold thy beauty with some awe.

阿尔卡纳：这倒让我想起……我们是不是共享了同一个“新生的纪念日”？你还适应这份新的力量吗？
Arcana: Did we not return to this world upon the same blessed day? Pray tell—how fares thy second life, and the powers thou hast seized?

诺谛卡：不……我和你没有任何关系，阿尔卡纳。
Nautika: My-My life has nothing to do with you, Arcana.

阿尔卡纳的思维奇异地翻转，注意力毫无预兆地从诺谛卡身上跳走。
Arcana's focus shifts, not dismissively, but as effortlessly as a turning wind.

阿尔卡纳：——韦耶豪瑟女士，你也在。
Arcana: Ms. Weyerhaeuser? Charming as ever. What a delight to chance upon thee here as well.

槲寄生：……
Druvis III: ...

阿尔卡纳：好久不见，是什么让你愿意保护曾经那般伤害过你的人类？
Arcana: Too long has it been e'er last we spoke. And a question hath weighed upon me since then: why protect these humans, the cause of thy family's ruin?

祂向前一步。
She takes a step closer.

诺谛卡：我会拦住祂！
Nautika: Leave her to me!

巨兽张开羽翼，扑向阿尔卡纳，她的身旁环绕着德鲁伊唤起的尖锐荆棘。
Nautika spreads her wings and lunges at Arcana.

阿尔卡纳站在原地，像一个观看舞台表演的观众，丝毫不担心自己会被任何演绎冲击。
Yet Arcana stands unmoved and unafraid, as if she is only watching a performance.

阿尔卡纳：嘘——安静。
Arcana: Hush, child.

诺谛卡：……！
Nautika: ...!

德鲁伊的荆棘枯萎，巨兽的身躯也在一瞬间失去了全部的力量。诺谛卡直直倒在了大理石地面上。
Druvis's thorns wither away, and Nautika comes crashing to the marble floor below.

她的身体不再轻盈，只能勉强活动人形的肢体，无力驱动下肢再迈出哪怕一步。
Her entire body feels heavy, as if she had been drained of all strength. With effort, she manages to move her upper half, but her lower limbs are motionless.

诺谛卡：我……站不起来了。
Nautika: I ... I can't get up.

玛尔纱：不应该……这个感觉……
Marsha: Something's wrong.

一阵前所未有的高压与眩晕砸向在场的神秘学家。他们如同被浸入海底，阳光和空气变得触不可及。
Each and every arcanist feels it, a sensation like having the air ripped from their lungs, like being pulled down to the ocean floor, at once both pressure and suffocation.

在他们身后，那株攀附在大楼一侧的巨树似乎再也无法支撑自身的重量，发出惊人的破碎声后断裂倒下。
The massive tree behind them begins to buckle and shake until it comes crashing down.

槲寄生：不！树干里还有……其他人……
Druvis III: There are still people under the tree.

虚构集：唔，唔……等等，我的冒险……
Recoleta: No. My adventure ...

部分神秘学家陷入了更严重的昏迷。
Arcanists across the battlefield begin to waver and collapse into unconsciousness.

在厅堂的另一端，阿尔卡纳仍旧留在原地，注视着祂自己创造的混乱舞台。
Arcana stands at the other end of the long hall, wearing a satisfied expression, like an artist overlooking their work.

阿尔卡纳：起。
Arcana: Rise.

诺谛卡：……！
Nautika: ...!

奇美拉庞大的身躯轻而易举地被凭空托起，仿佛她只是一朵轻柔的棉花。
She is suddenly pulled up into the air as if weightless.

诺谛卡：怎么会，我被举起来了……
Nautika: Something's picking me up.

阿尔卡纳：落。
Arcana: Fall.

滞空的巨兽向下一沉，擦着满是残骸的石砖地面缓慢地、不可遏制地向楼层一侧滑动。
She sinks back down only to be pulled to one side, her body dragging against the rubble-strewn floor.

诺谛卡：不……不不——我控制不了——！
Nautika: N-No! I can't stop!

片刻后，诺谛卡连带途经的所有人员、物品与枯枝败叶，一同被扫出了高楼。
Then, with a flick of her wrist, Arcana sends Nautika and all the people, objects, and foliage in her path hurtling out of the building.

……
...

阿尔卡纳：这下清净多了。
Arcana: At last, some peace and quiet.

29  理性的狂热 / Rational Passion

“仿神体”科研立项请愿表
"No Longer Poles Apart" Research Project Proposal

尊敬的且神奇的诺谛卡女士，你好！拉普拉斯科算中心对您的新躯体异常感兴趣，此次来函，是希望您能同意并配合我们进行实验和研究。当然，我们始终保持积极的人文关怀和专业的科研伦理，因此您可以在您愿意配合的科研项目前打“√”。以下是针对“仿神体”的科研立项清单：

√ 1.10月13日-12月20日 主题方向：“仿神体”生物力场与神秘学细胞相容性研究
     项目：抽血（需要较多血液样本，怕痛的话您有权拒绝）、化验、苦目糖敏感性测试、铁人三项对比研究（人类作为对照组）、表皮细胞抗压测试、未知粘液脱氧核糖核酸测序……

√ 2.11月4日-1月29日 主题方向：基于“仿神体”重塑肉身的自我同一性及特殊社会化的心理学研究
     主题：在阶梯会议室讲座中进行社交焦虑测试、在演说厅进行回避-依恋关系测试（可以分享您的神话考古学）、在乌卢鲁运动场内进行肢体协调测试、情绪和心理临床测试……

√ 3.12月15日-1月30日 主题方向：光子动态全息礼服与“仿神体”空间适配性研究
     内容：人类形态光子全息礼服设计研究、个人审美取向研究（我们很感兴趣！）、光子礼服材质与粘液及骸骨等适配性研究、细胞感光光谱判定范围研究、身高测量、体重测量、臂（翼？）展测量……
Dear and wonderful Ms. Nautika, Laplace Scientific Computing Center has taken a keen interest in your new body. We are writing in hopes that you will agree to participate in our experiments and research. Rest assured, we remain fully committed to humanitarian care and professional research ethics—which is why you are welcome to check off only the projects you are willing to take part in. The research projects regarding "No Longer Poles Apart" are listed below:

√1. October 13 - December 20, Research Theme: Biotic Force Field and Arcane Cell Compatibility in "No Longer Poles Apart"
     Projects: Blood draw (a significant volume of blood samples is required—you are welcome to decline if you are sensitive to pain), lab analysis, Picrasma Candy sensitivity test, triathlon comparative study (humans as control group), epidermal cell stress test, unknown mucus DNA sequencing ...

√2. November 4 - January 29, Research Theme: Psychological Study of Self-Identity and Special Socialization Based on the Reconstructed Body of "No Longer Poles Apart"
     Theme: Social anxiety assessment in the tiered lecture hall, avoidance-attachment relationship test in the auditorium (you are welcome to share your mythological archaeology), physical coordination test in Uluru Stadium, emotional and psychological clinical evaluation ...

√3. December 15 - January 30, Research Theme: Spatial Compatibility of the Photon Dynamic Holographic Dress with "No Longer Poles Apart"
     Content: Human-form photon holographic dress design study, personal aesthetic preference research (we're very curious!), compatibility study of photon dress materials with mucus and bone, cell photosensitive spectrum determination range study, height measurement, weight measurement, arm—or wing—span measurement ...

30  在门后 · 1/2 / Behind the Door · 1/2

在门后
Behind the Door

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

第一防线学校广场
Square, SPDM

重塑·信徒：ㄢ——！
Manus Believer: ㄢ!

梅蕾尔飞身向前扑倒，神秘术辉光闪过后，小巧的月相招潮蟹成功从锤击中逃出生天。
Merel's soaring figure lunges forward. There's a flash of an arcane skill, then a nimble Moon-Eyed Fiddler dodges an incoming strike.

梅蕾尔：咔……！
Merel: Kaarrr!

招潮蟹翻滚着撞开了瞭望塔大门。
The rolling fiddler crab crashes into the watchtower's door, pushing it open.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

梅蕾尔：咔咔……嘶……<“按照一般避难程序，校园危急情况发生时，无课程安排的教员需集中于中央瞭望塔以供调遣……”>
Merel: Kaarr, hiss ... <Protocol says, in case of a campus-wide emergency, instructors without other duties are to assemble at the central watchtower and wait for deployment ...>

迎接访客的是一个窒闷、空旷的圆形大厅。
A rotunda, somehow both stifling and spacious, welcomes her.

梅蕾尔：嘶嘶？<没有人……教员们和校园安保都到哪里去了？>
Merel: Hiss? <But it's empty. No instructors. No security. Where did everyone go?>

梅雷尔：现状没有给她更多的思考时间，变形术瞬间解除，梅蕾尔三两下从地上爬起来就要去关瞭望塔大门。
She has no time to ponder the question further. The moment she dispels her transformation, Merel rises to her feet and goes to close the door.

梅蕾尔：哇啊！
Merel: Ah!

蛮力撞开了未能及时合拢的门缝，没来得及躲闪的梅蕾尔被掀翻在地。
But before she can shut it, something powerful shoves it back open, knocking her to the ground.

信徒撞入门内，举起沉重的巨锤。
A heavy hammer swings toward her.

重塑·信徒：——ㄠ！
Manus Believer: ㄠ!

梅蕾尔：不，来不及——
Merel: No way out.

叶尔莫莱：人数悬殊的情况？当然是跑了。
Yermolai: If you're outnumbered? You run. Simple as that.

叶尔莫莱：别像个傻子一样逞英雄，还轮不到你当救世主。
Yermolai: No heroics. Dead soldiers don't win wars.

叶尔莫莱：隐身的把戏可以用用，但不要太相信神秘术，枪械才是你最重要的伙伴，明白吗？
Yermolai: Invisibility incantations might help, but arcanum isn't reliable. Guns never fail. Burn that into your skull.

在巨锤落下的前一刻，梅蕾尔从原地翻滚躲开。
Merel rolls out of the path of the hammer just as it slams down.

神秘术辉光藏起了身形，只留上膛的声音回响在圆形大厅。
Arcane radiance conceals her body. All that's left is her voice echoing in the rotunda.

梅蕾尔：……吃子弹吧！
Merel: Let's see how you like this.

“砰砰砰——”
*bang*

第一匣子弹很快被清空。
She empties her magazine.

重塑·信徒：ㄞ！
Manus Believer: ㄞ!

枪声与敌人的惨叫施与梅蕾尔短暂的镇静。然后是第二个弹匣被推入弹匣井。
Gunshots. Screams. A brief pause on both sides. Then she reloads.

重塑·信徒：ㄤ……？
Manus Believer: ㄤ?

聚集在一起的信徒被粗暴地撞开，从敌人伤处涌出的脓血勾勒出了少女的形体，光学神秘术也随之被解除。
The cluster of believers falls back. Black ooze and blood splatter reveal Merel's silhouette even before her optical illusion fades.

她冲向楼梯，面朝敌人，后退着向二层撤退。
She charges toward the stairs and turns to face her enemies. They're retreating upstairs.

梅蕾尔：冲上二层，锁上门，这样应该能撑得更久一些……肯定可以！
Merel: Second floor. There's a door. I can lock them out. Should buy a little time.

梅蕾尔：希望秩序协助小组的万能钥匙能管用……
Merel: I just hope the security team's master key works here.

“砰砰——”
*bang-bang*

梅蕾尔朝面前点射两枪，为自己争取了几秒钟的喘息时间，随后转身冲向二楼的防爆门。
Merel fires two shots straight ahead in an attempt to buy herself a few seconds of respite, then turns and runs toward the second floor's blast door.

钥匙卡在锁里，声音听起来并不悦耳。
The key sticks in the lock with a frustrating clink.

梅蕾尔：求求你，不要在这个关头——
Merel: Come on. Don't do this to me.

重塑·信徒：ㄠ！
Manus Believer: ㄠ!

梅蕾尔：——！
Merel: ...!

她反射性抬手护住头脸，却发现敌人的神秘术并未落在自己身上，只有一片被冲击力打弯的方形金属网在地上乒乓作响。
She reflexively shields her face with her hand only to find that her foe's arcane skill hasn't hit her. A square of metal mesh, curved by the impact, rocks back and forth on the ground.

梅蕾尔：铁网……？哪里来的？
Merel: Wh-Where did that come from?

艾米莉亚：这儿！
Emilia: Heads up!

女孩的脑袋从通风管出口探出来，手中挥舞着的术杖尚未散去辉光。
A young girl pokes her head out from the vent. Radiance still lingers from the staff in her hand.

艾米莉亚：看来我的咒语没有失误。
Emilia: Seems like I did the incantation right.

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚，你怎么在这里？！
Merel: Emilia? What on earth are you doing here?

艾米莉亚：那你又为什么在这里？
Emilia: Shouldn't I be asking you that?

女孩反问了一个问题，也不等对方回答，她就轻巧地从通风管里翻了下来。
Though she answers the question with one of her own, she exits the vent rather than waiting for a response.

梅蕾尔：等等——你别出来！
Merel: No, wait. Just stay right there.

艾米莉亚：它们数量好多！……是不是整个学校的怪物都靠到这边来了？
Emilia: There are so many of them. Why are they gathering around like this?

特意将敌人引向瞭望塔的毕业生不知道如何回话。
Regret wells up in Merel's throat.

她与在校生被步步紧逼，冰冷的防爆门在沉默地嘲笑着她们。
The blast door feels like the jaws of a trap. They're cornered now.

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚，我托着你回通风管里！
Merel: Hop on, Emilia! I'll get you back into the air vent.

艾米莉亚：可是你钻不进去。
Emilia: What about you? You won't fit in there.

梅蕾尔：你别管我呀！
Merel: I'll find a way.

“咔——”
*click*

身后的防爆门发出一声悦耳的脆响。
The sound of salvation comes from the door behind them.

梅蕾尔：门开了？
Merel: Wait. It's open?

眼下的情形让她来不及多想。
She doesn't have time to consider the answer.

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚，我们一起推门！
Merel: Emilia, help me open this door.

卢卡斯：嘿！
Lucas: Hey!

门后出现了一个眼熟的小男孩。他从内侧帮着拉开了防爆门，一张稚嫩的脸涨得通红。
Lucas's youthful face flushes red with effort as he pulls on the door.

卢卡斯：快进来！
Lucas: Quick! Get in.

三人合力将追兵隔绝在了门外，直到自动落锁的“咔哒”声响起，三人才暂时松了一口气。
The three of them shut their pursuers out. Only when the lock clicks into place do they let out a sigh of relief.

中央瞭望塔·二层
Second Floor, Central Watchtower

梅蕾尔：呼……
Merel: *pant*

梅蕾尔直起身子，摆出难得的严肃表情。
Merel straightens up and turns to the young children, her saviors, adopting an uncharacteristically serious expression.

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚，卢卡斯，你们怎么会在这里？
Merel: Lucas, Emilia, what are you two doing out here?

艾米莉亚：警报响起来的时候我们还在禁闭室里，后来不知道什么东西爆炸了，墙被炸出一个洞……我们就自己逃出来了。
Emilia: They put us in detention, remember? Then the alarm went off, and an explosion blew a hole through the wall, so we ran.

她扭过头，仿佛即便是在这种情况下，擅自逃离禁闭室仍然是一件会被批评的事。
Merel stumbles over her thoughts and looks away. She can hardly criticize them for running to safety.

梅蕾尔：这……对不起，害你们被关禁闭……
Merel: Sorry. You wouldn't have been left in there if it weren't for me.

艾米莉亚：哦，那件事……没关系，确实是我们触犯校规了。
Emilia: Oh. That wasn't your fault. We broke the rules, after all.

艾米莉亚：我们本来以为中央瞭望塔会比较安全，所以躲了进来，没想到反而被困在了这里……
Emilia: We knew the central tower was where people were supposed to go, so we headed here. Looks like that was a mistake.

卢卡斯：从禁闭室过来的路上我们也碰到了一些落单的怪物……我现在是知道学校外面有多危险了。
Lucas: We ran into a whole load of weird monsters on the way here. Guess what the instructor said about the dangers outside wasn't just to scare us after all.

卢卡斯：你又是从哪里来的，梅蕾尔姐姐？
Lucas: But where is everyone, miss?

梅蕾尔：图书馆。现在其他学生都在那里。
Merel: The library. That's where the instructors sent everyone. I just came from there.

卢卡斯猛地跳了起来。
Lucas leaps to his feet.

卢卡斯：——艾米莉亚，我就说你完全搞错了，我们一开始就应该往图书馆跑！
Lucas: See, Emilia? I told you we should've gone to the library.

艾米莉亚：照理来说安保都在瞭望塔值守，我怎么知道这儿一个人都没有！
Emilia: There's always security here at the tower. How was I supposed to know they'd all be gone?

梅蕾尔：停，你们两个，现在不是吵架的时候。
Merel: Alright, you two, now's not the time.

梅蕾尔：我要去塔顶取一个很重要的东西，然后会带着你们一起回图书馆。
Merel: I'll get you to the library. But first, there's something important I need to get from upstairs.

梅蕾尔：你们俩先在通风管道里躲着，然后……
Merel: You two get back in the vent and wait.

她低着头思索如何带着两个孩子从大厅满满当当的敌人中突围，思路突然被女孩稚嫩而坚决的声音打断。
She looks down in thought, attempting to devise some plan to get these kids from here to safety, but her thoughts are quickly interrupted.

艾米莉亚：为什么不让我们和你一起去拿那个“很重要的东西”呢？
Emilia: What do you mean? Can't we go with you?

梅蕾尔：这……太危险了。
Merel: Well, it ... it's too dangerous.

艾米莉亚：正因危险才要一起。
Emilia: All the more reason to not split up.

卢卡斯：没错，而且我比艾米莉亚还要厉害一点——我可不只是会书本上教的那些没用的神秘术。
Lucas: She's right. And trust me. You're gonna need us. I know all kinds of stuff they don't teach in class.

梅蕾尔：我说了——不行！我在学校外面也遇见过那些家伙……它们可不在乎你是不是学生。
Merel: No. Please, both of you listen. I've fought those monsters before. They don't care how smart you are or how many incantations you know.

梅蕾尔：看到领头那家伙的锤子了吗？我曾亲眼看见有士兵被砸成……
Merel: See that hammer that guy's holding? I've seen it smash trained soldiers to pieces.

点到即止的话成功震慑了未出过笼的幼鸽，两名学生怔怔地望着她，过了许久才有人打破沉默。
The young, caged doves stare at her blankly for a long moment.

艾米莉亚：……可是你要怎么上去？我们之前也想到塔上去，但除大门外，每扇门都锁得严严实实的。
Emilia: But how are you going to get up there? Every door is locked except the main one. We've checked.

卢卡斯：所以我们才爬到通风管道里藏着的。
Lucas: That's why we ended up hiding in the vent.

梅蕾尔：我有秩序协助小组的万能钥匙。
Merel: I've got a master key.

艾米莉亚：那东西连二楼的门都打不开，我都看见了。
Emilia: But it didn't look like it worked on that door you tried it on.

梅蕾尔：……那我可以用变形术从通风管走。拿到很重要的东西以后，从内侧一扇扇开门走下来。
Merel: Well, then I'll shapeshift and get to the top through the vent. Once I've got what I need, I'll open the door from the inside and take the stairs.

艾米莉亚：管道被碎石堵死了一部分，连三楼都上不去。
Emilia: That won't work. The vent was blocked in the explosions. You can't get past the second floor. We've tried.

卢卡斯：而且我试过了，这种防爆门从内侧是没法打开的。
Lucas: And the blast doors won't open from the inside.

梅蕾尔：刚刚不是你开的门吗？
Merel: What do you mean? Didn't you just open that one for me?

卢卡斯：不是呀，难道不是你从外面打开的门吗？
Lucas: No. I thought you opened it.

艾米莉亚：我都说了，她那把钥匙根本打不开防爆门！
Emilia: I just told you. Her key doesn't work.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

梅蕾尔：那是……谁开的门？
Merel: Then who did open it?

？？？
???

方位感已然失陷。
All sense of direction is lost.

无数尘埃在空间中盘桓迁移。

咒语诵出。尖锐的嗡鸣声像一条尖牙的蠕虫——刺破眼球，钻入大脑，一路噬咬着柔软的血肉。
An incantation is chanted. The piercing hum bores through her eyeballs and into her brain like a fanged worm, devouring the tender flesh in its path.

四肢的骨骼微微颤抖，她几乎能听见自己身体正在发出声音，正如那些劣化的信徒。
Her limbs tremble, as if her own body were joining the sickening orchestra.

The final note plays, and in that instant, she faintly hears a prolonged wail of agony coming from the abyss.

这声鸣响被一阵如风的力量轻柔环绕着，向遥远时空的深处退去。
A force wraps around the sound like a gentle cover before whisking it away into the recesses of a far-off time.

The incantation worked. She knows she's succeeded. Yet her success will come at a price.

失去趋光性定律的控制，她根本没有力量抵抗面具的试炼。
Without control over the Phototaxis, she is powerless to resist the mask's trial.

无名者：不……不要是……现在——不！
Ms. Stranger: No. Don't do this to me. No!

无名者：我不想……变成怪物……不要……
Ms. Stranger: I don't ... want to ... go ...

无名者：好不容易才……丢下了那些名字，摆脱了……控制……
Ms. Stranger: No more names ... No more orders ...

无名者：我还没有……真正地——ㄜ、活……活过……
Ms. Stranger: I've ... never ... really lived ... ㄜ ...

无名者：我……不、ㄨ——
Ms. Stranger: I ... No ... ㄨ ...

无名者：嗬——嗬——！！咳咳……
Ms. Stranger: Harrrgh! *cough*

无名者：呕——
Ms. Stranger: *vomit*

无名者：我……还不想死……
Ms. Stranger: I want to live.

无名者：我要……离开这里……我……
Ms. Stranger: To leave here.

无名者：……我绝不会回去。
Ms. Stranger: To never, never come back.

红弩箭：这是闹哪出？我还没想过要靠这双腿上战场。
Lilya: Fighting on foot? Ugh. You gotta be kidding me.

飞行女巫看着面前的飞行扫帚，已经接受自己无力回天的事实。
Lilya gives a disheartened look over to her inert broom. She won't be returning to the sky for now.

但她仍然弄不明白，为什么不久前红色38的神秘学引擎会哑然熄火，连同自己的神秘术也完全失效。
But she can't make clear sense of why. It seemed that her Red 38's arcane engine stalled all at once and for no clear reason. The conclusion only comes to her as she realizes she's lost her arcane skills as well.

红弩箭：……我只能先把你留在这里了，伙计，但愿你不要被重塑之手捡走。
Lilya: Looks like I need to leave you here, buddy. Better hope the Manus don't snatch you up.

红弩箭：嗯？
Lilya: Hm?

还好，她还有一把能够防身的手枪。
But she still has a pistol.

红弩箭：谁在那儿？
Lilya: Who's there?

无名者：……
Ms. Stranger: ...

来者的模样逐渐清晰——她慢慢走近，走到足够红弩箭认清面容的距离。
A figure staggers into view, only noticeable when she's come close enough for Lilya to make out her face.

无名者：请……帮我……
Ms. Stranger: Help, help.

红弩箭：——是你？！
Lilya: It's you! You—

红弩箭接住向前摔倒的“间谍”。
She catches Ms. Stranger as she sinks to her knees.

红弩箭：你身上的这些黏糊糊的东西……你去干什么了？
Lilya: You're all covered in slime. What the hell happened?

无名者：通道……重塑之手进入基金会的通道……已经关闭。
Ms. Stranger: The portal ... The Manus's portal is closed.

无名者：我的任务……完成。
Ms. Stranger: Mission complete.

37  护身符 / Protection Amulet

护身符
Amulets

一扇门打开。
A door opens.

梅蕾尔：呼……
Merel: *pant*

紧接着的是又一扇门——它同样为攀爬高塔者开启。一切顺利得有如神助。
Then another, as if by divine intervention.

中央瞭望塔·四层
Fourth Floor, Central Watchtower

艾米莉亚：这究竟是怎么回事？就好像有隐形人在帮我们打开这些门一样……
Emilia: It's been a little too easy. Don't you think? It's like someone's walking ahead and unlocking all the doors for us.

回应她的只有前路上一扇扇打开的门，以及空荡的回音。
As if by way of reply, the next door opens to greet them.

卢卡斯不安地用手肘撞了撞艾米莉亚，凑到她的耳边。
Clearly unsettled, Lucas huddles up to Emilia to whisper into her ear.

卢卡斯：……中央瞭望塔不会有鬼魂吧？
Lucas: You think it's ghosts?

艾米莉亚：别、别瞎说。就算真有恶鬼，也该在禁闭室里才对……
Emilia: Ah! D-Don't be ridiculous. And even if there were ghosts, they'd obviously be haunting the detention room.

梅蕾尔：学校里哪有什么恶鬼，每年春分学校不都会请除灵师来检查吗？
Merel: It couldn't be. The school hires exorcists for a full inspection every vernal equinox. Didn't you know that?

安慰的话并没有起到作用，眼前两个不过六年级的孩子仍寒颤不止。
The attempt to calm their fears falls flat. Assurances of safety do little in the face of the crisis around them.

梅蕾尔：好吧，让我看看能不能给你们这两个胆小鬼找点护身符。
Merel: But if you're still worried, I've got just the thing for you two.

梅蕾尔伸手到口袋里掏了掏。
She reaches into her pocket.

梅蕾尔：收好，一人一个。
Merel: Here. Keep these close.

冷硬的金属静静躺在孩子们柔软的掌心。
Cold, hard metal sits in their palms.

艾米莉亚：……你刚刚说，要给我们找护身符的。
Emilia: I thought you were going to give us a talisman or an amulet to keep the ghosts away.

梅蕾尔：这怎么不算呢？
Merel: Well, these are pretty much the same thing.

梅蕾尔：卢卡斯手里的这颗没有杀伤性，但爆炸后可以触发一次光学神秘术，关键时刻可以让人隐身。
Merel: Lucas, yours has got an optical incantation that can make you invisible.

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚的这颗扔出去可以引发小规模爆炸。怎么样，都是在学校里从没接触过的东西吧！
Merel: But yours, Emilia, is a real-live grenade. Throw it out, and it'll set off a small explosion, understand? I bet the instructors never let you try these before. Pretty cool, right?

卢卡斯：为什么只有艾米莉亚的能爆炸？
Lucas: Yeah, sure, but why does she get the real thing?

梅蕾尔：因为艾米莉亚更擅长做计划，对吗？如果只剩最后一次机会，我相信艾米莉亚会知道怎么做选择的。
Merel: Because as far as I've seen, Emilia thinks before she acts. I can trust her to use it responsibly.

梅蕾尔：而你——卢卡斯，你肯定一着急就会把手雷扔出去！
Merel: You, on the other hand, would probably lob it out the first chance you got.

卢卡斯：我、我才不会！
Lucas: I would not.

艾米莉亚：我会在恰当的时机用它的，我保证。
Emilia: I'll be careful. I promise.

女孩轻声回复着，像是在对自己说话。
Her voice is so low it's as if she's speaking to herself.

梅蕾尔的手在两个孩子的发顶轻拍了两下，领头踏上通向更上一层的台阶。
Merel ruffles the hair of her two young friends and then turns to make for the stairs.

梅蕾尔：继续向上走吧。
Merel: Alright then. Let's keep moving.

阶梯盘旋向上，楼梯间相似的装潢模糊了旅者们的感官认知，将这场冒险的时间拉得极长。
The staircase's spiral rise blurs the trio's perception. It feels as if each turn should be the last, and yet it continues onward until the climb becomes almost excruciating.

艾米莉亚：上面就是顶层了。
Emilia: The top floor is right above us.

艾米莉亚忧心忡忡地看向窗外。狭窄的窗户或许能抵挡入侵者，但无法阻止目光穿行于建筑内外。
Emilia pauses before the window ahead, perhaps too narrow for an intruder to get through, but wide enough to make them visible to enemy eyes.

艾米莉亚：……！
Emilia: ...!

梅蕾尔：小心！
Merel: Watch out!

她踉跄了两步，多亏了毕业生及时伸出的手才不至于向后栽倒。
She stumbles back, but Merel's outstretched hand grabs hers before she can tumble backward.

卢卡斯：怎么了，艾米莉亚？你在发抖……
Lucas: Emilia? What is it? You're shaking.

艾米莉亚：我……
Emilia: I ...

艾米莉亚：窗外……
Emilia: Look. Over there.

坚不可摧的高墙正从顶部开始熔化。血迹、锈痕突兀地侵染了洁白的壁垒。火星从石料上飞溅而下，引燃了草地。
The exterior walls of the SPDM have melted like candlewax. Bloodstains and rust mar the once-pristine, indestructible barriers. Sparks dance along the ground, igniting the grass into growing flames.

第一防线学校的一部分，已经暴露在了“暴雨”中。时代的异象无差别地扭曲了建筑。
The "Storm" is crossing over the now-unprotected campus. Clashing eras warp the outer buildings as the architecture shifts rapidly between different shapes and styles.

梅蕾尔：……会没事的。
Merel: We'll be okay.

梅蕾尔：维尔汀……还有很多人一定都在努力。我们都会没事的。
Merel: Vertin's doing everything she can. So, we've got to do the same.

忐忑的心跳卡在嗓子眼，梅蕾尔用指腹触碰了一下紧锁的门。
Her pounding heart feels as if it is lodged in her throat as she taps on the locked door.

梅蕾尔：是你们在帮我吗，维尔汀，十四行诗……？
Merel: Vertin, Sonetto, is this you helping us?

梅蕾尔：我不知道这是怎么做到的……但如果可以的话，请再帮我最后一次……
Merel: I don't know how you're doing this. But if it really is you, then please, we need your help one last time.

仿佛回应她的许愿一般，门应声而开。门后，无光的空间恍若不可预知的黑洞。
As if answering her plea, the door opens. Behind it lies darkness—an unpredictable black hole.

卢卡斯：门……又打开了。
Lucas: The door's open, again.

梅蕾尔抓紧了孩子们的手。
Merel grasps the children's hands, trying to mask the trembling in her own.

梅蕾尔：我们要找的东西，就在门的后面。
Merel: The thing we're after should be right behind it.

维尔汀：……这是我能看到的最后一个结。
Vertin: That was the last knot.

十四行诗：如果结已经解开，说明梅蕾尔顺利进入顶层平台了？
Sonetto: If all the knots are undone, will Merel be able to get to the top floor safely?

堆积在半路的金色丝线猛地扎进了门后的黑暗中。
A jumble of golden thread that's accumulated halfway in pushes through to the darkness beyond the door.

维尔汀：我想是的。
Vertin: I think so.

十四行诗：她一定能把气象鸽带回图书馆的，我们也得及时赶去会合才行。
Sonetto: Then she'll be able to get the Dove to the library. I know she will. Let's head back and meet her there.

十四行诗：不过……我们暂时还没有离开此地的线索。
Sonetto: But first, we need to find a way out.

十四行诗：……司辰？
Sonetto: Timekeeper?

她这才发现搭档失去视力的双眼紧紧盯着另一个方向。
Before Sonetto, a pair of sightless eyes stare off in another direction.

维尔汀：我看着的方向是哪里，十四行诗？
Vertin: Which way am I facing, Sonetto? What's in this direction?

十四行诗：是一扇门，要打开它吗，司辰？
Sonetto: It's a door. Shall I open it?

维尔汀：嗯，我想知道门后有什么。
Vertin: Yes, please. And tell me what's behind it.

十四行诗：……！
Sonetto: ...!

引路人的脚步陡然停下。
She comes to a sudden halt.

维尔汀：怎么了，十四行诗？
Vertin: What do you see?

十四行诗：门的另一边……不是学校的瞭望塔。我没认错的话，这里是……
Sonetto: It opens into another space. If I'm not mistaken ...

十四行诗：委员会大楼的走廊。
Sonetto: It's the Committee Building's hallway.

维尔汀：也就是说，基金会总部居然也被囊括在“奇迹”的迷宫中……
Vertin: Then this maze can lead us to Foundation headquarters.

视线中出现了更多金线，但它们没有如同梅蕾尔的线那般闪耀，若有似无，如漂浮不定的命运般若隐若现。
More golden threads sprawl out, but none glow like Merel's did. These new lines are faint, as if drifting between the destined and the uncertain.

混沌的黑暗在追逐着它们，让它们原本各行其是的流向缠绕在了一起，而黑暗也转瞬间到了眼前。
Something in this chaotic darkness is pursuing the strings, causing them to tangle along their original paths. Then the darkness takes shape.

？？？：……
???: ...

维尔汀：——！
Vertin: ...!

黑暗穿身而过，在某个瞬间掠走了喉间的空气，让大脑陷入了数秒的窒息。
The shadow flickers. Each time it appears, it feels as if it siphons the air from the lungs.

维尔汀：这是……阿尔卡纳。
Vertin: It's Arcana. She's here.

十四行诗：——阿尔卡纳？！
Sonetto: Arcana?

十四行诗看了一眼空无一人的走廊——她仍然无法看见任何人。
Sonetto can't see anything in the empty hall around them.

十四行诗：祂出现在了学校吗？还是……出现在了基金会总部？
Sonetto: You mean, at the school? Or at headquarters?

维尔汀：祂在基金会总部的委员会大楼里——我知道援军为什么迟迟未到了。
Vertin: At headquarters, inside the Committee Building. I think I know why the Foundation never sent more reinforcements to the school.

38  命运的一截 · 1/2 / A Peek into Fate · 1/2

命运的一截
A Peek into Fate

十四行诗：重塑之手袭击学校，只是为了将部分有经验的队伍调离，削弱基金会总部防御。
Sonetto: Then the attack on the SPDM was a diversion. They wanted to pull Foundation forces away from headquarters.

十四行诗：现在阿尔卡纳出现在基金会总部，第一防线学校正在受到“暴雨”的威胁……
Sonetto: Right now, Arcana has found her way into the Foundation, and the "Storm" is closing in on SPDM.

十四行诗：……而我们被困在这个奇怪的空间里。
Sonetto: And here we are stuck in whatever this place is, doing nothing.

十四行诗的低声自语让空无一人的走廊显得更为沉寂。
Her low muttering makes the corridor feel all the more desolate.

维尔汀：……？
Vertin: ...?

手指抚过从眼前流过的黯淡金线，它们在黑暗中缠绕出的结因这轻柔的抚摸震颤了一瞬。
Brushed by a fingertip, the golden threads begin to drift through the air. Their knotted forms tremble in the darkness.

在身体内的某个地方，一种神秘而久远的本能正在悄然复苏。
Somewhere within the body, an innate ability, mysterious and distant, is quietly waking.

维尔汀：我好像……找到了一些新的方向感。我有办法在这里做些什么。
Vertin: I think I've found a path. Let me try something.

维尔汀：十四行诗，我们现在要继续前进——向这个方向。
Vertin: Sonetto, this way. This is our way out.

十四行诗：可这是……窗外。
Sonetto: But you're pointing out the window.

双眼接收到的信息刺激着十四行诗的杏仁核。
Her mind tries in vain to make sense of what her eyes are taking in.

维尔汀：没错，我们需要准备好接受一场坠落。我们不会有事的。
Vertin: Then we need to jump out of it. Just trust me.

十四行诗：……我明白。
Sonetto: Of course.

她踮脚推开被指定的那扇窗户。委员会大楼之下，被职员精心养护的花园抬头与她对视。
She opens the window. Below them lies the garden that the Foundation so carefully tends to its trademark austere perfection.

信念与信任压过了基因中对高处的原始恐惧。
Conviction and trust conquer her innate fear of heights.

她率先爬上窗台，蹲在窗沿上，向着伙伴伸出了手。
She climbs onto the windowsill first and crouches there, then offers her hand.

十四行诗：请握住我的手，司辰，小心脚下。
Sonetto: Please take my hand. Slowly, please. Careful.

维尔汀：跳吧！
Vertin: Let's get out of here.

由“奇迹”缔造的迷宫静如死地，没有给任何生命的流动留下余地。
The labyrinth created by the "boon" is silent and void of any traces of life.

但自由落体仍让风呼啸着灌进了耳道。
But in free fall, the wind's howl still passes through the ears.

维尔汀：十四行诗……！
Vertin: Sonetto!

紧握的手不知不觉间松开，两具身躯一同坠入了黑暗。
At some point, the tightly clasped hands loosen as the two plunge into darkness.

维尔汀：……嗯？
Vertin: Mmm?

下坠停止了，身体翻转，双脚向下站立在虚空中，感官却向大脑传递着稳定感。
The fall has stopped. The body is upright, feet pointed down, no longer adrift in the void.

维尔汀：我的视力恢复了……
Vertin: My sight's restored. I can see again.

维尔汀：不，不对……
Vertin: No. This isn't right.

维尔汀：……
Vertin: ...

维尔汀：这些线，我见过它们……无数次……
Vertin: These threads, the woven net ... I've seen this before.

指腹悬停在线条之上，近得能清晰感觉到线的紧绷与颤抖——它们像能奏出音律的琴弦。
The golden threads drift against the fingertips, like the strings of an instrument waiting to be played.

维尔汀：它们是什么？我为什么能看见它们、触碰它们……
Vertin: What are they? I can touch them, even pluck them.

维尔汀：为什么是我？
Vertin: Why are they revealing themselves to me?

“你理应了解时间与命运的秘密。”
"You know the ways of time and fate."

维尔汀：……
Vertin: ...

手掌按在了几乎要被黑色洪流淹没的沙盘上。
The palm presses onto the sand table, which is nearly submerged in the black torrent.

维尔汀：……！
Vertin: ...!

黑色潮汐飞速坍缩，留下一枚不稳定的种子。
The black tide recedes, and in its wake, it leaves an unstable seed.

维尔汀：这是你留给我的谜题吗，阿尔卡纳？
Vertin: Is this another game of yours, Arcana?

线的高塔被啃食得千疮百孔，在退潮的滩涂上摇晃。
The tower of threads, gnawed full of holes, sways as if built on shifting sands.

在崩塌前一刻抬起手，接住了摇摇欲坠的命运——如同本能一般。
Right before it crumbles, the hand instinctively reaches out and catches this precarious destiny.

维尔汀：终有一天，我会得到谜题背后的真相。
Vertin: One day, I will uncover its secret.

卢卡斯：门……又打开了。
Lucas: The door's open again.

梅蕾尔：我们要找的东西，就在门的后面。
Merel: The thing we're after should be right behind it.

梅蕾尔将手按上不知为何解锁的门，轻轻推开一条缝，谨慎地向内张望。
Merel presses a hand to the somehow unlocked door and pushes it open a crack to peer inside.

梅蕾尔：——！
Merel: ...!

不由分说，梅蕾尔把孩子们推到一步之外，随后飞快地钻进打开了一条缝的门内。
Merel pushes the children back before slipping inside.

她猛地把门关上。
She pulls the door shut.

梅蕾尔：别进来！
Merel: Stay back. Don't come in.

卢卡斯：怎么了？里面有什么？
Lucas: What? What's wrong? What's in there?

梅蕾尔：你们别进来，这……不适合你们看。
Merel: It's ... It's better if you don't see.

她转身，锁上门，把孩子拦在混乱的世界外。
She turns around and locks the door, locking the children away from the sight that now confronts her.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

她终于知道学校里消失的教师和警卫都去了哪里。
At last, an explanation for the missing teachers and guards, dreadful as it may be.

梅蕾尔：老……老师——校长……
Merel: All the instructors, the guards, even the principal ...

梅蕾尔：唔——
Merel: Ugh!

她把涌上喉头的呕吐感咽了回去。
She forces back the bile rising in her throat at the sight.

这份冷静让她自己也吓了一跳。
Yet despite the carnage, she feels almost calm.

梅蕾尔：我……才不要在这种时候掉链子。
Merel: Alright, Merel. You can't mess this up.

第一防线学校在“暴雨”中最后的倚仗脱离了基座，血迹和伤痕将洁白的鸽子斜斜切分成两截。
The SPDM's last line of defense against the "Storm" has fallen from its perch. The Dove's body, split into two, lies bloodstained and battered on the floor.

梅蕾尔：中央塔的气象鸽果然也已经……没关系，还有备用的……
Merel: Shelter Dove's gone. But there should still be spares somewhere.

她蹲下身，推开一具尸体，试图在基座下找到一些完好的备用气象鸽。
She kneels and pushes a corpse aside, searching for an intact Shelter Dove.

梅蕾尔：不，不至于……我不会害怕……
Merel: Come on, now. They're only dead people. They can't hurt you.

梅蕾尔：我和卡戎一起去过更可怕的地方……这样的场面我早就有经验了……
Merel: You've seen worse. With Charon, remember? I wonder what he'd do if he were here.

她眨了眨眼，像一台机械般运转起来，将教员失去生气的躯体轻轻翻了过来。
She blinks. With mechanical movements, she turns over another instructor's lifeless body.

梅蕾尔：……！
Merel: ...!

教员女士的身下压着几只完好的备用气象鸽。
Sheltered beneath the body lie several Shelter Doves, mercifully undamaged.

梅蕾尔：福格尔女士……
Merel: Ms. Fogel ...

回忆一股脑涌进毕业生的脑子里，可当年被福格尔女士关禁闭的那些懊恼，却无论如何都回忆不起来。
Memories of her flood through Merel's mind, but one thing she refuses to do is think on the anger she once felt at her for sending her to detention all those years ago.

她颤抖着抬手擦掉了险些夺眶而出的眼泪，抱出了仅存的气象鸽。
She wipes away the tears welling in her eyes with a shaky hand, then gathers the Shelter Doves in her arms.

艾米莉亚：梅蕾尔姐姐，你找到要找的东西了吗？外面的天越来越黑了……
Emilia: Merel? Are you okay in there? It's getting dark outside.

梅蕾尔：找到了！我马上出来，你们在外面等我。
Merel: Y-Yes. I found it. Just a minute. Stay right there.

她郑重地合上了瞭望塔顶层的防爆门，如同为伟大者的坟茔添上最后一把土。
The solemn shutting of the door behind her feels like shoveling the last bit of dirt over a grave.

卢卡斯：里面……
Lucas: So, inside there ...

梅蕾尔：老师和警卫们一起为我们留下了希望。
Merel: Your teachers and the guards did everything they could to protect you. That's all you need to know.

学生们似乎从她的脸上读到了一出壮烈的悲剧。他们嗫嚅着，不知道该如何开口。
The students can read the overwhelming sorrow on her face, but they remain silent, unsure of what to say.

“暴雨”前的阴云遮蔽了天光，失去电力供应的校园宛如炼狱。怀中的“鸽子”似乎成了唯一的光源。
The clouds heralding the "Storm's" arrival have blotted out the sun, and the loss of power has plunged the school into darkness. The Doves she holds are their only source of light.

梅蕾尔：你们拿好这些。
Merel: Take these.

几只气象鸽落进了小鸽子们的手里，而梅蕾尔也让一只气象鸽栖息在了自己怀里。
She hands the Shelter Doves to the students, keeping one tucked into her own arms.

艾米莉亚：……发光的鸽子？
Emilia: Glowing doves?

卢卡斯：这就是你要找的东西吗？
Lucas: You came all this way for some dove sculptures?

梅蕾尔：没错，我们成功了。只要能把它们带回去，图书馆的同学就都能从这场“暴雨”中活下来。
Merel: Yep. Now, we need to get these to the library. They're our only chance to survive the "Storm."

事已至此，她已经顾不上那些针对学生的“暴雨”禁令。
She's given up on concealing the "Storm" from them, protocol or not.

艾米莉亚：可……万一那些怪物还堵在一楼呢？
Emilia: But what about the monsters? They could still be on the first floor.

梅蕾尔：别担心，我会想办法让你们出去的。
Merel: Leave them to me. I'll get you out safely.

梅蕾尔领着学生们回到二层，通向一层大厅的防爆门依旧如他们上楼时那般紧锁着。
Merel leads them back to the second floor. The blast door leading to the first floor is just as they'd left it—locked.

毕业生将耳朵贴在门上，门后纷乱的脚步声让她皱起了眉。
She presses an ear to the door and frowns when she hears footsteps behind it.

梅蕾尔：听好了，你们先钻进通风管道躲起来。
Merel: Listen carefully. You two hide in the air vent.

梅蕾尔：我会把门打开，把怪物都引进二楼，然后你们就从一楼的通风管口钻出去，往图书馆跑。
Merel: I'll go first and draw the monsters upstairs. You go down the vent to the ground floor and run as fast as you can to the library.

卢卡斯：图书馆……可是学校中庭也还有怪物游荡。我和艾米莉亚能活着跑回去吗……
Lucas: But what about the monsters outside? How-How can Emilia and I get past them?

恐惧让曾嚷嚷着“我也很厉害”的孩子露了怯。
Lucas's bravado disappears, and the terrified child remains.

艾米莉亚：不需要你指挥，我的耳朵能听得比你更清楚。
Emilia: I know exactly what to do.

艾米莉亚：我和卢卡斯会在跳下来之前用手雷里的神秘术隐身，然后一口气冲回图书馆。
Emilia: We use your invisibility grenade before we get out of the vent. Then we just have to get to the library before it wears off.

擅长做计划的艾米莉亚一口气说出了自己的计划。
Emilia details her plan with confidence.

梅蕾尔：这就对了，这样你们就能先带着重要的“鸽子”回图书馆，从“暴雨”中保护同学们。拉文德一定会称赞你们是无与伦比的大英雄。
Merel: Brilliant, Emilia. You do exactly that. Take the Dove to the library to save your friends. Assistant Monitor Lavender will be there. I'm sure she'll be impressed.

艾米莉亚&卢卡斯：……
Emilia & Lucas: ...

梅蕾尔：——！
Merel: ...!

防爆门被神秘术能量猛击，金属门板破开了一道缺口。
A powerful burst of arcane energy blasts a hole through the door.

梅蕾尔：这个术式是……
Merel: No. That glow ... I've seen it before.

梅蕾尔：糟了，难道那种家伙不止一个……
Merel: So, there's more than one of those things.

又一道毁灭性的能量束从破开的洞口中射入，直直地击中通风管道入口上方的墙面。
Another beam cuts through the hole, this time striking the wall just above the vent.

坠落的残骸堵住了通风管入口。与此同时，神秘术冲击也让瞭望塔的墙面破开了一个口子。
Rubble falls and blocks the vent's entrance, but the impact also blasts a hole in the watchtower's wall.

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚，卢卡斯，你们还好吗？！
Merel: Emilia? Lucas? Are you alright?

艾米莉亚：我……我们没有事。
Emilia: I'm fine. We're fine.

卢卡斯：墙、墙壁被炸出了洞……
Lucas: It-It tore a hole through the wall, just like that.

重塑·门徒：……
Manus Disciple: ...

一颗熟悉的、完好的头颅正透过门板上的缺口向内张望。
A familiar, intact head peeks through the hole in the door.

梅蕾尔：——糟了！
Merel: Bloody!

梅蕾尔：艾米莉亚，卢卡斯，你们从墙上的洞口跳出去，我会用悬浮咒让你们平稳落地。
Merel: Move, now. Through the opening. I'll help you land with a levitation incantation.

艾米莉亚：那你呢？
Emilia: What about you?

梅蕾尔：你们直接往图书馆的方向跑，我会跟在你们后面回——躲开！
Merel: I'll be right behind you. Remember, once you land, run straight to the library. Watch out.

就在同一时间，防爆门终于无法支撑，被切开一个缺口的门板沉重地砸向地面。
The door, unable to withstand further damage, falls to the ground.

梅蕾尔：快走！
Merel: Go! Now!

梅蕾尔使劲托着他们，将他们从炸开的缺口推了出去。
Merel hoists them up and pushes them through the hole.

艾米莉亚&卢卡斯：呜啊！
Emilia & Lucas: Ah!

梅蕾尔：悬浮定止！
Merel: Ŝvebi   Stay!

艾米莉亚：梅蕾尔姐姐——！
Emilia: Merel!

梅蕾尔：去图书馆！
Merel: I'll see you at the library.

梅蕾尔：——！
Merel: ...!

她翻身躲过。能量束从先前被炸开的缺口中射向“暴雨”肆虐的天空。
She turns and dodges as an energy beam carves through the wall behind her and continues into the "Storm"-wrecked sky.

人形物放下手，缓缓走向墙上的缺口。它探出头向外张望，想从墙壁的缺口中钻出。
The disciple lowers its hand and approaches the opening in the wall, intent on breaking through it.

重塑·门徒：……？！
Manus Disciple: ...?!

梅蕾尔：别想走，你的敌人在这里！
Merel: Not so fast. You'll have to get through me first.

重塑·门徒：……
Manus Disciple: ...

怪物的头颅左右摇摆着，仿佛在做出一项抉择——终于，它回过头，面对留在塔里的敌人。
The disciple's head sways as if pondering a choice. Finally, it turns to face its enemy.

梅蕾尔换上最后一匣子弹，深吸一口气。
Merel loads her last clip and takes a deep breath.

梅蕾尔：我知道你不是杀不死的，怪物，我知道你的弱点在哪里。
Merel: I've killed your kind before. I know just where to aim.

梅蕾尔：就算这次……只有我一个人在，没有人会来帮我——
Merel: Even if I'm on my own this time—

梅蕾尔：——我也能干掉你！
Merel: I'm more than enough to take you down!

重塑·门徒：……
Manus Disciple: ...

内藏的软体组织流出破碎的颅腔。
Soft tissue flows from its cracked skull.

梅蕾尔：你……你也别想活着离开……呃——
Merel: That's for breaking my Dove. Ugh!

下肢传来一阵剧痛。
A sharp pain shoots up her leg.

梅蕾尔放下手枪，瞥了一眼落在角落里的，在战斗中破损的气象鸽。
Merel lowers her gun and looks to the Shelter Dove in the corner—damaged mid-combat.

梅蕾尔：这不难，玛尔纱教过我的……
Merel: Could be worse. Just need to remember what Marsha showed me.

梅蕾尔靠墙坐下，撕下一截衣摆，开始为撕裂的伤口包扎。
She slumps down against the wall, tears off part of her coat, and begins bandaging her wound.

布料压迫伤口，疼痛感正逼近峰值。
The pain spikes again as she tightens the cloth around it.

梅蕾尔：要是她看到我这样……嘶——肯定会吓一跳的……唔！
Merel: Imagine her face. Urgh, if she could see me now. Tch!

梅蕾尔：不……呃，所有人都会吓一跳的。卡戎，我的队友，维尔汀……我会告诉他们——呃，好了。
Merel: Not just Marsha, all of them—Charon, the "XII," Vertin ... I'll tell them—urgh! That should do it.

包扎完毕，她试着站起来。双腿还能活动——只要她能忍住随着心跳起伏的疼痛感。
With the wound now dressed, she lifts herself to her feet. She can walk, but each step comes with a wave of pain.

梅蕾尔：我会告诉他们，这回我真的没有逃……他们一定想不到。
Merel: I'll tell them I didn't run away this time.

梅蕾尔也不知道自己什么时候就来到了瞭望塔的底层。她靠在门边，看着瞭望塔外的世界正在以一种怪异的方式“燃烧”。
She finds herself at the watchtower's base and leans onto its doorframe. The world outside looks as though it's burning.

雨水在学校中庭被炸开的坑洞中聚集，成了一个个小水洼。
Rainwater gathers to form a puddle in a nearby crater.

梅蕾尔：爆炸……干得不错，艾米莉亚，卢卡斯……
Merel: That must've been Emilia's grenade. I knew you could do it, kid.

话音未落，雨滴从地面升起。
The raindrops begin to rise.

梅蕾尔：……“暴雨”。
Merel: The "Storm" ...

梅蕾尔：回图书馆去——和维尔汀还有十四行诗约好了的。
Merel: I've got to get back to the library. I have a promise to keep.

她扔下已经无法再击发的手枪及身上其他的负担，只剩怀中黯淡的气象鸽。
She casts aside her now-useless pistol and everything else weighing her down. All that's left in her arms is the dimming Shelter Dove.

梅蕾尔：……希望这真的是最后一场战斗了。
Merel: I really hope there won't be any more battles after this.

梅蕾尔：过去那么多次我都活下来了，这一次——
Merel: Now, come on. After everything I've been through ...

梅蕾尔：我也可以活下来！
Merel: I can survive this!

她跑入雨中。
She rushes into the rain.

39  白色方尖碑 · 1/2 / The White Tower · 1/2

白色方尖碑
The White Tower

高塔走廊
Hallway, Committee Tower

刻雷乌斯：向高塔有序撤退！
Creius: Fall back to the Spire.

刻雷乌斯：如果遇见了阿尔卡纳，不要与祂对视，不要向祂表现出敌意。
Creius: If you encounter Arcana, do not engage. I repeat, no hostile action.

刻雷乌斯：如果你们身边的人出现了精神恍惚的症状或是变得极度消极，限制他们的行动，并将他们带到远离阿尔卡纳的地方。
Creius: If anyone shows signs of panic or pessimism, restrain them and get them away from Arcana immediately.

伊里内伊：……这就是结局？我们两个，手拉手死在这儿？
Irinei: You ever pictured your own death, Pedra? Ever thought it would feature the two of us, side by side?

佩德拉：不是我最期盼的死法，但我知道怎么维持体面的姿势。有需要的话，我可以教你怎么做。
Pedra: I'd usually suggest a bit more positivity, but that may be naive given the situation. I might have a few tips on dying with dignity, though.

伊里内伊：我还是更愿意拿起枪来。刻雷乌斯，把你的手枪借给我。
Irinei: Thanks, but that much I've already figured out. Creius, give me your pistol.

刻雷乌斯：我可以借给你配枪，只要你不是在考虑最后一颗子弹的事……

伊里内伊：如果我们真的只剩下最后一颗子弹，我也要把它钉进阿尔卡纳的脑袋里。
Irinei: If I go down to my last bullet, I'm gunning for Arcana's head.

佩德拉：说得好，伊里内伊……这是我这么多年来最赞同你的一次。
Pedra: Well said, Irinei. I don't think we've ever agreed more.

在他们脚下，委员会大楼已经完全被格鲁托夫的军队占领，留给幸存者的生存空间正不断被向上压缩。
Krutov's army has occupied the entirety of the Committee Building beneath them. The remaining resistance forms a shrinking cordon at the upper levels.

刺入云端的白色高塔本不是一个为了避难而建造的建筑，但此刻人们已经无处可去。
The Spire was not built for shelter, but at this point, they have nowhere else to run.

槲寄生：……
Druvis III: ...

她渴望再一次倾听树木的声音，催动新芽的生长，但一如之前的无数次尝试那样失败。
She yearns to hear the trees' whispers once more, to nurture new saplings, but try as she might, it's all in vain.

星锑：槲寄生，原来你在这里——
Regulus: Druvis! There you are.

落魄的摇滚明星从人潮中分离出来。
Regulus splits away from the crowd.

槲寄生：星锑？你找到APPLe先生了吗？
Druvis III: Regulus? Did you find Mr. APPLe?

星锑：找到了，他现在正躺在我的包里，我差点以为他和其他人一样被甩出去了！
Regulus: That I did. He's here in my bag. I thought he'd flown out when we landed.

星锑：从那么高的地方掉下去——天呐，不知道诺谛卡他们是不是还安全！
Regulus: That was a rough fall. How's Nautika holding up?

在惊呼之中，她感觉有熟悉的人从自己身边经过。
In her surprise, she senses someone familiar brush up against her.

她侧过身，看见战地医生托着一具无力的躯体靠了过来。
She turns and sees Marsha carrying someone over.

星锑：玛尔纱？你背着的是……小吟游诗人？
Regulus: Marsha? Who've you got there? The bard?

玛尔纱：是的——呃，当时她被埋在一个文件柜下面。
Marsha: *sigh* I found her trapped under a filing cabinet.

骑士说话时吃痛了一下——失去神秘术的她只能用意志来抵抗遍布全身的伤口疼痛。
There's obvious pain in her voice. Without her arcane skill, she's forced to consciously bear each and every wound she's sustained.

玛尔纱：她还活着，只不过和意识唤醒者一样昏迷不醒……
Marsha: She's alive. Just in a coma, same as the Awakened.

虚构集：……
Recoleta: ...

行动队长Ⅰ：——我看见阿尔卡纳了。
Operation Team Leader I: We have eyes on Arcana.

敌情通告在人群中又引发了一阵惊骇与恐慌。
The alert incites a wave of fear.

槲寄生的目光投向身后，他们正退向楼层的尽头，退无可退之地。
Druvis looks behind her. They're being pushed back as far as they can possibly go.

槲寄生：高塔之上的空间很小，最终我们所有人都会被驱赶到一个很小的房间。
Druvis III: There isn't much space in the Spire. We'll all end up crowded in a room.

星锑：难道我最后要死在基金会会长那无聊的办公室里？！我一点儿也不想这样！
Regulus: Ugh, gods of rock have mercy. Please don't let me die in some stodgy old president's office.

玛尔纱：重塑之手的军队失去了增援，他们被堵在了通往高塔的入口处无法前进，只有阿尔卡纳本人穿过了防线。
Marsha: The rest of the Manus are cut off. Without reinforcements, our soldiers should be able to keep them out of the Spire. It's only Arcana that has broken through.

玛尔纱：只要不阻拦阿尔卡纳，祂不会在乎我们。各位，我们不能放弃生存的机会。
Marsha: We are not her goal. Whatever it is, she's decided to leave us be for now.

槲寄生：……我理解。
Druvis III: I see.

她不喜欢这种束手无策的感觉，但也只能放任自己走向终点。
She hates this feeling of helplessness, but there's nothing more that she can do.

槲寄生：玛尔纱，我们一起把虚构集带走。
Druvis III: Let's get Recoleta somewhere safer.

独占高塔顶层的会长办公室，作为房间来说固然辽阔开敞——
The president's office—the sole room at the top of the Spire—would be considered rather spacious under normal circumstances.

但当一批又一批避难者带着灰烬与血腥味涌入时，这里也很快变得无比拥挤。
But the crowds of survivors make it feel unbearably cramped.

会长：……
President: ...

基金会会长站在桌边，静静地注视着眼前的混乱，等待着。
The president stands behind his desk, observing the chaos and waiting.

基金会安保：祂、祂就在后面！
Foundation Security: She ... She's right behind us.

很快，最后一批避难者进入了办公室。
Before long, the final group of survivors enters the office.

在幸存者的注视下，阿尔卡纳缓缓踱入房间。
They all look on as Arcana steps inside.

阿尔卡纳：多么险峻的高塔。
Arcana: A most remarkable spire, I must admit, Mr. President.

阿尔卡纳：基金会耗费了多少时间，才构筑起这座俗世权力的纪念碑？
Arcana: How many uncounted years of labor were devoted to its construction? To this pretty monument to man's hubris?

基金会安保：别……别让祂靠近！
Foundation Security: D-Don't let her come any closer.

基金会安保：——！
Foundation Security: ...!

忠诚的护卫跪倒在地，集结在顶层的其他武装人员也几乎在同一时间丧失了抵抗的意志。
The loyal guard falls to his knees, and as he does, all the other armed personnel in the room seem to lose their will to resist, too.

无论他们拥有的勇气曾有多么充沛，现在都只像是拍打在堤岸上的潮水，顷刻间化作泡沫并消散。
All courage seems to disappear from them like smoke wisping away into the breeze.

会长：……
President: ...

会长：够了，停下。
President: Enough of this.

秘书长：会长？
Secretary General: Sir?

会长用一个手势压制了反对的声音，走出众多护卫的保护，来到阿尔卡纳面前。
He waves away the rebuttal and leaves his guards' protection to stand before Arcana.

会长：你想得到什么，阿尔卡纳？
President: What is it you want, Arcana?

阿尔卡纳：一场谈话。
Arcana: Only to speak.

会长：你策划了这场袭击，就只是想要谈话？
President: You would do all this, bring about so much pain and destruction, just to speak?

阿尔卡纳：袭击？
Arcana: Destruction?

祂竟有一丝不解。
She seems inexplicably puzzled.

阿尔卡纳：那和我没关系。
Arcana: To cause such misery hath never been my intent.

阿尔卡纳：我知道的是，一些人热情有加，创造了有趣的混乱。
Arcana: But alas, my children are unruly at times. Their chaotic natures drive them to violent ends.

会长：有趣？你的追随者在这场袭击中也死伤惨重。
President: Violent indeed. I'm sure you'd agree your forces have suffered considerably to get you this far.

会长：重塑之手发动的战争，制造的苦难，对你来说也是一场娱乐？
President: So, what is this? Just entertainment to you? Like all the wars you've provoked?

阿尔卡纳：我不会为你口中的战争负责，它的根源是人类的傲慢。
Arcana: What wars have I provoked? None that were not first designed in the arrogance and bloodlust of human hearts.

阿尔卡纳：千万人怀着仇恨死去，这是你们的罪过。
Arcana: Millions lie dead—fallen to thy machines, lost in the bloody hatreds of thine own kind.

会长：……？
President: ...?

一道椭圆形的屏障忽然升起——会长与阿尔卡纳恰好站在椭圆的两个焦点上。
An elliptical barrier appears around them. The president and Arcana stand at its two focal points.

阿尔卡纳：不要紧张，我只是需要一个安静的对话空间。
Arcana: Fear not. I desire only a little privacy.

会长：……你想说什么？
President: For what purpose?

阿尔卡纳：我要见他，哲人王，鸽子屋的统治者。
Arcana: An audience with the Philosopher King, ruler of the Pax House.

会长：如果你有问题，可以向我提问。
President: I am authorized to speak on his behalf.

阿尔卡纳：你没有亲历过空白时代，无法回答我的问题。
Arcana: Yet thou art too young to speak on matters of the Blank Era.

会长：哲人王不会为了你离开鸽子屋。
President: The Philosopher King will not leave the Pax House just to answer your questions.

阿尔卡纳：不会为我离开，还是他从来就没有离开过？
Arcana: Indeed not. For hath anyone e'er seen him stride beyond those walls?

阿尔卡纳：他多久没有亲自走入历史？他是否知道他的理想国绝对无法实现？
Arcana: How long hath he dwelt therein, isolated from this world? Doth he know his utopia shall never come to pass?

会长：我相信他的国度终会降临。
President: His kingdom will come.

阿尔卡纳：你不了解多少真相，只能用个人的信念来回答我的疑问。
Arcana: Thou speakest words of faith, Mr. President, not of fact.

会长：至少，我了解哲人王。
President: I trust in his vision.

阿尔卡纳：他放任你们被毁灭。
Arcana: Pray tell, was his vision unable to foresee this day, or was he simply too feeble to prevent it?

会长：在他伟大的构思中，没有不可牺牲、不可替换的组件。即使今日我们被摧毁，未来也会有人类去实现他的理想。
President: In his design, all may be sacrificed, all may be replaced. Whether we live or die today makes no difference. Should we fall, more of us will arise to carry on the mission.

阿尔卡纳：呵……人类。只有人类有资格践行他的理想，只有人类有资格留在他的世界中。
Arcana: "More of us"—thy meaning is clear. Ever obsessed with these inferior beings. Only they might fulfill his ideals; only they may prove worthy of his new world.

阿尔卡纳：可人类的诞生终究是一场谎言——
Arcana: Alas, that this vision of humanity is mere fabrication—

一些意料之外的噪音打断了谈话。
A noise interrupts their discussion.

屏障之外，雕塑般的战士们正在切割似乎绝不可能被撕裂的壁垒。
Outside, statue-like warriors cleave at the barrier.

阿尔卡纳：……内务部。
Arcana: The Core Guard.

阿尔卡纳：这就是哲人王给我的答复。
Arcana: Then the Philosopher King foresaw some answer for me after all.

会长：……你知道他们。
President: You knew about them?

阿尔卡纳：我确实想看看他们的样子，我猜在场的其他人也会对他们的存在感到好奇。
Arcana: And long have I been curious of them. I suspect I am not alone in that.

阿尔卡纳：完美的人类，未来世界的模范公民……灵性绝对无法触碰的空洞。
Arcana: Who would not wish to meet with mankind perfected? The ideal citizens of a brave new world, unshakable against the tides of pneuma.

阿尔卡纳：鸽子屋最“纯洁”的造物。
Arcana: The purest creations of the Pax House.

会长：你对人类一无所知，阿尔卡纳。你太年轻，不了解这个种族真正的限度。
President: Taunt all you like, Arcana, but there's little you know about humans. You have always underestimated their potential.

会长：你无法挑战统治历史的主人。
President: The master of history is not easily challenged.

环绕四周的结界出现了细小的裂缝，不断侵入的刀刃还在扩大裂口。
The barrier begins to crack under the warriors' blades.

“破壁者”：屏障很快就会被摧毁。
"Breacher": The barrier will be breached shortly.

“领队”：准备驱逐阿尔卡纳。
"Leader": Prepare to engage.

阿尔卡纳：……
Arcana: ...

阿尔卡纳：维尔汀，你醒了。
Arcana: Welcome back, Vertin.

阿尔卡纳：很高兴得知你拆开了那份小礼物。
Arcana: I am heartened to see that thou hast opened my "boon."

阿尔卡纳：这么急着驱赶我离开，看来你已经适应了新的力量。
Arcana: Thine attempts to turn thy newfound power on me have not gone unnoticed.

阿尔卡纳：花些时间享受它带给你的乐趣吧，我们之后再见。
Arcana: Very well. I shall leave thee to thine workings. May thou find it worthwhile.

在片刻失神之后，阿尔卡纳的注意力再度回到了眼前的结界之内。
Following the brief diversion, Arcana returns her focus to the president.

阿尔卡纳：我不喜欢这样的待客之道。谈话该结束了。
Arcana: Thy Foundation's hospitality hath grown cold, Mr. President.

会长：很快，你会被彻底消灭。
President: Stay awhile. I promise things will get heated shortly.

阿尔卡纳：呵呵……我很期待。
Arcana: Heh. How enticing.

阿尔卡纳：我要的答案已经得到。
Arcana: Yet my aims have been met.

神明抬起祂的一只手。
She raises a hand.

一颗种子从祂的掌心落下。
A seed falls from within her palm.

阿尔卡纳：我会给鸽子屋年轻的造物留下一名玩伴。
Arcana: As a token of courtesy, allow me to leave something behind for this young brood of the Pax House.

阿尔卡纳：再见了，凡人。
Arcana: Farewell, mortals.

白色的光芒在高悬的屋宇中闪烁，让高塔犹如古代奇迹中的灯塔。
White light shines from the floating room, making the Spire look like a lighthouse from a bygone era.

充盈一切空隙的光芒缓缓退却，世界逐渐夺回了它明与暗的光影构成。
The radiance recedes, and the world takes back its contrast.

玛尔纱：消失了……我们胜利了吗？
Marsha: It's gone. Have we won?

星锑：别太忧心，难道你不觉得基金会沉闷的空气都变得清新了吗？
Regulus: Seems like that might have been the ticket. The air feels a bit lighter now, don't you think?

槲寄生：我想那只是因为这幢委员会大楼都即将坍塌了。来吧，乘上树，我带大家撤离。
Druvis III: I'm concerned that might have something to do with the building collapsing around us. Everyone, onto the branches, please. I'll bring us down safely.

秘书长：不必担心，诸位。负责维系楼宇结构的建筑师已经开始履行她的职责了。
Secretary General: No need to panic. That's just the architect beginning her work.

“领队”：所有人，在入口处集合。
"Leader": All units, gather at the entrance.

“领队”：敌人溃退了，向下反攻，把委员会大楼内残余的重塑之手全部消灭。
"Leader": The enemy is retreating. Eliminate any remaining Manus Vindictae forces inside the building.

鸽子屋的白衣卫士很快恢复了现场的秩序，尚有余力的战斗人员渐次离开办公室，准备夺回委员会大楼的控制权。
The Pax House's guard soon restores order, and those still capable of fighting leave the office and prepare to retake the Committee Building.

秘书长靠近会长身边。
As the room quiets, the Secretary General approaches her superior.

秘书长：结束了，会长。阿尔卡纳留下的造物已死亡。
Secretary General: It's over, Mr. President. The creature has been eliminated.

秘书长：阿尔卡纳离开后，基金会总部内的重塑之手军队支撑不了太久。
Secretary General: Without Arcana's presence, the Manus army won't hold out for long.

会长站在一滩黏稠的深色液体前。
On the floor before him lies a puddle of dark sludge.

会长：这些年轻的神明啊……
President: One would expect more maturity from a god.

会长：对阿尔卡纳的神性观测还顺利吗？
President: Did we learn anything regarding Arcana's power?

秘书长：基金会拖住了很长时间，我们获得了预期之中的情报。
Secretary General: The Foundation held her back long enough for us to conduct a full assessment. The results align with our speculation.

秘书长：阿尔卡纳的权能是执掌灵性影响世界的“通道”，也可以说，祂掌控着神秘学能量本身。
Secretary General: She has dominion over the "channels" of pneuma in this world. In other words, she can control the very flow of arcanum itself.

会长：……所以祂可以剥夺我方使用神秘术的能力。
President: That explains how she severed us from our powers.

秘书长：通讯恢复了。我们和部分委员会大楼外的幸存人员取得了联系。无名者称她已经发现了重塑之手进入基金会的方式。
Secretary General: Precisely. Now that communications have resumed, we've been able to reestablish contact with our field operatives. Ms. Stranger reports that she's discovered how Manus Vindictae breached our security.

会长：无名者……她身上的信息很宝贵，尽快让人接触她，保证她的安全。
President: Excellent. Send support to her immediately. Her safety is of extreme importance to our efforts.

秘书长：她目前正受一名飞行女巫的保护。
Secretary General: I understand that she's already under the protection of a Flying Witch.

会长：嗯……这一次阿尔卡纳的目的不在毁灭，只在于混乱。
President: Good. We should count ourselves lucky. Had Arcana wished it, we would have suffered much greater losses.

秘书长：神明终究不是能靠人类的力量杀死的。
Secretary General: A god cannot be killed by mortal hands.

秘书长：能撼动神明的，只有另一位神明。
Secretary General: Only slain by the hand of another.

会长：……
President: ...

会长：维尔汀是不是做到了？
President: Do we know Vertin's status?

秘书长：根据我的判断，她已经开始苏醒，就在刚刚……通过某种方法削弱了阿尔卡纳的存在。
Secretary General: If I'm not mistaken, she intervened somehow. She challenged Arcana in this very room.

秘书长：不然内务部没有办法破坏祂的屏障。
Secretary General: If not for her, the Core Guard might not have been able to breach the barrier.

会长：关于维尔汀的事，我们晚些时候再着手调查。
President: Then we can worry about her later.

会长朝东面的窗户看去。
He turns toward the window.

会长：第一防线学校的混乱，应该也快要结束了。
President: The SPDM should be secured by now.

40  伙伴集结 / Assembly

《纽约新闻报》节选
Excerpt from the New York Times

前日深夜，位于市郊西侧的橡木树林发生大范围火灾，由于预警不及，林中地形复杂且可燃物繁多，抢险困难，火势持续直至清晨时分才得以扑灭。
     于当日黄昏时分进入森林的十三名人员全部遇难。死亡人员中已确认身份的六人，包括韦耶豪瑟夫妇，与其交易者汤姆森·乔伊斯、雪莱·杰拉德、凯利·基思，施工负责人罗素·詹姆斯。其余人员身份均在进一步核实中。

     因近年来的木材供应风波，原本根基深重的韦耶豪瑟在前些日子宣告破产。韦耶豪瑟夫妇将基础版起死回生剂的配方，有偿捐赠与纽约玛利亚疗养院、俄亥俄州圣心疗养所与长老会医疗中心三方后，又将名下的所有林业用地对外抛售。
     火情发生当晚，韦耶豪瑟夫妇与购买者三人带领工作小组进入森林，预备开始铲除作业，随后遇难。经市警署排查与分析，火情现场的残留有神秘学家活动与少量汽油的痕迹，已可确定为人为纵火案件。更多关于犯罪嫌疑人的信息仍在进一步调查当中。

     报道记者：詹姆斯·威尔斯/多丽·约翰逊
Around midnight two days ago, a large fire broke out in the oak forest on the western outskirts. The delay of the warning and the complex terrains full of inflammables in the forest resulted in a fire that lasted a whole night till dawn and 13 deaths.
The victims entered the forest at dusk. Identities of 6 of them have been confirmed: the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers Tomson Joyce, Shelley Gerald, Kelly Keith, and the project manager Russell James. The others' are still being verified.

The Weyerhaeuser used to be a leader in the wood industry, yet it went bankrupt not long ago due to the wood supply crisis these years. After donating the formula of the Resurrection Potion (basic version) to the Maria Sanatorium in New York, the Sacred Heart Sanatorium in Ohio, and the Presbyterian Church Health Center on certain conditions, the Weyerhaeusers sold all of their forestry lands.
On the night of the fire, the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers, and the workers went into the forest to start the disafforestation project before they died. The police has found traces of arcanist's activities and a small amount of gasoline at the scene, which shows the case is an arson attack. More investigations are needed to identify the suspect.

Reporter: James Welles/Dorey Johnson

41  互相攀咬 / Blame Game

空运来的软盘
A Disk Delivered by Air

42  鸽子的愿望 / Wishing for Peace

鸽子的愿望
Wishing for Peace

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

距离“暴雨”落下还剩——
Countdown to the "Storm"

……
...

……时间与命运交织不休。
Time and fate ceaselessly intertwine.

……时间是命运的一个喻体。
Time is a metaphor for fate.

过去已逝，现在只是夹缝中的一瞬，而未来……
The past is dead and gone. The present is but a fleeting moment trapped within a tiny crevice. And the future ...

维尔汀：……未来已由我明见。
Vertin: And the future revealed itself before me.

维尔汀：……
Vertin: ...

十四行诗：司辰，你还能听见我的声音吗？
Sonetto: Can you hear me, Timekeeper?

维尔汀：我……十四行诗？
Vertin: I ... Sonetto?

十四行诗：我在这儿！
Sonetto: Timekeeper, I'm here.

周身被金色丝线缠绕，方向感还在失陷。
Golden threads coil all around. All sense of direction is lost.

只能凭借直觉伸出手——
Reach out a hand and—

十四行诗：抓到你了！
Sonetto: I've got you.

无名者：错乱的时空骤然收缩，熟悉的出发地在眼前逐渐成型。
Ms. Stranger: The jumbled timeline contracts, and a familiar starting place takes form.

十四行诗：你……看见了什么？
Sonetto: What just happened? What did you see in there?

维尔汀：我们做到了，十四行诗。
Vertin: We did it, Sonetto.

十四行诗：做到了……什么？
Sonetto: Did what?

维尔汀：阿尔卡纳离开了。
Vertin: Arcana has retreated.

十四行诗：你真的见到阿尔卡纳了？
Sonetto: You confronted her in there?

维尔汀：是……我能听到祂在对我说话。祂说……我醒了？
Vertin: She spoke to me. She said, "Welcome back."

维尔汀：我试着挥开阿尔卡纳的雾气，之后不久祂就消失了。
Vertin: I tried to dispel the mist around her, but then she just disappeared.

维尔汀：但这种感觉不太好……我完全不明白自己到底做了什么。
Vertin: It doesn't feel right. I don't understand what I did.

十四行诗：我相信终有一天你能掌握的……这、这看起来简直就是神明的力量。
Sonetto: We'll figure it out some day. Whatever you did, it must have been powerful.

维尔汀：我会的。不过眼下，我们必须尽快找办法回到现实。
Vertin: Yes, we can figure things out later. Let's just find a way back first.

维尔汀：我们现在在哪里？
Vertin: Do you know where we are?

十四行诗：我们好像回到图书馆了，是我们出发时的那间自习室……
Sonetto: I think we're back in the library. This is the same study room we left before going to the central tower.

十四行诗：嗯？
Sonetto: Hm?

形象与声音逐渐由四面八方涌来，凝聚成型。在空无一人的空间中，逐渐出现了闪烁的人影。
Images and sounds flood in from all directions, condensing and taking a new shape. And in that space, a gleaming silhouette manifests.

十四行诗：这是……
Sonetto: What's that?

她听到了再熟悉不过的声音。
She hears a familiar voice.

“这是在顿河调查时，伊戈尔交给我的东西。他称呼它为‘奇迹’。”
"It's an item Igor gave me during the Don Operation."

“这颗立方体中或许存在着某种能在‘暴雨’中提供庇护的力量，但……”
"It's possible that it could generate an immunity zone. But ..."

十四行诗：——！
Sonetto: ...!

十四行诗：司辰，我看到了……我们，还有梅蕾尔。这是我们准备开启“奇迹”的时候。
Sonetto: It's us, Timekeeper, and Merel, back when we were discussing whether to open the "boon."

维尔汀：什么？也就是说，很快我们就会……
Vertin: Then, they're about to ...

与预料中的相同，那扇黑暗的门扉再度出现在眼前。
Just as expected, the dark gate appears once more.

十四行诗：果然，她们……不，是我们打开了这扇门。
Sonetto: They're going through the gate, or we are.

维尔汀：十四行诗，我想我知道走出这里的方法了……带我去门边。
Vertin: I know how to get out. Sonetto, lead me to the gate.

十四行诗：你知道——好的，在这边。
Sonetto: Right. Stay close.

接近门边时，身体却感觉到了强大的斥力。
But the gate seems to exert a powerful repulsive force.

维尔汀：果然，门从里面是打不开的。
Vertin: It won't open from this side. Just as I thought.

维尔汀：这扇门应该只能“从外向内”开启。也就是说，要有外面的人穿过这扇门，我们才能借机走出去。
Vertin: The gate can only be opened from the other side. Someone has to come in first.

维尔汀：——进入这扇门，十四行诗，还有……维尔汀！你必须和十四行诗一起进来。
Vertin: Come in, Sonetto. Vertin, you have to bring Sonetto with you.

起先她并不确定声音能否穿透黑门，但过去的回忆带来了确定的答案。
At first, it isn't clear whether the voice can travel through the gate, but then, memories provide an answer.

十四行诗：司辰，“我们”准备穿过黑门了。
Sonetto: We're just about to enter through the gate.

维尔汀：我们要同时进出，抓住机会，十四行诗。
Vertin: Which means we need to leave at the same time.

十四行诗：嗯……
Sonetto: I'm ready.

十四行诗等待着，直到过去与未来走到交汇点。
She waits until the very moment that past and future converge.

“‘暴雨’前的最后一刻，我们都要平安地回到图书馆，在这里前往新的时代。”
"We meet here again before the 'Storm.' And whatever happens, we move forward together."

“嗯，约好了。”
"Mhm. I promise."

“我们出发吧，十四行诗。”
"It's time. Let's go, Sonetto."

十四行诗：——司辰！
Sonetto: Now, Timekeeper!

过去与未来的人擦肩而过，时间的首尾之衔已然完成。
Past and present selves brush shoulders. The loop is closed.

再一次，凭空出现的尘埃席卷万物。黑暗和沉默灌入感官。
Dust appears and sweeps everything away once more. Darkness and silence flood all senses.

维尔汀：十四行诗？！
Vertin: Sonetto?

十四行诗：我在，司辰，我们回到了原本的年龄，你能看清东西了吗？
Sonetto: I'm here. We're back to our real ages. How's your eyesight, Timekeeper?

维尔汀：我的视力也恢复了，我们应该已经回到了现实。
Vertin: I can see again. It seems we've made it back to reality.

十四行诗：太好了……！
Sonetto: Finally!

在房间正中央，黑暗的门扉已经消失，它退缩为原本的样子——悬浮着的黑色“魔盒”。
The dark gate at the center of the room disappears, and in its place, shrunk down to its original form, appears the floating black box.

维尔汀：距离“暴雨”落下还有三分钟……
Vertin: But there's only three minutes left before the "Storm."

拉文德：维尔汀小姐，十四行诗小姐？！
Lavender: Ah, Ms. Vertin, Ms. Sonetto!

维尔汀：……拉文德？
Vertin: Lavender?

拉文德：太好了，我听见房间里有声音，果然是你们回来了。
Lavender: I heard voices. Thank goodness you're back.

维尔汀：嗯，图书馆的情况怎么样？
Vertin: How's everything in the library? Is everyone alright?

拉文德：现在……
Lavender: For now ...

拉文德走向门外。
Lavender walks out toward the main hall.

学生们几乎全都挤在自习室外的藏书区里。
The students are huddled in the book storage area outside the study room.

艾米莉亚：这些鸽子……比我们带回来的时候更暗了……
Emilia: The Doves aren't glowing so much anymore.

他们沉默地守在一小堆光芒黯淡的石鸽子旁。
Yet they guard them in silence all the same.

仍然昏迷不醒的玛蒂尔达也被学生们带到了这里，气象鸽的黯淡光芒给她的身体打上一层模糊的光影。
They've brought Matilda here, too. A Shelter Dove casts a faint glow on her still-unconscious form.

拉文德：免疫区已经不包括图书馆了。多亏两个从中央瞭望塔过来的学生带回了备用的气象鸽，我们才撑到现在。
Lavender: The immunity zone has shrunk so far that it doesn't cover the library anymore. We'd all have been doomed if these two students hadn't brought us these backup Shelter Doves.

拉文德：他们说，是梅蕾尔小姐让他们把鸽子赶快带回来的。
Lavender: They said Ms. Merel was the one who got them.

拉文德：可是它们的光也快要熄灭了，我害怕我们……
Lavender: But the Doves may not last long. Without them ...

维尔汀：别担心，拉文德。
Vertin: Things will be alright now, Lavender.

维尔汀：我得到了能够让大家度过这次“暴雨”的庇护。
Vertin: I've found a shelter strong enough for all of us.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

她曾经见过“暴雨”落下的时刻。
This isn't the first time she's seen the "Storm" as it arrives.

但不像现在——世界在眼前碎裂，她身处其中，既没有身处安全的庇护所内，也无法撑起一把平衡伞。
But there's something different this time. As the world shatters before her eyes, she's without a safe place to hide or an Equilibrium Umbrella.

梅蕾尔：雨水……在倒流。
Merel: The rain ... It's rising.

肾上腺素的浪潮逐渐退去，受伤的双腿发出撕心裂肺的哀嚎，抗拒着跑完通往图书馆的最后一段路。
The wave of adrenaline is receding. Her wounded legs cry out their refusal in her sprint between her and the library.

小梅蕾尔：嘿，嘿嘿！
Merel: Heh heh!

梅蕾尔：……什么？
Merel: Who? Who's there?

小梅蕾尔：维尔汀和十四行诗成功了。
Merel: Vertin and Sonetto made it.

小梅蕾尔：我们要得救了，快跑。
Merel: We're going to be okay. Come on.

梅蕾尔：要……得救了？可是……
Merel: They made it? But ...

小梅蕾尔：别可是了，我们真的要得救了。
Merel: But what? They can save everyone, us included. Come! Come!

梅蕾尔：……我快走不动了。
Merel: I can't go any further.

梅蕾尔：我知道“暴雨”是怎么回事，当雨水开始倒流，做什么都晚了……
Merel: The reverse has already started. It's too late.

梅蕾尔：我甚至能看见你。我不应该看见这种幻觉的，是暴雨症候……我一定是要死了……
Merel: I'm just hallucinating. You're not real. This must be the Storm Syndrome.

小梅蕾尔：别放弃，梅蕾尔！
Merel: Don't give up now, Merel.

小梅蕾尔：跑到图书馆去，用光你所有的力气，我们还能活下来。
Merel: You can make it. Just give it everything. If you do, we all live.

小梅蕾尔：——快看！
Merel: Look.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

她艰难地仰起头，视线中的一切都被倒流的雨水模糊。建筑正诡异地在雨中燃烧，树木、高墙甚至是天空都在被撕碎。
She forces her head up. The rain swirls everything into a blur, but she makes out buildings burning and trees, walls—even the sky itself—being ripped apart.

可是图书馆的一部分屹立不倒，她看到有阵阵光辉正从面向广场的门扉与窗户中泄出。
Yet part of the library doesn't yield. She sees light pouring out from the doors and windows.

梅蕾尔：图书馆……好远啊……
Merel: It's so far.

梅蕾尔：我第一次参加体育测试的时候，就没能一口气跑到操场尽头……
Merel: This is where I failed my first PE test. I just couldn't reach the end of the track in time.

小梅蕾尔：但现在不一样了，梅蕾尔！我们比过去要高大多了！
Merel: But you've grown up. You're not that little girl anymore.

小梅蕾尔：跑吧，梅蕾尔！跑起来……
Merel: Come on, Merel. Move your feet.

梅蕾尔：——！
Merel: ...!

拉文德：不要走到光的外面，大家都靠在一起！
Lavender: Everyone, stay together. Don't leave the light.

拉文德：都到维尔汀小姐身边来！
Lavender: Gather around Ms. Vertin.

维尔汀：梅蕾尔……
Vertin: Merel?

维尔汀：她还没有回来？
Vertin: Where's Merel?

梅蕾尔？：快，梅蕾尔。快跑。
Merel?: Keep going. Just a little more.

梅蕾尔：……
Merel: ...

梅蕾尔？：我们成功了，我们现在是第一防线学校的英雄了。
Merel?: It's so close. We can make it.

梅蕾尔？：维尔汀她们回来了，所有人都在图书馆等我们！
Merel?: We still have a promise to keep. Remember?

梅蕾尔：维尔汀？她们……
Merel: Vertin ... Sonetto ...

维尔汀：那是——梅蕾尔？！
Vertin: Is that Merel?

梅蕾尔：我就快……只差一点儿……
Merel: Just a little bit closer.

梅蕾尔：我还想……活下去。
Merel: I don't want to go.

梅蕾尔：不要……抛下我，维尔——
Merel: Don't leave me, Vert—

维尔汀：不，梅蕾尔！
Vertin: Merel! No!

43  纯白色文件夹 / Pure White Folder

一份不安
A Hint of Unease

阿尔卡纳入侵行动评估报告

现将本次阿尔卡纳入侵事件的战略分析与行动评估汇报如下：

一、 战略意图研判
阿尔卡纳此次入侵并非为领土或资源扩张，而是一场战略试探。其核心意图在于：  
1. 诱使“鸽子屋”现身，迫使其在压力下暴露行动轨迹；  
2. 恐意在测试内务部实战效能，采集我方核心安全力量的关键数据。

二、 当前局势与应对评估
内务部展现了忠诚与执行力，但过早介入正面战场，导致：  
1.战略底牌提前暴露，内务部的真实实力与战术特点或被对手掌握；  
2.战略主动权部分让渡，未来将面临针对性对抗，而对手仍保有后手。

三、 结论与建议
此次冲突使阿尔卡纳部分达成其战略目的。为此建议：  
1. 重新评估“内务部”启用阈值，避免在低成本试探中损耗战略储备；  
2. 对内务部进行战术伪装与升级，制造“虚实两套”能力画像以混淆对手；  
3. 构建分层应对机制，明确基金会防务力量、芝诺常驻部队与内务部的介入阶梯。
……
Arcana Invasion Assessment Report

The following is a strategic analysis and operational assessment of the recent Arcana invasion:

I. Assessment of Strategic Intent
Arcana's invasion was not aimed at territorial or resource expansion—it was a strategic probe. Her core intentions are assessed as follows:
1. To draw out the Pax House and force it to expose its operational movements under pressure.
2. To test the Core Guard's combat effectiveness and collect critical data on our core security forces.

II. Current Situation and Response Assessment
The Core Guard has demonstrated loyalty and effectiveness, but its premature entry into direct combat has resulted in the following consequences:
1. Our strategic cards were shown too early—the Core Guard's true capabilities and tactical characteristics may now be known to the enemy.
2. Strategic initiative has been partially ceded. We will face targeted countermeasures going forward, while the enemy still holds options in reserve.

III. Conclusions and Recommendations
This confrontation has allowed Arcana to partially achieve her strategic objectives. We recommend the following:
1. Reassess the threshold for deploying the Core Guard to avoid depleting strategic reserves in response to low-cost probes.
2. Apply tactical deception and upgrades to the Core Guard—projecting a false profile alongside the real one to mislead the enemy.
3. Establish a tiered response framework that clearly defines the escalation order of the Foundation's defense forces, Zeno forces, and the Core Guard.

Ward, Rehab Center
